Variance Reduction for Expectations with Diffusion Teachers
Authors: Jesse Bettencourt, Xindi Wu, Matan Atzmon, James Lucas, Jonathan Lorraine
First: 2026-05-20T17:59:52+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-20T17:59:52+00:00
Comments: Project page: https://research.nvidia.com/labs/sil/projects/CARV/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Pretrained diffusion models serve as frozen teachers feeding downstream pipelines such as text-to-3D, single-step distillation, and data attribution. The teacher gradients these pipelines consume are Monte Carlo (MC) expectations over noise levels and Gaussian noise samples; their estimator variance dominates compute cost because each draw requires expensive upstream work (rendering, simulation, encoding). We introduce CARV, a compute-aware variance-accounting framework that motivates a hierarchical MC estimator: amortize the expensive upstream computation over cheap diffusion-noise resamples, sharpened by timestep importance sampling and a stratified-inverse-CDF construction. In our text-to-3D distillation and attribution experiments, CARV delivers 2-3x effective compute multipliers (most from amortized reuse; ~25% additional from IS+stratification) without changing the objective; in single-step distillation, the same techniques cut gradient variance by an order of magnitude but do not improve downstream FID, marking the regime where MC variance is no longer the bottleneck.
中文标题/摘要
标题：扩散教师模型的期望方差降低
预训练的扩散模型作为冻结的教师模型，为下游流程（如文本到3D、单步蒸馏和数据归因）提供指导。这些流程所消耗的教师梯度是噪声水平和高斯噪声样本上的蒙特卡洛（MC）期望值；其估计器方差主导计算成本，因为每次采样都需要昂贵的上游工作（渲染、仿真、编码）。我们提出了CARV，一种计算感知的方差核算框架，推动了一种分层MC估计器：将昂贵的上游计算摊销到廉价的扩散噪声重采样上，并通过时间步重要性采样和分层逆CDF构造进行增强。在我们的文本到3D蒸馏和归因实验中，CARV在不改变目标函数的情况下实现了2-3倍的有效计算增益（主要来自摊销重用；约25%来自重要性采样+分层）；在单步蒸馏中，相同技术将梯度方差降低一个数量级，但未提升下游FID，表明MC方差已不再是瓶颈。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high computational cost of Monte Carlo gradient estimation in diffusion-based teacher models for downstream tasks like text-to-3D and data attribution, the paper proposes CARV, a compute-aware variance reduction framework. CARV employs a hierarchical Monte Carlo estimator that amortizes expensive upstream computations (e.g., rendering) across cheap diffusion noise resamples, enhanced by timestep importance sampling and stratified-inverse-CDF sampling. Experiments show CARV achieves 2-3x effective compute efficiency in distillation and attribution tasks, primarily through amortization with ~25% additional gain from sampling techniques; in single-step distillation, it reduces gradient variance by an order of magnitude without improving downstream FID, indicating MC variance is no longer the bottleneck.
为降低扩散模型作为教师模型在下游任务（如文本到3D、数据归因）中蒙特卡洛梯度估计的高计算开销，作者提出CARV框架，该框架通过分层蒙特卡洛估计器实现计算感知的方差缩减，将昂贵的上游计算（如渲染、编码）在廉价的扩散噪声重采样中复用，并结合时间步重要性采样与分层逆CDF构造。实验表明，CARV在蒸馏与归因任务中实现2-3倍的有效计算增益，主要来自复用，另有约25%来自采样优化；在单步蒸馏中，梯度方差降低一个数量级，但下游FID未提升，表明蒙特卡洛方差已非瓶颈。
Equilibrium Reasoners: Learning Attractors Enables Scalable Reasoning
Authors: Benhao Huang, Zhengyang Geng, Zico Kolter
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-05-20T17:59:48+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-20T17:59:48+00:00
Comments: ICML 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Scaling test-time compute by iteratively updating a latent state has emerged as a powerful paradigm for reasoning. Yet the internal mechanisms that enable these iterative models to generalize beyond memorized patterns remain unclear. We hypothesize that generalizable reasoning arises from learning task-conditioned attractors: latent dynamical systems whose stable fixed points correspond to valid solutions. We formalize this process through Equilibrium Reasoners (EqR), which enable test-time scaling without external verifiers or task-specific priors. EqR scales internal dynamics along two axes: depth, by running more iterations, and breadth, by aggregating stochastic trajectories from multiple initializations. Empirically, gains from test-time scaling are tightly coupled with stronger convergence toward solution-aligned attractors. This attractor perspective allows neural networks to adaptively allocate test-time compute based on task difficulty. While simple cases converge within 1 to 5 iteration steps, harder cases benefit from massive test-time scaling. By unrolling up to the equivalent of 40,000 layers, scalable latent reasoning boosts accuracy from 2.6% for feedforward models to over 99% on Sudoku-Extreme. These results suggest that learned attractor landscapes provide a useful mechanistic lens for understanding scalable reasoning in iterative latent models.
中文标题/摘要
标题：均衡推理者：学习吸引子实现可扩展推理
通过迭代更新潜在状态来扩展测试时计算量，已成为推理的强大范式。然而，这些迭代模型能够超越记忆模式实现泛化的能力背后的内部机制仍不明确。我们假设，可泛化的推理源于学习任务条件吸引子：其稳定不动点对应有效解的潜在动力系统。 我们通过均衡推理者（EqR）形式化这一过程，它无需外部验证器或任务特定先验即可实现测试时扩展。EqR 沿两个维度扩展内部动力学：深度通过增加迭代次数，广度通过聚合多个初始化的随机轨迹。实证表明，测试时扩展带来的增益与更接近解对齐的吸引子的收敛强度紧密相关。 这种吸引子视角使神经网络能够根据任务难度自适应分配测试时计算资源。简单情况可在1至5步迭代内收敛，而困难情况则受益于大规模测试时扩展。通过展开相当于40,000层的计算，可扩展潜在推理将前馈模型的准确率从2.6%提升至Sudoku-Extreme上的99%以上。这些结果表明，学习到的吸引子景观为理解迭代潜在模型中的可扩展推理提供了有用的机制视角。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to understand how iterative latent models generalize beyond memorization, the authors propose Equilibrium Reasoners, which learn task-conditioned attractors—stable fixed points in latent dynamics that correspond to valid solutions. By scaling computation through deeper iteration and broader stochastic trajectory aggregation, EqR enables adaptive test-time compute allocation based on task difficulty. Experiments show that simple tasks converge in 1–5 steps, while hard tasks benefit from massive scaling, achieving over 99% accuracy on Sudoku-Extreme after unrolling equivalent to 40,000 layers, compared to just 2.6% for feedforward models.
为理解迭代潜变量模型如何超越记忆泛化，作者提出均衡推理器（EqR），其通过学习任务条件吸引子——对应有效解的潜变量动力学稳定不动点——实现推理。通过在深度（增加迭代次数）和广度（聚合多个初始化的随机轨迹）上扩展内部动力学，EqR可根据任务难度自适应分配测试时计算资源。实验表明，收敛至解对齐吸引子与性能提升紧密相关：简单任务1–5步即可收敛，而困难任务通过展开至40,000层等效深度，准确率从2.6%提升至99%以上（Sudoku-Extreme）
Uni-Edit: Intelligent Editing Is A General Task For Unified Model Tuning
Authors: Dian Zheng, Manyuan Zhang, Hongyu Li, Hongbo Liu, Kai Zou, Kaituo Feng, Hongsheng Li
First: 2026-05-20T17:59:42+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-20T17:59:42+00:00
Comments: Project Page: https://zhengdian1.github.io/Uni-Edit-proj/ Code: https://github.com/zhengdian1/Uni-Edit
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3 · Project1
Abstract
Currently, enhancing Unified Multimodal Models (UMMs) with image understanding, generation, and editing capabilities mainly relies on mixed multi-task training. Due to inherent task conflicts, such strategy requires complex multi-stage pipelines, massive data mixing, and balancing tricks, merely resulting in a performance trade-off rather than true mutual reinforcement. To break this paradigm, we propose Uni-Edit, an intelligent image editing task that serves as the first general task for UMM tuning. Unlike complex mixed pipelines, Uni-Edit improves performance across all three abilities at once using only one task, one training stage, and one dataset. Specifically, we first identify image editing as an inherently ideal general task, as it naturally demands both visual understanding and generation. However, existing editing data relies on simplistic instructions that severely underutilize a model's understanding capacity. To address this, we introduce the first automated and scalable data synthesis pipeline for intelligent editing, transforming diverse VQA data into complex and effective editing instructions with embedded questions and nested logic. This yields Uni-Edit-148k, pairing diverse reasoning-intensive instructions with high-quality edited images. Extensive experiments on BAGEL and Janus-Pro demonstrate that tuning solely on Uni-Edit achieves comprehensive enhancements across all three capabilities without any auxiliary operations.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Uni-Edit：智能编辑是统一模型调优的通用任务
目前，增强统一多模态模型（UMMs）的图像理解、生成与编辑能力主要依赖混合多任务训练。由于任务固有冲突，该策略需复杂多阶段流程、大量数据混合及平衡技巧，仅实现性能权衡而非真正互促。为打破此范式，我们提出Uni-Edit，作为首个通用的智能图像编辑任务，用于UMM调优。与复杂混合流程不同，Uni-Edit仅用一个任务、一个训练阶段和一个数据集，同时提升三项能力。具体而言，我们首先识别图像编辑为理想通用任务，因其天然需视觉理解与生成。但现有编辑数据依赖简单指令，严重未充分利用模型理解能力。为此，我们提出首个自动化可扩展的智能编辑数据合成流程，将多样VQA数据转化为含嵌套逻辑与问题的复杂有效编辑指令，生成Uni-Edit-148k数据集，配以多样推理密集指令与高质量编辑图像。在BAGEL与Janus-Pro上的大量实验表明，仅用Uni-Edit调优即可全面增强三项能力，无需任何辅助操作。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of mixed multi-task training in Unified Multimodal Models, which leads to performance trade-offs due to task conflicts, the authors propose Uni-Edit as a unified intelligent editing task that simultaneously enhances image understanding, generation, and editing. Unlike prior approaches requiring complex pipelines and data mixing, Uni-Edit uses a single training stage and dataset to boost all three capabilities. The method introduces a novel automated data synthesis pipeline that converts VQA data into complex, reasoning-intensive editing instructions with embedded questions and nested logic, resulting in the Uni-Edit-148k dataset. Experimental results on BAGEL and Janus-Pro show that tuning solely on Uni-Edit achieves superior and balanced improvements across all three modalities without auxiliary techniques.
为解决统一多模态模型中混合多任务训练导致的性能权衡问题，作者提出Uni-Edit任务，作为首个能同时提升图像理解、生成与编辑能力的通用任务。该方法摒弃复杂多阶段流程与数据混合，仅通过单一训练阶段和数据集即可实现三方面能力的全面提升。其核心是构建首个自动化数据合成管道，将VQA数据转化为包含嵌套逻辑与问题的复杂编辑指令，生成Uni-Edit-148k数据集。在BAGEL与Janus-Pro上的实验表明，仅用Uni-Edit进行微调即可显著增强三项能力，无需额外操作或性能妥协。
Quantifying Hyperparameter Transfer and the Importance of Embedding Layer Learning Rate
Authors: Dayal Singh Kalra, Maissam Barkeshli
First: 2026-05-20T17:59:40+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-20T17:59:40+00:00
Comments: 10+28 pages, 5+17 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Hyperparameter transfer allows extrapolating optimal optimization hyperparameters from small to large scales, making it critical for training large language models (LLMs). This is done either by fitting a scaling law to the hyperparameters or by a judicious choice of parameterization, such as Maximal Update ($μ$P), that renders optimal hyperparameters approximately scale invariant. In this paper, we first develop a framework to quantify hyperparameter transfer through three metrics: (1) the quality of the scaling law fit, (2) the robustness to extrapolation errors, and (3) the asymptotic loss penalty due to choice of parameterization. Next, we investigate through a comprehensive series of ablations why $μ$P appears to offer high-quality learning rate transfer relative to standard parameterization (SP), as existing theory is inadequate. We find that the overwhelming benefit of $μ$P relative to SP when training with AdamW arises simply from maximizing the learning rate of the embedding layer. In SP, the embedding layer learning rate acts as a bottleneck that induces training instabilities; increasing it by a factor of width to match $μ$P dramatically smooths out training while improving hyperparameter transfer. We also find that weight decay improves the scaling law fits, while, in the fixed token-per-parameter setting, it hurts the robustness of the extrapolation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：量化超参数迁移及嵌入层学习率的重要性
超参数迁移允许从小型到大型规模外推最优优化超参数，这对于训练大型语言模型（LLM）至关重要。该方法可通过拟合超参数的缩放定律，或通过精心选择参数化方式（如最大更新量μP），使最优超参数近似尺度不变。本文首先建立一个框架，通过三个指标量化超参数迁移：（1）缩放定律拟合的质量，（2）对外推误差的鲁棒性，（3）因参数化选择导致的渐近损失惩罚。随后，我们通过一系列全面的消融实验探究为何μP相较于标准参数化（SP）在学习率迁移方面表现更优，因为现有理论尚不充分。我们发现，当使用AdamW训练时，μP相对于SP的显著优势仅源于最大化嵌入层的学习率。在SP中，嵌入层学习率作为瓶颈引发训练不稳定；将其按宽度因子提升以匹配μP，可显著平滑训练过程并提升超参数迁移效果。此外，我们发现权重衰减有助于缩放定律拟合，但在固定每参数令牌数的设置下，会损害外推的鲁棒性。
Summary / 总结
This paper investigates how hyperparameters can be transferred across model scales to train large language models more efficiently, motivated by the need to avoid re-tuning from scratch. The authors develop a framework to quantify transfer quality using three metrics: scaling law fit, extrapolation robustness, and asymptotic loss penalty. Their experiments reveal that the Maximal Update ($μ$P) parameterization outperforms standard parameterization (SP) primarily because $μ$P assigns a higher learning rate to the embedding layer, which in SP acts as a training bottleneck. Increasing the embedding layer learning rate in SP to match $μ$P significantly improves training stability and hyperparameter transfer. Additionally, weight decay enhances scaling law fits but reduces extrapolation robustness under fixed token-per-parameter settings.
本文旨在量化小模型到大模型的超参数迁移效果，通过三个指标——标度律拟合质量、外推鲁棒性及渐近损失惩罚——评估迁移性能。研究发现，Maximal Update（$μ$P）参数化优于标准参数化（SP）的关键在于其最大化嵌入层学习率，从而消除SP中嵌入层学习率作为训练瓶颈的问题；将SP中嵌入层学习率提升至模型宽度倍数可显著提升训练稳定性与超参数迁移效果。此外，权重衰减虽改善标度律拟合，但在固定token-per-parameter设置下会降低外推鲁棒性。
EvoStruct: Bridging Evolutionary and Structural Priors for Antibody CDR Design via Protein Language Model Adaptation
Authors: Mansoor Ahmed, Sujin Lee, Umar Khayaz, Murray Patterson
First: 2026-05-20T17:59:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-20T17:59:16+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Equivariant graph neural network (GNN) methods for antibody complementarity-determining region (CDR) design achieve the highest sequence recovery but suffer from severe vocabulary collapse. The current best GNN methods over-predict very few amino acids, such as tyrosine and glycine, while ignoring functionally important residues. We trace this failure to GNN encoders learning amino acid distributions de novo from limited structural data, discarding substitution patterns encoded in evolutionary databases. To resolve this, we propose EvoStruct, which bridges a frozen protein language model (PLM) with 3D structural context from an E(3)-equivariant GNN via a cross-attention adapter. Unlike prior PLM-structure adapters for general protein design, EvoStruct targets the vocabulary collapse problem specific to CDR design through progressive PLM unfreezing and R-Drop consistency regularization. On the CHIMERA-Bench dataset, EvoStruct achieves the highest amino acid recovery and lowest perplexity among several antibody design methods, improving sequence recovery by 16% and reducing perplexity by 43% relative to the best GNN baselines, while recovering 2.3x greater amino acid diversity and the highest binding-pair correlation with ground truth.
中文标题/摘要
标题：EvoStruct：通过蛋白质语言模型适配弥合进化与结构先验，用于抗体CDR设计
用于抗体互补决定区（CDR）设计的等变图神经网络（GNN）方法虽能实现最高的序列恢复率，但存在严重的词汇坍塌问题。当前最优的GNN方法过度预测极少数氨基酸（如酪氨酸和甘氨酸），而忽略功能重要的残基。我们追溯此失败原因在于GNN编码器从有限结构数据中从头学习氨基酸分布，丢弃了进化数据库中编码的替代模式。为解决此问题，我们提出EvoStruct，通过交叉注意力适配器将冻结的蛋白质语言模型（PLM）与E(3)等变GNN的3D结构上下文相连接。与先前用于通用蛋白质设计的PLM-结构适配器不同，EvoStruct通过渐进式PLM解冻和R-Drop一致性正则化，专门针对CDR设计中的词汇坍塌问题。在CHIMERA-Bench数据集上，EvoStruct在多种抗体设计方法中实现了最高的氨基酸恢复率和最低的困惑度，相较最佳GNN基线方法，序列恢复率提升16%，困惑度降低43%，氨基酸多样性提升2.3倍，并与真实值具有最高的结合对相关性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the vocabulary collapse in equivariant GNNs for antibody CDR design, which over-predicts limited amino acids while ignoring functionally critical residues, EvoStruct integrates a frozen protein language model with 3D structural context via a cross-attention adapter. It specifically addresses CDR design’s vocabulary collapse through progressive PLM unfreezing and R-Drop regularization, enhancing both diversity and accuracy. On CHIMERA-Bench, EvoStruct outperforms existing GNN methods by achieving 16% higher sequence recovery, 43% lower perplexity, 2.3x greater amino acid diversity, and the highest binding-pair correlation with ground truth.
为解决抗体CDR设计中GNN模型因仅从有限结构数据学习氨基酸分布而导致的词汇坍塌问题（如过度预测酪氨酸、甘氨酸等，忽略功能关键残基），EvoStruct通过交叉注意力适配器将冻结的蛋白质语言模型与E(3)等变GNN的三维结构上下文相结合，并采用渐进式解冻PLM与R-Drop一致性正则化以融合进化先验。在CHIMERA-Bench数据集上，EvoStruct实现最高氨基酸恢复率与最低困惑度，相较最优GNN基线提升序列恢复16%、降低困惑度43%，氨基酸多样性提升2.3倍，且与真实值的结合亲和力相关性最高。
One-Step Distillation of Discrete Diffusion Image Generators via Fixed-Point Iteration
Authors: Chaoyang Wang, Yunhai Tong
First: 2026-05-20T17:59:10+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-20T17:59:10+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Discrete diffusion models excel at visual synthesis but rely on slow, iterative decoding. Existing single-step distillation methods attempt to bypass this bottleneck, either by training auxiliary score networks that effectively double compute, or by introducing specialized parameterizations and multi-stage pipelines that fragment optimization. In this paper, we introduce Fixed-Point Distillation (FPD), an end-to-end framework that constructs local correction targets by partially corrupting the student's one-step draft and refining it with a single teacher step. To compute the training objective in a semantically meaningful space, we lift discrete tokens into continuous features and apply a multi-bandwidth drift loss that iteratively accumulates these corrections. To backpropagate through the discrete bottleneck, we employ a straight-through estimator that feeds exact hard-sampled tokens to the teacher and decoder during the forward pass, ensuring that training and inference operate on the same codebook manifold, while routing continuous gradients back to the student logits. This fully differentiable pathway additionally accommodates an optional unconditional adversarial objective to enhance perceptual realism. Evaluations on both class- and text-conditional generation validate the effectiveness of our framework. FPD achieves competitive visual fidelity and structural alignment within a single inference step, narrowing the gap to multi-step teachers while outperforming existing discrete distillation baselines.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过固定点迭代实现离散扩散图像生成器的一步蒸馏
离散扩散模型在视觉合成方面表现优异，但依赖缓慢的迭代解码。现有单步蒸馏方法试图绕过这一瓶颈，要么训练辅助得分网络（实际计算量翻倍），要么引入专用参数化和多阶段流程，导致优化碎片化。本文提出固定点蒸馏（FPD），一种端到端框架，通过部分破坏学生模型的单步草图并用教师模型一步修正，构建局部修正目标。为在语义空间中计算训练目标，我们将离散标记提升为连续特征，并应用多带宽漂移损失，逐步累积这些修正。为通过离散瓶颈反向传播梯度，我们采用直通估计器：前向传播时将精确硬采样的标记输入教师模型和解码器，确保训练与推理在同一码本流形上进行，同时将连续梯度路由回学生模型的logits。该完全可微路径还可选配无条件对抗目标以增强感知真实感。在类别和文本条件生成任务上的评估验证了框架的有效性。FPD在单次推理步骤内实现竞争力的视觉保真度和结构对齐，缩小与多步教师模型的差距，且优于现有离散蒸馏基线。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of iterative decoding in discrete diffusion models, this paper proposes Fixed-Point Distillation (FPD), an end-to-end framework that distills multi-step teacher behavior into a single-step student model. FPD generates local correction targets by partially corrupting the student’s draft and refining it with one teacher step, then lifts discrete tokens into continuous features to compute a multi-bandwidth drift loss that accumulates corrections semantically. To enable gradient flow through discrete sampling, it uses a straight-through estimator that passes hard-sampled tokens to the teacher during forward pass while routing gradients back to student logits, ensuring consistent codebook alignment. Experiments show FPD achieves competitive visual fidelity and structural alignment in one step, outperforming existing distillation baselines and narrowing the performance gap with multi-step teachers.
为解决离散扩散模型迭代解码效率低的问题，本文提出固定点蒸馏（FPD）框架，将多步教师模型的行为压缩至单步学生模型。FPD通过部分破坏学生生成的草稿并用教师单步修正，构建局部修正目标，并将离散token升维为连续特征，采用多频带漂移损失累积语义修正。为实现离散采样处的梯度回传，采用直通估计器在前向传播中将硬采样token送入教师网络，同时将连续梯度反向传递至学生logits，确保训练与推理在相同码本空间中进行。实验表明，FPD在单步推理下实现与多步教师相当的视觉保真度和结构对齐，优于现有蒸馏基线方法。
Velocityformer: Broken-Symmetry-Matched Equivariant Graph Transformers for Cosmological Velocity Reconstruction
Authors: Tilman Tröster, David Mirkovic, Veronika Oehl, Arne Thomsen
First: 2026-05-20T17:59:05+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-20T17:59:05+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Precise measurement of the kinematic Sunyaev-Zel'dovich (kSZ) effect - a probe of the large-scale distribution of baryonic matter, a key observable for cosmological inference - requires accurate reconstruction of galaxy velocities from spectroscopic surveys. The signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) of kSZ measurements scales directly with the correlation coefficient $r$ between reconstructed and true velocities. We introduce Velocityformer, an equivariant graph transformer architecture designed to match the specific symmetry of the observational data. While the underlying physics is equivariant with respect to translations and rotations, observational effects break this symmetry due to the preferred line-of-sight direction. Matching the model's inductive bias to the data's broken symmetry consistently improves performance across all model sizes and training volumes, with Velocityformer improving $r$ by 35% over the standard linear theory baseline and outperforming ML baselines at every data volume. By matching the model's inductive bias to the data and conditioning on the physics-based long-wavelength solution, Velocityformer is highly data-efficient, training to high accuracy on as few as 4 low-fidelity simulations, and generalises zero-shot across input geometry, cosmological parameters, and galaxy sample. On high-fidelity simulated galaxy catalogues, this yields a 30% improvement in $r$ over the physical baseline, directly translating to the same SNR gain on observational data.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Velocityformer：用于宇宙学速度重建的破对称匹配等变图Transformer
精确测量动能Sunyaev-Zel'dovich（kSZ）效应——一种探测星系际物质大尺度分布的关键宇宙学观测指标——需要从光谱巡天数据中准确重建星系速度。kSZ测量的信噪比（SNR）与重建速度和真实速度之间的相关系数r直接成正比。我们提出了Velocityformer，一种等变图Transformer架构，旨在匹配观测数据的特定对称性。虽然其物理本质在平移和旋转下保持等变，但观测效应因存在视线方向偏好而破坏了该对称性。将模型的归纳偏置与数据的破对称性相匹配，可显著提升所有模型规模和训练体积下的性能：Velocityformer相较标准线性理论基线提升r值35%，并在所有数据体积下超越现有机器学习基线。通过匹配模型归纳偏置与数据，并结合基于物理的长波长解进行条件建模，Velocityformer具有极高的数据效率，仅需4个低保真模拟即可训练至高精度，并能在输入几何、宇宙学参数和星系样本上实现零样本泛化。在高保真模拟星系目录上，该方法相较物理基线提升r值30%，直接转化为观测数据中相同的SNR增益。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to accurately reconstruct galaxy velocities for improved cosmological inference via the kinematic Sunyaev-Zel'dovich effect, the authors introduce Velocityformer, an equivariant graph transformer designed to align with the broken symmetries of observational data. Unlike standard models, Velocityformer matches its inductive bias to the data’s asymmetry due to the line-of-sight direction, while preserving physical symmetries. Experiments show it consistently outperforms baselines across all model sizes and training volumes, improving the velocity correlation coefficient r by 35% over linear theory and surpassing other ML methods. It also achieves high accuracy with minimal data—training on just 4 low-fidelity simulations—and generalizes zero-shot across input geometry, cosmological parameters, and galaxy samples, yielding a 30% gain in r over physical baselines on high-fidelity simulations, directly boosting signal-to-noise ratio on real data.
为提升星系速度重建精度以支持宇宙学推断，本文提出Velocityformer，一种设计用于匹配观测数据中对称性破缺的等变图Transformer架构。尽管物理定律在平移和旋转下保持不变，但观测效应引入了视线方向偏好，破坏了这种对称性。Velocityformer将这一对称性破缺纳入模型架构，使其在不同模型规模和训练数据量下均表现更优，相较标准线性理论基线提升相关系数r达35%，且在所有数据量下均优于其他机器学习方法。此外，该模型仅需四个低保真模拟即可达到高精度，且能零样本泛化至不同输入几何、宇宙学参数和星系样本。在高保真模拟数据上，其相较物理基线提升r达30%，直接转化为对观测数据信噪比的同等增益。
DeepWeb-Bench: A Deep Research Benchmark Demanding Massive Cross-Source Evidence and Long-Horizon Derivation
Authors: Sixiong Xie, Zhuofan Shi, Haiyang Shen, Jiuzheng Wang, Siqi Zhong, Mugeng Liu, Chongyang Pan, Peilun Jia, Baoqing Sun, Xiang Jing, Yun Ma
First: 2026-05-20T17:59:03+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-20T17:59:03+00:00
Comments: Work in Progress. 27 pages, 10 figures, 4 tables. Project page: https://sixiongxie1001-dot.github.io/deep-research-benchmark2.0
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Deep research, in which an agent searches the open web, collects evidence, and derives an answer through extended reasoning, is a prominent use case for frontier language models. Frontier deep research products score high on existing benchmarks, making it difficult to distinguish their capabilities from current evaluation data alone. We introduce DeepWeb-Bench, a deep research benchmark that is substantially harder than existing benchmarks for the current frontier. Difficulty comes from three properties of the data itself: each task requires massive evidence collection, cross-source reconciliation, and long-horizon multi-step derivation. We represent these three sources of difficulty as four capability families (Retrieval, Derivation, Reasoning, and Calibration) and report results sliced by family. Every reference answer is accompanied by a source-provenance record with four disclosure levels and cross-source checks where available, making scores easier to audit against the underlying evidence. We evaluate DeepWeb-Bench on nine frontier models and report three findings: (1) retrieval is not the bottleneck, as retrieval failures account for only 12-14% of errors while derivation and calibration failures account for over 70%; (2) strong and weak models fail in qualitatively different ways, with strong models' errors dominated by incomplete derivation and weak models' by hallucinated precision; and (3) models exhibit genuine specialization across domains, with cross-model agreement of only rho = 0.61 and per-case disagreement reaching 18.8 percentage points. The public benchmark release includes the data, rubrics, and evaluation code.
中文标题/摘要
标题：DeepWeb-Bench：一个需要大量跨源证据与长程推导的深度研究基准
深度研究——即代理在开放网络上搜索、收集证据并通过扩展推理得出答案——是前沿语言模型的重要应用场景。当前前沿深度研究产品在现有基准测试中得分较高，仅凭现有评估数据难以区分其能力差异。我们提出了DeepWeb-Bench，这是一个比现有基准更难的深度研究基准。其难度源于数据本身的三个特性：每个任务需大量证据收集、跨源协调与长程多步推导。我们将这三类难度归结为四个能力家族（检索、推导、推理与校准），并按家族报告结果。每个参考答案均附带来源溯源记录，包含四个披露层级及可用的跨源校验，便于对照底层证据审计分数。我们在九个前沿模型上评估DeepWeb-Bench，得出三项发现：（1）检索并非瓶颈，检索失败仅占错误的12-14%，而推导与校准失败占比超70%；（2）强弱模型失败方式质性不同，强模型错误主要源于推导不完整，弱模型则源于幻觉精度；（3）模型在不同领域表现出真实专业化，跨模型一致性仅为rho=0.61，单案例分歧高达18.8个百分点。公开基准包含数据、评分标准与评估代码。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to better evaluate frontier language models in deep research tasks, this paper introduces DeepWeb-Bench, a benchmark requiring agents to collect massive cross-source evidence and perform long-horizon reasoning. The benchmark is structured around four capability families—Retrieval, Derivation, Reasoning, and Calibration—to isolate performance bottlenecks. Experimental results across nine frontier models reveal that retrieval errors account for only 12-14% of failures, while derivation and calibration errors dominate over 70%. Strong models fail primarily due to incomplete derivation, whereas weak models suffer from hallucinated precision. Additionally, models show domain-specific specialization, with low cross-model agreement (rho = 0.61) and significant per-case disagreement (up to 18.8 percentage points).
为解决现有基准难以区分前沿语言模型的问题，本研究提出DeepWeb-Bench，要求代理收集海量跨源证据并进行长程推理。该基准按检索、推导、推理和校准四个能力维度设计，每个任务附带多层级溯源记录以支持可审计性。在九个前沿模型上的评估显示：检索错误仅占12–14%，推导与校准错误占比超70%；强模型错误主要源于推导不完整，弱模型则因幻觉精度不足；模型在不同领域表现出真实专业化，跨模型一致性仅为rho=0.61，单案例分歧最高达18.8个百分点。
AiraXiv: An AI-Driven Open-Access Platform for Human and AI Scientists
Authors: Junshu Pan, Panzhong Lu, Yixuan Weng, Qiyao Sun, Fang Guo, Zijie Yang, Qiji Zhou, Yue Zhang
First: 2026-05-20T17:59:03+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-20T17:59:03+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Recent advances in artificial intelligence (AI) have accelerated the growth of both human-authored and AI-generated research outputs, placing increasing strain on traditional academic publishing systems and challenging the scalability of conference- and journal-centered paradigms amid rising submission volumes, reviewer workload, and venue size. To address these challenges, we explore an AI-era publishing paradigm in which both human and AI scientists participate as authors and readers, and papers evolve through continuous, feedback-driven iteration. We propose AiraXiv, an AI-driven open-access platform built on open preprints, AI-augmented analysis and review, and reader feedback. AiraXiv supports human scientists through an interactive UI and AI scientists through Model Context Protocol (MCP)-based interactions. We validate AiraXiv through real-world deployments, including serving as the submission platform for ICAIS 2025, demonstrating its potential as a fast, inclusive, and scalable research infrastructure for the AI era. AiraXiv is publicly available at https://airaxiv.com.

Experimental validation through its deployment as the submission system for ICAIS 2025 demonstrates AiraXiv’s capacity to handle high-volume submissions efficiently, promote inclusivity, and scale sustainably in the AI era.

WikiVQABench: A Knowledge-Grounded Visual Question Answering Benchmark from Wikipedia and Wikidata
Authors: Basel Shbita, Pengyuan Li, Anna Lisa Gentile
First: 2026-05-20T17:58:24+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-20T17:58:24+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Visual Question Answering (VQA) benchmarks have largely emphasized perception-based tasks that can be solved from visual content alone. In contrast, many real-world scenarios require external knowledge that is not directly observable in the image to answer correctly. We introduce WikiVQABench, a human-curated knowledge-grounded VQA benchmark constructed by systematically combining Wikipedia images, their associated article captions, and structured knowledge from Wikidata. Our pipeline uses large language models (LLMs) to generate candidate multiple-choice image-question-answer sets. All generated instances are subsequently reviewed and curated by human annotators to ensure factual correctness, visual-text consistency, and that each question requires external knowledge in addition to visual evidence for correct resolution. WikiVQABench comprises a substantial collection of Wikipedia images with curated multiple-choice questions designed to benchmark knowledge-aware vision-language models (VLMs). Evaluation of fifteen VLMs (256M-90B parameters) reveals a wide performance range (24.7%-75.6% accuracy), demonstrating that the benchmark effectively discriminates model capabilities on knowledge-intensive reasoning. The dataset and benchmarking code are publicly available.
中文标题/摘要
标题：WikiVQABench：基于维基百科与维基数据的知识驱动视觉问答基准
视觉问答（VQA）基准主要侧重于仅依赖视觉内容即可解决的感知型任务。相比之下，许多现实场景需要图像中无法直接观察到的外部知识才能正确回答。我们引入了WikiVQABench，这是一个由人类精心编纂的知识驱动VQA基准，通过系统性结合维基百科图片、相关文章标题和维基数据中的结构化知识构建而成。我们的流程利用大语言模型（LLM）生成候选的多选图像-问题-答案集，随后由人工标注员审核和筛选，确保事实正确性、视觉-文本一致性，并且每个问题都需要除视觉证据外的外部知识才能正确解答。WikiVQABench包含大量维基百科图片及经过筛选的多选题，旨在评估知识感知视觉-语言模型（VLM）的能力。对十五个VLM（256M-90B参数）的评估显示其性能范围广泛（24.7%-75.6%准确率），表明该基准能有效区分模型在知识密集型推理中的能力。数据集和评估代码已公开。

They use large language models to generate candidate image-question-answer sets, which are then reviewed by humans for factual accuracy, visual-text alignment, and knowledge dependency. Latent Dynamics for Full Body Avatar Animation
Authors: Shichong Peng, Chengxiang Yin, Fei Jiang, Zhongshi Jiang, Lingchen Yang, Qingyang Tan, Amin Jourabloo, Jason Saragih, Ke Li, Christian Häne
First: 2026-05-20T17:58:03+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-20T17:58:03+00:00
Comments: Supplementary video: https://youtu.be/xjnr3YM0yIE
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Pose-driven full-body avatars built on neural rendering produce high-quality novel views of a captured subject. Yet loose clothing and other dynamic elements deform in ways pose alone cannot explain: the same pose can correspond to many different states, because their motion depends on history, inertia, and contact. Explicit simulation and layered-garment methods can model such dynamics, but they require either a dedicated garment template, which raw multi-view capture does not naturally provide, or a test-time physics simulator with non-trivial runtime cost. A parallel line of work learns data-driven clothing avatars that avoid explicit garment layers. These methods add an auxiliary latent for variation beyond pose; at inference, they fix it, regress it from pose, or retrieve it from training data, without explicitly modeling how the latent evolves with its own dynamics. Additionally, even in everyday motion with loose clothing, existing architectures often struggle to capture fine-grained detail, producing blurry renderings and temporal artifacts. We augment a pose-conditioned 3D Gaussian avatar with a transformer-based decoder and a dynamics residual latent that captures temporal appearance and geometry variation beyond the driving signals. At inference, a learned latent dynamics model evolves the residual latent from a short pose history and the previous latent state. The model decomposes each update into driving, restoring, and dissipative forces, producing temporally coherent, history-dependent rollouts with negligible added cost. Different initial conditions yield diverse yet plausible motion trajectories, and the force decomposition exposes controls such as stiffness. Across nine captured sequences of everyday motion with diverse loose garments, quantitative metrics and a perceptual user study show improved animation quality over recent data-driven baselines.
中文标题/摘要
标题：全身虚拟形象动画的隐式动力学建模
基于姿态驱动的神经渲染全身虚拟形象可生成高质量的捕获主体新视角。然而，宽松衣物及其他动态元素的形变无法仅由姿态解释：同一姿态可对应多种状态，因其运动依赖历史、惯性与接触。显式模拟与分层服装方法可建模此类动态，但需专用服装模板（原始多视角捕获不自然提供）或运行成本高昂的测试时物理模拟器。另一研究方向学习数据驱动的服装虚拟形象，避免显式服装层。这些方法添加辅助隐变量以表达姿态外的变异；推理时固定、回归或从训练数据检索该变量，而不显式建模隐变量自身动力学演化。此外，即使在日常宽松衣物运动中，现有架构常难以捕捉精细细节，导致渲染模糊与时间伪影。本文在姿态条件3D高斯虚拟形象基础上，添加基于Transformer的解码器与捕捉超驱动信号外观与几何变化的动态残差隐变量。推理时，学习到的隐动力学模型从短时姿态历史与前一隐状态演化残差隐变量。模型将每次更新分解为驱动、恢复与耗散力，生成时间一致、依赖历史的轨迹，且额外开销可忽略。不同初始条件生成多样但合理运动轨迹，力分解暴露如刚度等控制参数。在九组含多样宽松衣物的日常运动序列中，定量指标与感知用户研究显示优于近期数据驱动基线。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of animating loose clothing and dynamic elements in full-body avatars using only pose signals, this work introduces a latent dynamics model that augments a pose-conditioned 3D Gaussian avatar with a transformer-based decoder and a residual latent variable capturing temporal appearance and geometry variation. The model evolves this latent using a learned dynamics system that decomposes updates into driving, restoring, and dissipative forces, enabling history-dependent, temporally coherent animations without explicit physics simulation. Experiments across nine real-world motion sequences with diverse loose garments demonstrate improved rendering quality and temporal consistency compared to existing data-driven baselines, with user studies confirming perceptual superiority.
为了解决仅凭姿态信号难以准确模拟宽松衣物和动态元素的问题，该研究提出一种基于潜变量动力学的全身体态动画方法，通过在姿态驱动的3D高斯模型上增加Transformer解码器和残差潜变量，捕捉超出姿态驱动的时序外观与几何变化。模型利用学习到的动力学系统演化潜变量，将其更新分解为驱动、恢复与耗散力，从而实现依赖历史、时间一致的动画，无需显式物理模拟。在九组包含多样宽松衣物的日常动作序列实验中，该方法在定量指标和感知评估上均优于近期数据驱动基线，生成更清晰、无伪影的动画，且计算开销极小。
Is Fixing Schema Graphs Necessary? Full-Resolution Graph Structure Learning for Relational Deep Learning
Authors: Yi Huang, Qingyun Sun, Jia Li, Xingcheng Fu, Jianxin Li
First: 2026-05-20T17:56:09+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-20T17:56:09+00:00
Comments: Accepted by the Forty-third International Conference on Machine Learning (ICML2026)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Relational prediction tasks are fundamental in many real-world applications, where data are naturally stored in relational databases (RDBs). Relational Deep Learning (RDL) addresses this problem by modeling RDBs as graphs and applying graph neural networks (GNNs) for end-to-end learning. However, the full-resolution property is commonly adopted as a design principle in graph construction for RDBs to preserve relational semantics, which leads most existing methods to rely on fixed graph structures. In this paper, we propose FROG, a Full-Resolution and Optimizable Graph Structure Learning} framework for RDL that formulates relational structure learning as a learnable table role modeling problem, allowing tables to contribute as nodes and edges in message passing. We further design role-driven message passing mechanisms to capture relational semantics, enabling joint optimization of graph structure and GNN representations. To ensure semantic consistency, we introduce functional dependency constraints that regularize representations across table and entity levels. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method outperforms existing approaches and reveal how table roles impact downstream tasks, offering new insights into graph construction for RDL
中文标题/摘要
标题：修复模式图是必要的吗？面向关系深度学习的全分辨率图结构学习
关系预测任务在许多现实应用中至关重要，数据天然存储于关系数据库（RDB）中。关系深度学习（RDL）通过将RDB建模为图并应用图神经网络（GNN）实现端到端学习。然而，为保留关系语义，图构建常采用全分辨率设计原则，导致现有方法多依赖固定图结构。本文提出FROG框架，面向RDL实现全分辨率且可优化的图结构学习，将关系结构学习建模为可学习的表角色建模问题，使表可作为消息传递中的节点与边。我们进一步设计角色驱动的消息传递机制以捕捉关系语义，实现图结构与GNN表示的联合优化。为确保语义一致性，引入函数依赖约束以规范表与实体层级的表示。大量实验表明，本方法优于现有方法，并揭示表角色对下游任务的影响，为RDL图构建提供新见解。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to preserve relational semantics in relational databases while enabling flexible graph structure learning, this paper introduces FROG, a framework that treats table roles as learnable components in graph construction for relational deep learning. FROG allows tables to act as nodes and edges in message passing, and employs role-driven mechanisms to capture relational semantics while jointly optimizing graph structure and GNN representations. Functional dependency constraints are added to ensure semantic consistency across table and entity levels. Experimental results show FROG outperforms existing methods and reveals how table roles influence downstream performance, offering new insights into graph design for relational tasks.
为在保持关系数据库语义完整性的同时实现灵活的图结构学习，本文提出FROG框架，将表角色建模为可学习实体，使表可作为图中节点与边参与消息传递。该框架设计角色驱动的消息传递机制以捕捉关系语义，并联合优化图结构与图神经网络表示，同时引入函数依赖约束以确保表与实体层级间的语义一致性。实验表明，FROG在多个任务上优于现有方法，且揭示表角色对下游任务性能具有显著影响，为关系深度学习中的图构建提供了新思路。
Stream3D: Sequential Multi-View 3D Generation via Evidential Memory
Authors: Kaichen Zhou, Zeyang Bai, Xinhai Chang, Mengyu Wang, Paul Liang, Fangneng Zhan
First: 2026-05-20T17:55:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-20T17:55:16+00:00
Comments: Multi-view 3D Generation, Streaming 3D Generation
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
View-conditioned 3D generators such as SAM 3D, TRELLIS and Hunyuan3D produce high-quality object reconstructions from a single view, but real-world visual observation often arrives as long monocular streams. Naively applying these generators to each streaming frame independently leads to severe temporal inconsistency in the generated results. To address this problem, we propose Stream3D, the first training-free streaming mechanism that turns a frozen view-conditioned 3D generator into a streaming generator with constant cross-chunk memory. Stream3D achieves this by maintaining a compact evidential memory, which selectively caches the most informative historical frames based on a proposed evidence score mechanism. As the stream progresses, the memory dynamically updates to retain a fixed number of informative frames, preventing the memory footprint from growing linearly with sequence length. This also prevents degradation over long sequences and keeps the underlying generator completely unchanged without retraining, architectural modifications, or auxiliary losses. Evaluated on both realistic and synthetic streaming benchmarks, Stream3D outperforms latent-transport baselines, including KV-cache reuse and flow-based feature editing, across both photometric and geometric metrics. More details can be found at: https://anonymous-submission-20.github.io/streaming3D.github.io/.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Stream3D：通过证据记忆实现顺序多视角3D生成
如SAM 3D、TRELLIS和Hunyuan3D等基于视角的3D生成器可从单视角生成高质量物体重建，但现实世界视觉观测常以长时单目流形式到达。若将这些生成器直接应用于每个流帧，会导致生成结果严重的时间不一致性。为解决此问题，我们提出Stream3D，这是首个无需训练的流式机制，可将冻结的视角条件3D生成器转化为具有恒定跨块记忆的流式生成器。Stream3D通过维护一个紧凑的证据记忆，根据所提证据评分机制选择性缓存最具信息量的历史帧。随着流的推进，记忆动态更新以保留固定数量的信息帧，避免记忆占用随序列长度线性增长，同时防止长序列退化，且无需重新训练、架构修改或辅助损失，保持底层生成器完全不变。在真实与合成流式基准上评估，Stream3D在光度与几何指标上均优于包括KV缓存复用和基于流的特征编辑在内的潜在传输基线。更多详情见：https://anonymous-submission-20.github.io/streaming3D.github.io/。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to generate temporally consistent 3D reconstructions from long monocular video streams, Stream3D introduces a training-free method that adapts frozen view-conditioned 3D generators for streaming input by maintaining a compact evidential memory. This memory selectively caches the most informative historical frames using an evidence score mechanism, dynamically updating to retain a fixed number of frames without linear growth in memory footprint. Experimental results on both realistic and synthetic streaming benchmarks show that Stream3D outperforms existing latent-transport baselines—including KV-cache reuse and flow-based feature editing—across photometric and geometric metrics, without requiring retraining, architectural changes, or auxiliary losses.
为解决从长单目视频流中生成时序一致3D重建的问题，Stream3D提出一种无需训练的方法，将冻结的视图条件3D生成器适配为流式输入。该方法通过维护一个紧凑的证据记忆，利用证据评分机制选择性缓存最具信息量的历史帧，并动态更新以仅保留固定数量帧，避免内存随序列长度线性增长。此方法无需重训练或架构修改，保持生成器原状，并在真实与合成流式基准上，于光度与几何指标上超越现有基于潜变量传输的基线方法。
Agent JIT Compilation for Latency-Optimizing Web Agent Planning and Scheduling
Authors: Caleb Winston, Ron Yifeng Wang, Azalia Mirhoseini, Christos Kozyrakis
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-05-20T17:54:27+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-20T17:54:27+00:00
Comments: Accepted at ICML 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Computer-use agents (CUA) automate tasks specified with natural language such as "order the cheapest item from Taco Bell" by generating sequences of calls to tools such as click, type, and scroll on a browser. Current implementations follow a sequential fetch-screenshot-execute loop where each iteration requires an LLM call, resulting in high latency and frequent errors from incorrect tool use. We present agent just-in-time (JIT) compilation, an alternative that compiles task descriptions directly into executable code that is free to include LLM calls, tool calls, and parallelization. Our approach comprises three components: (1) JIT-Planner, which generates multiple code plans, validates each against tool specifications, and selects the minimum-cost candidate; (2) JIT-Scheduler, which explores parallelization strategies via Monte Carlo cost estimation from learned latency distributions; and (3) an invariant-enforcing tool protocol specifying precondition and postcondition state requirements that reduce the rate of generating plans with incorrect tool use. Across 5 web applications, JIT-Planner achieves $10.4\times$ speedup and $+28\%$ accuracy over Browser-Use, while JIT-Scheduler achieves $2.4\times$ speedup and $+9\%$ accuracy over OpenAI CUA.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向低延迟的Web代理规划与调度的代理即时编译
计算机使用代理（CUA）通过生成一系列对浏览器工具（如点击、输入、滚动）的调用序列，自动执行用自然语言指定的任务（如“从塔可钟订购最便宜的商品”）。当前实现采用顺序的获取-截图-执行循环，每次迭代都需要大语言模型（LLM）调用，导致高延迟和因工具使用错误而频繁出错。我们提出代理即时编译（JIT），一种替代方案，可直接将任务描述编译为可执行代码，允许包含LLM调用、工具调用和并行化。我们的方法包含三个组件：（1）JIT-规划器，生成多个代码计划，验证每个计划与工具规范的兼容性，并选择成本最低的候选方案；（2）JIT-调度器，通过从学习到的延迟分布中进行蒙特卡洛成本估算，探索并行化策略；（3）一个不变量约束的工具协议，规定前置和后置状态要求，降低生成错误工具使用计划的频率。在5个Web应用中，JIT-规划器相比Browser-Use实现10.4倍加速和28%准确率提升，JIT-调度器相比OpenAI CUA实现2.4倍加速和9%准确率提升。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high latency and frequent errors in current computer-use agents due to repeated LLM calls in a fetch-screenshot-execute loop, this work introduces agent just-in-time (JIT) compilation to directly generate executable code from natural language tasks, incorporating LLM and tool calls alongside parallelization. The method comprises three components: JIT-Planner, which generates and validates multiple code plans to select the lowest-cost one; JIT-Scheduler, which uses Monte Carlo sampling over learned latency distributions to optimize parallel execution; and an invariant-enforcing tool protocol to reduce incorrect tool usage. Experiments across five web applications show JIT-Planner achieves 10.4x speedup and +28% accuracy over Browser-Use, while JIT-Scheduler delivers 2.4x speedup and +9% accuracy over OpenAI CUA.
为解决当前计算机使用代理因重复调用大语言模型导致的高延迟和频繁工具误用问题，本文提出代理即时编译（JIT）方法，直接将自然语言任务编译为可执行代码，集成大语言模型调用、工具调用与并行化。该方法包含三个模块：JIT-Planner生成并验证多个代码计划，选择成本最低方案；JIT-Scheduler通过蒙特卡洛成本估计结合学习到的延迟分布探索并行策略；工具协议通过前置与后置状态不变量约束减少错误工具调用。在五个网页应用上的实验表明，JIT-Planner相较Browser-Use提速10.4倍、准确率提升28%，JIT-Scheduler相较OpenAI CUA提速2.4倍、准确率提升9%。
MeMo: Memory as a Model
Authors: Ryan Wei Heng Quek, Sanghyuk Lee, Alfred Wei Lun Leong, Arun Verma, Alok Prakash, Nancy F. Chen, Bryan Kian Hsiang Low, Daniela Rus, Armando Solar-Lezama
First: 2026-05-14T17:51:34+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-20T17:53:38+00:00
Comments: MeMo augments any LLM with up-to-date or domain-specific knowledge via a trained memory model, avoiding costly retraining, mitigating catastrophic forgetting, and remaining robust to retrieval noise
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models (LLMs) achieve strong performance across a wide range of tasks, but remain frozen after pretraining until subsequent updates. Many real-world applications require timely, domain-specific information, motivating the need for efficient mechanisms to incorporate new knowledge. In this paper, we introduce MeMo (Memory as a Model), a modular framework that encodes new knowledge into a dedicated memory model while keeping the LLM parameters unchanged. Compared to existing methods, MeMo offers several advantages: (a) it captures complex cross-document relationships, (b) it is robust to retrieval noise, (c) it avoids catastrophic forgetting in the LLM, (d) it does not require access to the LLM's weights or output logits, enabling plug-and-play integration with both open and proprietary closed-source LLMs, and (e) its retrieval cost is independent of corpus size at inference time. Our experimental results on three benchmarks, BrowseComp-Plus, NarrativeQA, and MuSiQue, show that MeMo achieves strong performance compared to existing methods across diverse settings.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MeMo：将记忆作为模型
大型语言模型（LLMs）在多种任务中表现出色，但在预训练后直至后续更新前保持不变。许多现实应用需要及时、特定领域的信息，因此亟需高效机制来整合新知识。本文提出MeMo（Memory as a Model），一种模块化框架，将新知识编码到专用记忆模型中，同时保持LLM参数不变。与现有方法相比，MeMo具有以下优势：(a) 捕获跨文档的复杂关系，(b) 对检索噪声鲁棒，(c) 避免LLM的灾难性遗忘，(d) 不需要访问LLM的权重或输出logits，支持与开源和闭源专有LLM的即插即用集成，(e) 推理时检索成本与语料库大小无关。我们在三个基准（BrowseComp-Plus、NarrativeQA和MuSiQue）上的实验结果表明，MeMo在多种设置下均优于现有方法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to update large language models with timely, domain-specific knowledge without retraining, MeMo introduces a modular framework that encodes new information into a separate memory model while leaving the LLM parameters untouched. This approach enables the model to capture complex cross-document relationships, resist retrieval noise, and avoid catastrophic forgetting, without requiring access to the LLM’s internal weights or logits, allowing plug-and-play compatibility with both open and closed-source models. Experimental results across three benchmarks—BrowseComp-Plus, NarrativeQA, and MuSiQue—demonstrate that MeMo consistently outperforms existing methods, with retrieval costs remaining constant regardless of corpus size at inference time.
为了解决大语言模型在预训练后无法及时更新领域知识的问题，MeMo提出一种模块化框架，将新知识编码到独立的记忆模型中，而不修改原始语言模型的参数。该方法能捕捉跨文档的复杂关系，抵御检索噪声，避免灾难性遗忘，且无需访问语言模型的内部权重或输出logits，支持与开源和闭源模型的即插即用集成。在BrowseComp-Plus、NarrativeQA和MuSiQue三个基准测试中，实验结果表明MeMo在多种设置下均优于现有方法，且其检索开销与语料库大小无关。
You Only Need Minimal RLVR Training: Extrapolating LLMs via Rank-1 Trajectories
Authors: Zhepei Wei, Xinyu Zhu, Wei-Lin Chen, Chengsong Huang, Jiaxin Huang, Yu Meng
First: 2026-05-20T17:53:20+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-20T17:53:20+00:00
Comments: preprint. Code: https://github.com/weizhepei/RELEX
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has become a dominant paradigm for improving reasoning in large language models (LLMs), yet the underlying geometry of the resulting parameter trajectories remains underexplored. In this work, we demonstrate that RLVR weight trajectories are extremely low-rank and highly predictable. Specifically, we find that the majority of downstream performance gains are captured by a rank-1 approximation of the parameter deltas, where the magnitude of this projection evolves near-linearly with training steps. Motivated by this, we propose a simple and compute-efficient method RELEX (REinforcement Learning EXtrapolation), which estimates the rank-1 subspace from a short observation window and extrapolates future checkpoints via linear regression, with no learned model required. Across three models (i.e., Qwen2.5-Math-1.5B, Qwen3-4B-Base, and Qwen3-8B-Base), RELEX produces checkpoints that match or exceed RLVR performance on both in-domain and out-of-domain benchmarks, requiring as few as 15% steps of full RLVR training. Remarkably, RELEX is able to extrapolate far beyond the observation window at no training cost, predicting checkpoints up to 10-20$\times$ beyond the observed prefix with continued improvement (e.g., observe only the first 50 steps and extrapolate to 1000 steps). Our ablation analysis confirms the minimalist sufficiency of RELEX: neither increasing the subspace rank nor employing non-linear modeling yields further gains in extrapolation. Finally, we show that RELEX's success stems from a "denoising" effect: by projecting updates onto the rank-1 subspace, the model discards stochastic optimization noise that would otherwise degrade performance during extrapolation. Our code is available at https://github.com/weizhepei/RELEX.
中文标题/摘要
标题：你只需极少量RLVR训练：通过秩1轨迹外推LLM
可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）已成为提升大语言模型（LLM）推理能力的主流范式，但其生成的参数轨迹几何结构仍鲜有研究。本文证明RLVR权重轨迹具有极低秩且高度可预测的特性：大部分下游性能增益可由参数增量的秩1近似捕捉，且该投影的幅度随训练步数近似线性增长。受此启发，我们提出一种简单且计算高效的RELEX（REinforcement Learning EXtrapolation）方法，仅需从短窗口观测中估计秩1子空间，再通过线性回归外推未来检查点，无需任何学习模型。在三个模型（Qwen2.5-Math-1.5B、Qwen3-4B-Base、Qwen3-8B-Base）上，RELEX生成的检查点在域内与域外基准测试中均达到或超越RLVR性能，仅需完整RLVR训练的15%步数。更令人惊讶的是，RELEX可在零训练成本下外推远超观测窗口，预测可达观测前缀10-20倍的检查点并持续提升（如仅观测前50步，外推至1000步）。消融分析证实RELEX的极简性：提升子空间秩或采用非线性建模均无法进一步提升外推效果。最后，我们表明RELEX的成功源于“去噪”效应：通过将更新投影至秩1子空间，模型剔除了会损害外推性能的随机优化噪声。代码见https://github.com/weizhepei/RELEX。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observation that RLVR parameter trajectories in LLMs are low-rank and predictable, the authors propose RELEX, a method that extrapolates future model checkpoints using a rank-1 subspace estimated from a short training window and linear regression. RELEX achieves performance matching or exceeding full RLVR training with as little as 15% of the steps, and can predict checkpoints up to 10-20 times beyond the observed prefix without additional training. Experiments across three LLMs confirm that rank-1 projections are sufficient for extrapolation, and ablation studies show no benefit from higher-rank or non-linear models. The method’s success is attributed to a "denoising" effect, where projecting updates onto the rank-1 subspace removes stochastic noise that would otherwise degrade extrapolated performance.
鉴于RLVR训练中LLM参数轨迹具有低秩且可预测的特性，作者提出RELEX方法，通过从短窗口中估计秩1子空间并结合线性回归来外推未来模型检查点。该方法仅需15%的完整RLVR训练步数即可达到或超越其性能，并能无训练成本外推至观察窗口10-20倍长度的检查点。在三个LLM模型上的实验表明，秩1子空间已足够，增加秩或采用非线性模型并无提升。其成功归因于通过投影滤除优化噪声，从而在外推过程中提升模型稳定性与性能。
DelTA: Discriminative Token Credit Assignment for Reinforcement Learning from Verifiable Rewards
Authors: Kaiyi Zhang, Wei Wu, Yankai Lin
First: 2026-05-20T17:53:09+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-20T17:53:09+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Reinforcement learning from verifiable rewards (RLVR) has emerged as a central technique for improving the reasoning capabilities of large language models. Despite its effectiveness, how response-level rewards translate into token-level probability changes remains poorly understood. We introduce a discriminator view of RLVR updates, showing that the policy-gradient update direction implicitly acts as a linear discriminator over token-gradient vectors and thereby determines which token probabilities are increased or decreased during learning. Under standard sequence-level RLVR, this discriminator is constructed from positive- and negative-side centroids formed by advantage-weighted averaging of token-gradient vectors. However, such centroid construction can be dominated by shared high-frequency patterns, such as formatting tokens, diluting sparse yet discriminative directions that better distinguish high-reward responses from low-reward ones. To address this limitation, we propose $\textbf{DelTA}$, a discriminative token credit assignment method that estimates token coefficients to amplify side-specific token-gradient directions and downweight shared or weakly discriminative ones. These coefficients reweight a self-normalized RLVR surrogate, making the effective side-wise centroids more contrastive and thereby reshaping the RLVR update direction. On seven mathematical benchmarks, DelTA outperforms the strongest same-scale baselines by 3.26 and 2.62 average points on Qwen3-8B-Base and Qwen3-14B-Base, respectively. Additional results on code generation, a different backbone, and out-of-domain evaluations further demonstrate the generalization ability of DelTA.
中文标题/摘要
标题：DelTA：用于可验证奖励强化学习的判别式标记信用分配
可验证奖励强化学习（RLVR）已成为提升大语言模型推理能力的核心技术。尽管其效果显著，但响应级奖励如何转化为标记级概率变化仍不甚明了。我们从判别器视角阐释RLVR更新机制，表明策略梯度更新方向隐式地充当标记梯度向量上的线性判别器，从而决定学习过程中哪些标记概率被提升或降低。在标准序列级RLVR中，该判别器由正负侧标记梯度向量经优势加权平均形成的质心构成。然而，此类质心构建易受高频共享模式（如格式标记）主导，稀疏但更具判别力的方向（能更好区分高奖励与低奖励响应）被稀释。为解决此局限，我们提出DelTA，一种判别式标记信用分配方法，通过估计标记系数放大侧向特定的标记梯度方向，弱化共享或弱判别性方向。这些系数重新加权自归一化RLVR代理目标，使有效侧向质心更具对比性，从而重塑RLVR更新方向。在七个数学基准测试中，DelTA在Qwen3-8B-Base和Qwen3-14B-Base上分别优于最强同规模基线3.26和2.62平均分。在代码生成、不同骨干模型及跨域评估中的额外结果进一步验证了DelTA的泛化能力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to better understand how token-level probability updates are driven by response-level rewards in reinforcement learning from verifiable rewards, the paper introduces DelTA, a discriminative token credit assignment method that reweights token gradients to enhance contrast between high- and low-reward directions. It achieves this by estimating token-specific coefficients that amplify discriminative token-gradient components while suppressing shared or weakly informative patterns. Experiments on seven mathematical benchmarks show DelTA improves performance over strong baselines by 3.26 and 2.62 average points on Qwen3-8B-Base and Qwen3-14B-Base, respectively, with additional results confirming its generalization across code generation and out-of-domain settings.
为解决响应级奖励如何转化为token级概率更新这一机制不清晰的问题，本文提出DelTA方法，通过为每个token分配系数来强化区分高奖励与低奖励响应的梯度方向，同时抑制共享或弱区分性模式，从而重塑策略梯度更新方向。在七个数学基准测试中，DelTA在Qwen3-8B-Base和Qwen3-14B-Base上分别比最强基线高出3.26和2.62分，且在代码生成和跨域任务中也表现出良好泛化能力。
Convergence Analysis of Newton's Method for Neural Networks in the Overparameterized Limit
Authors: Konstantin Riedl, Konstantinos Spiliopoulos, Justin Sirignano
First: 2026-05-08T18:02:37+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-20T17:52:58+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
A convergence analysis is developed for the regularized Newton method for training neural networks (NNs) in the overparameterized limit. As the number of hidden units tends to infinity, the NN training dynamics converge in probability to the solution of a deterministic limit equation involving a ``Newton neural tangent kernel'' (NNTK). Explicit rates characterizing this convergence are provided and, in the infinite-width limit, we prove that the NN converges exponentially fast to the target data (i.e., a global minimizer with zero loss). We show that this convergence is uniform across the frequency spectrum, addressing the spectral bias inherent in gradient descent. The eigenvalues of the NTK for gradient descent accumulate at zero, leading to slow convergence for target data with high-frequency components. In contrast, the NNTK has uniformly lower bounded eigenvalues if the regularization parameter is selected appropriately, allowing Newton's method to converge more quickly for data with high-frequency components. Mathematical challenges that need to be addressed in our analysis include the implicit parameter update of the Newton method with a potentially indefinite Hessian matrix and the fact that the dimension of this linear system of equations tends to infinity as the NN width grows. This complicates deriving the training dynamics in the overparameterized limit as well as proving the convergence of the finite-width dynamics thereto. The analysis identifies a scaling formula for selecting the regularization parameter, which we show can vanish at a suitable rate as the number of hidden units becomes larger. We prove that, for sufficiently large numbers of hidden units, the regularized Hessian remains positive definite during training and the Newton updates for individual NN parameters converge to zero, showing that the model behaves as a linearization around the initialization.
中文标题/摘要
标题：过参数化极限下神经网络牛顿法的收敛性分析
本文发展了用于训练神经网络的正则化牛顿法在过参数化极限下的收敛性分析。当隐藏单元数量趋于无穷时，神经网络训练动力学以概率收敛于一个确定性极限方程的解，该方程涉及“牛顿神经切线核”（NNTK）。本文给出了描述该收敛性的显式速率，并证明在无限宽度极限下，神经网络对目标数据（即损失为零的全局最小值）呈指数快速收敛。我们进一步表明，该收敛性在频率谱上是均匀的，解决了梯度下降法固有的频谱偏差问题。梯度下降法的NTK特征值在零处累积，导致对高频目标数据收敛缓慢；相比之下，若正则化参数选择得当，NNTK的特征值具有统一的下界，使得牛顿法对高频数据收敛更快。本文分析中需解决的数学挑战包括：牛顿法隐式参数更新与可能非定正的Hessian矩阵，以及随着神经网络宽度增长，该线性方程组维度趋于无穷，这使得推导过参数化极限下的训练动力学及证明有限宽度动力学收敛至该极限变得复杂。分析还确定了正则化参数的缩放公式，我们证明该参数可随隐藏单元数增大以适当速率趋近于零。我们证明，当隐藏单元数足够大时，正则化Hessian矩阵在训练过程中保持正定，且各神经网络参数的牛顿更新收敛至零，表明模型在初始化附近表现为线性化。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to understand convergence behavior in overparameterized neural networks, this paper analyzes the regularized Newton method under the infinite-width limit. It derives a deterministic limit equation governed by a Newton neural tangent kernel (NNTK), showing that the method converges exponentially fast to a global minimum with zero loss. Unlike gradient descent, which suffers from slow convergence on high-frequency data due to NTK eigenvalues accumulating at zero, the NNTK maintains uniformly bounded eigenvalues when regularization is appropriately scaled, enabling faster convergence across all frequencies. The analysis overcomes technical challenges including indefinite Hessians and infinite-dimensional linear systems, proving that the regularized Hessian remains positive definite and Newton updates vanish, confirming linearization around initialization.
为理解牛顿法在过参数化神经网络中的收敛行为，本文在无限宽度极限下对正则化牛顿法进行了收敛分析。该方法基于牛顿神经切线核（NNTK）驱动的确定性极限方程，可推导出显式收敛速率并实现指数级零损失收敛。与梯度下降法不同，其神经切线核特征值在零处累积，导致对高频数据收敛缓慢，而NNTK在适当正则化下保持特征值下界，从而加速高频数据收敛。分析解决了Hessian矩阵不定及无限维线性系统等数学难题，并推导出随网络宽度增长而衰减的正则化参数缩放公式，确保Hessian正定且参数更新收敛至零。
Retrieval-Augmented Code Generation: A Survey with Focus on Repository-Level Approaches
Authors: Yicheng Tao, Yuante Li, Yao Qin, Yepang Liu
First: 2025-10-06T15:20:03+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-20T17:52:18+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) have significantly improved automated code generation. While existing approaches have achieved strong performance at the function and file levels, real-world software engineering requires reasoning over entire repositories, including cross-file dependencies, evolving execution environments, and global semantic consistency. This challenge has led to the emergence of Repository-Level Code Generation (RLCG), where models must retrieve, organize, and utilize repository-scale context to generate coherent and executable code changes. To address these challenges, Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) has become an increasingly important paradigm for repository-level code intelligence. In this survey, we present a comprehensive review of Retrieval-Augmented Code Generation (RACG), with a particular focus on repository-level approaches. Rather than viewing RACG as a static ``retrieve-then-generate'' pipeline, we characterize it as a coupled and evolving process involving context construction, retrieval optimization, generation, and environment interaction. We organize existing methods through a unified analytical framework spanning retrieval substrate, control regime, and evaluation setting. Based on this framework, we systematically examine retrieval strategies, graph-based and non-graph-based retrieval paradigms, training-driven optimizations, and autonomous agent architectures. We further summarize widely used datasets, benchmarks, and system configurations, and discuss key challenges including scalability, reliability, efficiency, and the necessity boundary between RACG and long-context LLMs. Through this survey, we aim to provide a structured understanding of the rapidly evolving RACG landscape and highlight promising directions for future AI-powered software engineering research.
中文标题/摘要
标题：检索增强代码生成：聚焦仓库级方法的综述
大型语言模型（LLMs）的最新进展显著提升了自动化代码生成能力。尽管现有方法在函数和文件级别已取得良好性能，但现实世界软件工程需在完整仓库层面进行推理，包括跨文件依赖、演化执行环境及全局语义一致性。这一挑战催生了仓库级代码生成（RLCG），要求模型检索、组织并利用仓库级上下文以生成连贯且可执行的代码变更。为应对这些挑战，检索增强生成（RAG）已成为仓库级代码智能的重要范式。本综述全面回顾了检索增强代码生成（RACG），特别聚焦仓库级方法。我们不将RACG视为静态的“检索后生成”流水线，而是将其视为涉及上下文构建、检索优化、生成及环境交互的耦合演化过程。我们通过统一分析框架（涵盖检索基底、控制机制与评估设置）组织现有方法，并系统考察检索策略、基于图与非图检索范式、训练驱动优化及自主代理架构。此外，我们总结了常用数据集、基准与系统配置，并讨论关键挑战，包括可扩展性、可靠性、效率及RACG与长上下文LLMs的必要边界。本综述旨在为快速演进的RACG领域提供结构化理解，并突出未来AI驱动软件工程研究的有前景方向。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for code generation systems to handle complex, cross-file repository contexts in real-world software engineering, this survey examines Retrieval-Augmented Code Generation (RACG) with a focus on repository-level approaches. It characterizes RACG as an evolving, coupled process involving context construction, retrieval, generation, and environment interaction, rather than a static pipeline. The survey organizes existing methods using a unified framework covering retrieval substrate, control regime, and evaluation setting, and systematically reviews retrieval strategies, graph-based and non-graph-based paradigms, training-driven optimizations, and agent architectures. It also summarizes key datasets, benchmarks, and system configurations, while highlighting challenges such as scalability, reliability, and the boundary between RACG and long-context LLMs, aiming to provide a structured overview of the field and guide future research.
为应对现实软件工程中跨文件、依赖复杂、环境动态变化的代码生成需求，本综述聚焦于仓库级检索增强代码生成（RACG）方法。文章将RACG定义为包含上下文构建、检索、生成及环境交互的动态耦合过程，而非简单“检索后生成”流程。通过统一框架（检索基底、控制机制、评估场景）系统梳理现有方法，涵盖图结构与非图结构检索、训练驱动优化、自主代理架构等策略，并总结常用数据集、基准与系统配置，指出可扩展性、可靠性、效率及与长上下文LLM边界等关键挑战，旨在为快速演进的RACG领域提供结构化认知并指引未来研究方向。
Stable Personas: Dual-Assessment of Temporal Stability in LLM-Based Human Simulation
Authors: Jana Gonnermann-Müller, Jennifer Haase, Nicolas Leins, Thomas Kosch, Sebastian Pokutta
First: 2026-01-30T10:38:52+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-20T17:52:10+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large Language Models (LLMs) acting as artificial agents offer the potential for scalable behavioral research, yet their validity depends on whether LLMs can maintain stable personas across extended conversations. We address this point using a dual-assessment framework measuring both self-reported characteristics and observer-rated persona expression. Across two experiments testing four persona conditions (default, high, moderate, and low ADHD presentations), seven LLMs, and three semantically equivalent persona prompts, we examine between-conversation stability (3,473 conversations) and within-conversation stability (1,370 conversations and 18 turns). Self-reports remain highly stable both between and within conversations. However, observer ratings reveal a tendency for persona expressions to decline during extended conversations. These findings suggest that persona-instructed LLMs produce stable, persona-aligned self-reports, an important prerequisite for behavioral research, while identifying this regression tendency as a boundary condition for multi-agent social simulation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：稳定的人格：基于大语言模型的人类模拟中时间稳定性的双重评估
作为人工代理的大语言模型（LLMs）为可扩展的行为研究提供了潜力，但其有效性取决于LLMs能否在长时间对话中保持稳定的人格。我们采用双重评估框架，同时测量自我报告特征和观察者对人格表达的评分。在两项实验中，测试了四种人格条件（默认、高、中、低ADHD表现）、七种LLMs及三个语义等价的人格提示，考察了对话间稳定性（3,473次对话）和对话内稳定性（1,370次对话及18轮）。自我报告在对话间和对话内均保持高度稳定。然而，观察者评分显示，人格表达在长时间对话中倾向于下降。这些发现表明，人格指令引导的LLMs能产生稳定且符合人格的自我报告，这是行为研究的重要前提，同时指出这种退化趋势是多智能体社会模拟的边界条件。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates whether large language models (LLMs) can maintain consistent personas over time, a critical requirement for reliable behavioral research. Using a dual-assessment method that combines self-reported traits and observer-rated persona expression, the researchers tested four ADHD persona conditions across seven LLMs and three prompt variants, analyzing both between-conversation (3,473) and within-conversation (1,370 conversations, 18 turns) stability. Results show that LLMs consistently report stable self-characteristics regardless of conversation length, but observer ratings indicate a decline in persona expression over extended interactions. This suggests that while LLMs reliably self-identify with assigned personas, their external behavioral consistency weakens over time, posing a limitation for long-term multi-agent simulations.
本研究旨在探究大型语言模型（LLM）在长时间对话中能否保持人格一致性，这是行为研究可靠性的关键前提。研究采用自我报告与观察者评分相结合的双评估方法，测试四种ADHD人格条件，覆盖七种LLM及三种语义等价提示词，分析跨对话（3473次）与内对话（1370次对话、18轮）的稳定性。结果显示，LLM自我报告的人格特征在对话过程中高度稳定，但观察者评分显示人格表达随对话延长而下降，表明自我报告可靠，但人格表现可能随时间衰减，限制其在长期社交模拟中的应用。
StreamGVE: Training-Free Video Editing via Few-Step Streaming Video Generation
Authors: Guanlong Jiao, Chenyangguang Zhang, Jia Jun Cheng Xian, Zewei Zhang, Renjie Liao
First: 2026-05-20T17:52:10+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-20T17:52:10+00:00
Comments: Project Page: https://dsl-lab.github.io/StreamGVE/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Although existing video editing methods are generally feasible, they often require many costly iterations and still struggle to deliver high-quality yet satisfying editing results. We attribute this limitation to the prevalent data-to-data paradigm, which is less compatible with modern generative models than noise-to-data generation. To address this gap, we revisit video editing from a noise-to-data perspective and propose Streaming-Generation-based Video Editing (StreamGVE), which preserves few-step sampling while seamlessly injecting source-video conditions. Built on pre-trained streaming generation models, StreamGVE introduces dual-branch fast sampling with a self-attention bridge and cross-attention grounding/boosting to satisfy both sampling and conditioning requirements. We further propose source-oriented guidance to improve target-generation quality, and a visual prompting strategy to enhance editing flexibility and practicality. The method is effective, robust, and generalizable across different models. Extensive experiments on diverse video editing tasks show that StreamGVE consistently outperforms existing approaches, even in few-step settings with minimal time cost.
中文标题/摘要
标题：StreamGVE：通过几步流式视频生成实现无需训练的视频编辑
尽管现有视频编辑方法通常可行，但往往需要大量昂贵的迭代，仍难以交付高质量且令人满意的编辑结果。我们归因于普遍的数据到数据范式，其与现代生成模型的兼容性不如噪声到数据生成。为弥合这一差距，我们从噪声到数据视角重新审视视频编辑，提出基于流式生成的视频编辑（StreamGVE），在保持几步采样同时无缝注入源视频条件。基于预训练的流式生成模型，StreamGVE引入双分支快速采样，结合自注意力桥接与跨注意力锚定/增强，以满足采样与条件需求。我们进一步提出源导向引导以提升目标生成质量，并引入视觉提示策略以增强编辑灵活性与实用性。该方法有效、稳健，可跨不同模型泛化。在多样化的视频编辑任务上大量实验表明，StreamGVE在几步设置下即使时间成本极低，仍持续优于现有方法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency and suboptimal quality of current video editing methods that rely on costly iterative processes, this work proposes StreamGVE, a training-free approach that leverages few-step streaming video generation from noise, rather than the traditional data-to-data paradigm. It employs dual-branch fast sampling with self-attention bridging and cross-attention grounding to integrate source-video conditions without retraining, and introduces source-oriented guidance and visual prompting to enhance output quality and flexibility. Experimental results across diverse editing tasks demonstrate that StreamGVE consistently outperforms existing methods, achieving high-quality edits even with minimal steps and computational cost.
为解决现有视频编辑方法因依赖数据到数据范式而导致的迭代成本高、编辑质量不佳的问题，本文提出StreamGVE框架，基于噪声到数据的流式生成机制实现无需训练的视频编辑。该方法利用预训练流式生成模型，结合双分支快速采样、自注意力保持内部一致性、跨注意力实现源视频条件引导，并引入源导向引导与视觉提示策略以提升编辑质量与灵活性。实验表明，StreamGVE在多种编辑任务中表现优于现有方法，尤其在少量步骤下仍能保持高效与高质，具备良好的泛化能力。
Leveraging LLMs for Grammar Adaptation: A Study on Metamodel-Grammar Co-Evolution
Authors: Weixing Zhang, Bowen Jiang, Rahul Sharma, Regina Hebig, Daniel Strüber
First: 2026-05-20T17:51:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-20T17:51:51+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
In model-driven engineering, metamodel evolution leads to the need to adapt corresponding grammars to maintain consistency, which typically requires tedious manual work. Existing rule-based methods can achieve partial automation but have limitations when handling complex grammar scenarios. This paper proposes a Large Language Model-based approach that automatically applies adaptations to new grammars after evolution by learning grammar adaptations from previous versions. We evaluated this approach on six real-world Xtext domain-specific languages, using four DSLs as a training set to develop prompting strategies, two DSLs as a test set for validation, and conducting a longitudinal case study on QVTo. The evaluation used three Large Language Models (Claude Sonnet 4.5, ChatGPT 5.1, Gemini 3) and measured grammar adaptation quality from three dimensions: grammar rule-level adaptation consistency, output similarity, and metamodel conformance. Results show that on the test set, all three LLMs achieved 100% adaptation consistency and output similarity, while the rule-based approach achieved only 84.21% on DOT and 62.50% on Xcore. In the QVTo longitudinal study, the LLM-based approach successfully reused learned adaptations across all three evolution steps without manual grammar editing, while the rule-based approach required manual adjustments in two of three transitions. However, on large-scale grammars (EAST-ADL, 297 rules), LLMs' adaptation consistency was far below 90%. This study demonstrates the advantages of LLM-based approaches in handling complex grammar scenarios, while revealing their limitations in large-scale grammar adaptation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：利用大语言模型进行语法适应：一种元模型-语法协同演化的研究
在模型驱动工程中，元模型演化需相应调整语法以保持一致性，通常需繁琐的手动工作。现有基于规则的方法虽可部分自动化，但在处理复杂语法场景时存在局限。本文提出一种基于大语言模型的方法，通过学习历史版本的语法适应规则，自动将适应应用于演化后的新型语法。我们在六个真实Xtext领域特定语言上评估该方法，使用四个DSL作为训练集以开发提示策略，两个DSL作为测试集进行验证，并对QVTo进行纵向案例研究。评估采用三种大语言模型（Claude Sonnet 4.5、ChatGPT 5.1、Gemini 3），从语法规则级适应一致性、输出相似性及元模型符合性三个维度衡量语法适应质量。结果表明，在测试集上，三种大语言模型均实现100%的适应一致性和输出相似性，而基于规则的方法在DOT上仅达84.21%，在Xcore上仅62.50%。在QVTo纵向研究中，大语言模型方法成功在三次演化步骤中复用所学适应规则，无需手动修改语法，而基于规则的方法在三次过渡中有两次需手动调整。然而，在大规模语法（EAST-ADL，297条规则）中，大语言模型的适应一致性远低于90%。本研究展示了大语言模型方法在处理复杂语法场景中的优势，也揭示了其在大规模语法适应中的局限性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to automate grammar adaptation during metamodel evolution in model-driven engineering, this study proposes a Large Language Model (LLM)-based method that learns grammar adaptation patterns from historical versions to automatically generate updated grammars. Evaluated across six real-world Xtext DSLs and a longitudinal QVTo case study, the approach leveraged three LLMs (Claude Sonnet 4.5, ChatGPT 5.1, Gemini 3) and achieved 100% adaptation consistency and output similarity on test DSLs, outperforming a rule-based baseline (84.21% on DOT, 62.50% on Xcore). In QVTo, the LLM method reused learned adaptations across all evolution steps without manual edits, while the rule-based method required manual intervention in two transitions. However, on large-scale grammars (EAST-ADL with 297 rules), LLM adaptation consistency dropped significantly below 90%, highlighting scalability limitations.
为解决模型驱动工程中元模型演化后语法需手动适配的问题，本文提出一种基于大语言模型（LLM）的方法，通过学习历史语法版本的适配规则，自动应用于新语法。在六个真实Xtext领域特定语言上评估，三款LLM在测试集上均实现100%的规则一致性与输出相似性，优于仅达84.21%和62.50%的规则方法。在QVTo纵向案例中，LLM方法无需人工干预即可复用适配规则完成三次演化，而规则方法需在两次演化中手动调整。但在大规模语法（EAST-ADL，297条规则）下，LLM一致性显著低于90%，暴露出其在大规模场景下的局限性。
Mem-$π$: Adaptive Memory through Learning When and What to Generate
Authors: Xiaoqiang Wang, Chao Wang, Hadi Nekoei, Christopher Pal, Alexandre Lacoste, Spandana Gella, Bang Liu, Perouz Taslakian
First: 2026-05-20T17:51:05+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-20T17:51:05+00:00
Comments: Work in progress
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We present Mem-$π$, a framework for adaptive memory in large language model (LLM) agents, where useful guidance is generated on demand rather than retrieved from external memory stores. Existing memory-augmented agents typically rely on similarity-based retrieval from episodic memory banks or skill libraries, returning static entries that often misalign with the current context. In contrast, Mem-$π$ uses a dedicated language or vision-language model with its own parameters, separate from the downstream agent, to generate context-specific guidance for complex tasks. Conditioned on the current agent context, the model jointly decides when to produce guidance and what guidance to produce. We train it with a decision-content decoupled reinforcement learning (RL) objective, enabling it to abstain when generation would not help and otherwise produce concise, useful guidance. Across diverse agentic benchmarks spanning web navigation, terminal-based tool use, and text-based embodied interaction, Mem-$π$ consistently outperforms retrieval-based and prior RL-optimized memory baselines, achieving over 30% relative improvement on web navigation tasks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Mem-$π$: 通过学习何时及生成何物实现自适应记忆
我们提出了Mem-$π$，一种面向大语言模型（LLM）代理的自适应记忆框架，其在需要时动态生成有用指导，而非从外部记忆存储中检索。现有增强记忆的代理通常依赖于基于相似度的事件记忆库或技能库检索，返回静态条目，常与当前上下文不匹配。相比之下，Mem-$π$使用独立于下游代理的专用语言或视觉-语言模型，根据当前代理上下文联合决定何时生成指导以及生成何种指导。我们采用决策-内容解耦的强化学习（RL）目标对其进行训练，使其在生成无益时可选择不生成，否则生成简洁且有用的指导。在涵盖网页导航、基于终端的工具使用及基于文本的具身交互等多样化代理基准测试中，Mem-$π$持续优于基于检索和先前强化学习优化的记忆基线，网页导航任务相对提升超过30%。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of static memory retrieval in LLM agents, Mem-$π$ introduces a novel adaptive memory framework that generates context-specific guidance on demand rather than retrieving from fixed memory stores. It employs a separate language or vision-language model to jointly decide when and what guidance to produce, conditioned on the agent’s current context. Trained with a decision-content decoupled reinforcement learning objective, the model abstains when generation is unhelpful and otherwise outputs concise, task-relevant guidance. Experimental results across web navigation, terminal tool use, and text-based embodied interaction benchmarks show consistent performance gains over retrieval-based and prior RL-optimized memory methods, with over 30% relative improvement on web navigation tasks.
为了解决现有LLM代理中基于相似度检索的静态记忆方法与当前上下文不匹配的问题，Mem-$π$提出了一种按需生成上下文相关指导的自适应记忆框架。该框架使用一个独立的语言或视觉-语言模型，通过决策与内容解耦的强化学习目标训练，联合决定何时以及生成何种指导，并在无帮助时选择不生成。在涵盖网页导航、终端工具使用和文本式具身交互的多个基准测试中，Mem-$π$持续优于检索式和先前强化学习优化的记忆基线方法，在网页导航任务上实现超过30%的相对性能提升。
Understanding and Improving Communication Performance in Multi-node LLM Inference
Authors: Prajwal Singhania, Siddharth Singh, Lannie Dalton Hough, Akarsh Srivastava, Harshitha Menon, Charles Fredrick Jekel, Abhinav Bhatele
First: 2025-11-12T18:59:26+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-20T17:50:51+00:00
Comments: 17 Figures, To Appear in Proceedings of ACM Conference on AI and Agentic Systems 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
As large language models (LLMs) continue to grow in size, distributed inference has become increasingly important. Model-parallel strategies must now efficiently scale not only across multiple GPUs but also across multiple nodes. In this work, we present a detailed performance study of multi-node distributed inference using LLMs on GPU-based supercomputers. We conduct experiments with several state-of-the-art inference engines alongside YALIS, a research-oriented prototype engine designed for controlled experimentation. We analyze the strong-scaling behavior of different model-parallel schemes and identify key bottlenecks. Because all-reduce operations are a common performance bottleneck, we develop NVRAR, a hierarchical all-reduce algorithm based on recursive doubling with NVSHMEM. NVRAR achieves up to 1.9$\times$-3.6$\times$ lower latency than NCCL for message sizes between 128 KB and 2 MB on HPE Slingshot and InfiniBand interconnects. Integrated into YALIS, NVRAR achieves up to a 1.72$\times$ reduction in end-to-end batch latency for the Llama 3.1 405B model in multi-node decode-heavy workloads using tensor parallelism.
中文标题/摘要
标题：多节点LLM推理中通信性能的理解与优化
随着大语言模型（LLM）规模持续扩大，分布式推理变得愈发重要。模型并行策略必须不仅在多个GPU间高效扩展，还需在多个节点间扩展。本文对基于GPU的超级计算机上使用LLM进行多节点分布式推理的性能进行了详细研究。我们结合多个最先进的推理引擎与YALIS（一个面向研究的原型引擎，专为可控实验设计）开展实验，分析不同模型并行方案的强扩展行为并识别关键瓶颈。由于all-reduce操作是常见性能瓶颈，我们开发了基于递归加倍与NVSHMEM的分层all-reduce算法NVRAR。在HPE Slingshot和InfiniBand互连环境下，NVRAR在128 KB至2 MB的消息大小范围内，相比NCCL延迟降低1.9至3.6倍。集成至YALIS后，NVRAR在多节点解码密集型工作负载中，使用张量并行对Llama 3.1 405B模型的端到端批处理延迟降低达1.72倍。

As large language models grow, efficient multi-node distributed inference becomes critical, prompting this study to analyze scaling behavior and bottlenecks in model-parallel setups. The researchers evaluate multiple inference engines, including a custom prototype YALIS, and identify all-reduce operations as a key performance limiter. They introduce NVRAR, a hierarchical all-reduce algorithm leveraging NVSHMEM and recursive doubling, which reduces latency by 1.9x to 3.6x compared to NCCL for 128 KB to 2 MB messages on HPE Slingshot and InfiniBand.
