随着大语言模型规模扩大，多节点分布式推理效率成为关键，本研究旨在分析模型并行方案的扩展性能与瓶颈。作者对比多个推理引擎（含自研原型YALIS），发现All-Reduce操作是主要性能瓶颈，于是提出NVRAR算法，基于NVSHMEM和递归加倍实现分层All-Reduce，相较NCCL在128KB至2MB消息量下延迟降低1.9至3.6倍。集成至YALIS后，该算法在Llama 3.1 405B模型的多节点解码密集型任务中，端到端批处理延迟最高降低1.72倍。
Building Deep Graph Predictors with Graph Imitation Learning
Authors: André Eberhard, Gerhard Neumann, Pascal Friederich
First: 2026-01-21T16:07:17+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-20T17:50:47+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Recent years have seen substantial progress in neural generation of text, images, and audio, supported by mature training pipelines and large-scale optimization. For graphs, however, comparable progress has been more limited. We attribute this gap to graph-specific optimization and representation challenges that undermine the effectiveness of training neural networks with backpropagation and gradient descent. We argue that representing graphs on a fixed-size Euclidean grid, as is common in recently proposed models for supervised graph prediction, may not be the optimal choice in these settings. To support our view, we provide an analysis of neural graph generation methods and identify theoretical challenges that lead to pitfalls when training neural networks to produce graphs as their output. Motivated by this analysis, we introduce \textbf{GRA}ph~\textbf{I}mitation~\textbf{L}earning~(GRAIL), a framework for training neural networks in supervised settings in which the supervision signal is a graph. GRAIL generates graphs sequentially through a Markov decision process over embeddings of partial graphs, thereby avoiding the representation issues associated with fixed-size grid graph representations. We empirically show that GRAIL achieves competitive results on supervised graph prediction across a comprehensive suite of 18 benchmarks, matching or surpassing state-of-the-art methods in several settings.
中文标题/摘要
标题：使用图模仿学习构建深度图预测器
近年来，神经网络在文本、图像和音频生成方面取得了显著进展，得益于成熟的训练流程和大规模优化。然而，对于图结构，类似进展则较为有限。我们归因于图特有的优化与表示挑战，这些挑战削弱了使用反向传播和梯度下降训练神经网络的有效性。我们认为，在近期提出的监督图预测模型中，将图表示为固定大小的欧几里得网格可能并非最优选择。为支持这一观点，我们分析了神经图生成方法，并识别出导致训练神经网络生成图输出时陷入陷阱的理论挑战。受此分析启发，我们提出了\textbf{GRA}ph~\textbf{I}mitation~\textbf{L}earning~(GRAIL)，一种在监督设置下训练神经网络的框架，其监督信号为图结构。GRAIL 通过在部分图嵌入上的马尔可夫决策过程顺序生成图，从而避免了固定大小网格图表示所引发的表示问题。实验证明，GRAIL 在18个基准测试中均取得具有竞争力的结果，在多个场景中达到或超越当前最先进方法的性能。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of fixed-size grid representations in graph generation that hinder neural network training via backpropagation, the paper proposes GRAIL, a framework that generates graphs sequentially through a Markov decision process over partial graph embeddings, thereby avoiding representation pitfalls. Experimental results across 18 benchmarks demonstrate that GRAIL achieves competitive or superior performance compared to state-of-the-art methods in supervised graph prediction tasks.
鉴于固定大小网格表示在图生成中阻碍了神经网络通过反向传播训练，本文提出GRAIL框架，通过在部分图嵌入上进行马尔可夫决策过程来顺序生成图，从而规避表示缺陷。在18个基准测试中，实验表明GRAIL在监督图预测任务中达到与当前最优方法相当甚至更优的性能。
A Machine Learning Framework for Weighted Least Squares GNSS Positioning based on Activation Functions
Authors: Pin-Hsun Lee, Harry Leib
First: 2026-05-20T17:50:14+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-20T17:50:14+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Global Navigation Satellite Systems (GNSS) are widely used to provide position, velocity, and timing (PVT) information for various applications, including transportation, location-based communication services, and intelligent agriculture. In urban canyons, high-rise buildings and narrow streets can cause signal obstruction, non-line-of-sight (NLOS) reception, and multipath effects that introduce errors in GNSS pseudorange measurements. Although multi-constellations GNSS effectively increase the number of available satellites, the inclusion of degraded signals can lead to severe positioning errors. This study proposes a machine learning framework for the weighted least squares (WLS) algorithm incorporating activation functions to enhance positioning accuracy. Several signal quality indicators are employed as training features for ensemble learning algorithms to identify poor quality signals by providing quality scores. Then, activation functions are employed to transform the machine learning predicted scores to appropriate weights for WLS positioning. To evaluate the performance of our approach, experiments are conducted using real-world datasets from Hong Kong and Tokyo urban areas. Comparative analysis of activation functions reveals that sigmoid functions consistently yield the greatest improvements with different machine learning algorithms and GNSS constellation configurations. The proposed algorithm demonstrates substantial reductions in positioning errors for both single- and multiconstellation scenarios. Furthermore, our results indicate that the proposed algorithm exhibits strong geographical transferability. The proposed algorithm maintains comparable level of performance when trained on data from other regions with similar levels of urbanization.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于激活函数的加权最小二乘GNSS定位机器学习框架
全球导航卫星系统（GNSS）广泛用于为各种应用提供位置、速度和时间（PVT）信息，包括交通运输、基于位置的通信服务和智能农业。在城市峡谷中，高层建筑和狭窄街道会导致信号遮挡、非视距（NLOS）接收和多路径效应，从而引入GNSS伪距测量误差。尽管多星座GNSS有效增加了可用卫星数量，但包含劣质信号可能导致严重定位误差。本研究提出一种结合激活函数的加权最小二乘（WLS）算法机器学习框架，以提高定位精度。多个信号质量指标被用作训练特征，用于集成学习算法识别劣质信号并提供质量评分。随后，激活函数将机器学习预测评分转换为适用于WLS定位的适当权重。为评估本方法性能，使用香港和东京城市区域的真实数据集进行实验。激活函数的比较分析表明，Sigmoid函数在不同机器学习算法和GNSS星座配置下始终带来最大改进。所提算法在单星座和多星座场景下均显著降低定位误差。此外，结果表明该算法具有较强的地理迁移能力，在训练数据来自其他城市化水平相似区域时，仍能保持相近性能。
Summary / 总结
To address positioning inaccuracies in urban canyons caused by signal obstructions and multipath effects, this study proposes a machine learning framework that enhances the weighted least squares (WLS) GNSS positioning algorithm by integrating activation functions. The method uses signal quality indicators as input features to train ensemble learning models that predict signal quality scores, which are then transformed via activation functions into adaptive weights for WLS. Experiments using real-world datasets from Hong Kong and Tokyo show that sigmoid activation functions consistently yield the best performance across different machine learning models and GNSS constellations, significantly reducing positioning errors in both single- and multi-constellation scenarios. Additionally, the algorithm demonstrates strong geographical transferability, maintaining performance when trained on data from other similarly urbanized regions.
为解决城市峡谷中因信号衰减导致的定位误差，本研究提出一种结合激活函数的机器学习框架，用于增强加权最小二乘（WLS）GNSS定位算法。该方法利用信号质量指标作为输入特征，训练集成学习模型预测信号可靠性评分，再通过激活函数将评分转化为WLS权重。在港、东京实地数据实验中，发现Sigmoid激活函数在不同机器学习算法和GNSS星座配置下均能显著提升定位精度，有效降低单/多星座场景下的定位误差。此外，该方法具备良好的地理迁移能力，在相似城市化水平区域的数据上仍能保持稳定性能。
HITL-D: Human In The Loop Diffusion Assisted Shared Control
Authors: Riley Zilka, Sergey Khlynovskiy, Allie Wang, Martin Jagersand
Venue: ICRA 2026
First: 2026-05-20T17:49:56+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-20T17:49:56+00:00
Comments: Accepted for presentation at ICRA 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Autonomous manipulation systems have achieved remarkable capabilities, yet the integration of human expertise with diffusion-based policies in shared control remains relatively unexplored. In this paper, we propose Human-In-The-Loop Diffusion (HITL-D), a shared control framework that enhances user performance in multi-step, insertion, and fine manipulation tasks. HITL-D leverages a novel combination of diffusion-based policies and human control to provide autonomous end effector orientation updates conditioned on a scene point cloud and the Cartesian position of the end effector. This approach reduces the number of joystick control axes required, thereby lowering mental workload. In a multi-task user study with 12 participants, HITL-D reduced average task completion times by 40%, decreased perceived workload by 37%, and improved Likert-scale ratings for independence, intuitiveness, and confidence compared to traditional teleoperation methods. These results demonstrate that HITL-D effectively integrates human expertise with autonomous assistance, improving both objective and subjective aspects of teleoperation.
中文标题/摘要

为提升人机协作中人类经验与自主系统的融合，本文提出HITL-D框架，结合扩散模型与人类操作，辅助完成多步插入与精细操作任务。该方法根据场景点云与末端执行器的笛卡尔位置，自动生成末端姿态更新，减少所需摇杆轴数，降低认知负荷。在12名参与者实验中，HITL-D使任务平均完成时间缩短40%，感知负荷降低37%，且在独立性、直观性与信心等主观评分上优于传统遥操作方法。
Toward User Comprehension Supports for LLM Agent Skill Specifications
Authors: Zikai Alex Wen
First: 2026-05-19T04:50:42+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-20T17:49:17+00:00
Comments: To appear at ACM CAIS Workshop Agent Skill 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Users often interpret and select agent skills through their SKILL markdown specifications. To protect users, existing audits mainly focus on malicious or unsafe skills. We study the complementary question of whether specifications help users form bounded expectations about what a skill consumes, produces, and covers. Across 878 cybersecurity skills, we used rule-based coding to measure textual cues for four comprehension anchors, namely operational basis, output contract, boundary disclosure, and example capability demonstration. Cues for operational basis were common, but only 19.0% of specifications exhibited cues for an example task, sample, or expected outcome, and only 2.3% exhibited cues for all four anchors. We further examined a small DNS/C2 telemetry subset (n$=$6) to illustrate why missing examples may matter. Examples appeared to make first local checks easier to construct, while no-example skills typically required helper code inspection to recover command arguments or output fields. We argue that agent-skill evaluation should treat specifications as user-facing capability disclosures, not merely as containers for executable instructions.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向用户理解支持的大型语言模型代理技能规范研究
用户常通过技能的SKILL标记规范解读并选择代理技能。为保护用户，现有审计主要关注恶意或不安全技能。我们研究了互补问题：规范是否有助于用户形成关于技能消耗、产出和覆盖范围的有界预期。通过对878个网络安全技能进行基于规则的编码，我们测量了四个理解锚点（操作基础、输出契约、边界披露、示例能力演示）的文本线索。操作基础线索常见，但仅有19.0%的规范包含示例任务、样本或预期结果线索，仅2.3%包含全部四个锚点线索。我们进一步以小规模DNS/C2遥测子集（n=6）为例，说明缺失示例可能带来的影响：示例有助于构建更易操作的本地检查，而无示例技能通常需通过辅助代码检查才能恢复命令参数或输出字段。我们认为，代理技能评估应将规范视为面向用户的能力建议，而不仅是可执行指令的容器。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to improve user understanding of LLM agent skills, this study investigates whether skill specifications help users form bounded expectations about what skills consume, produce, and cover. Using rule-based coding across 878 cybersecurity skills, the authors analyze textual cues for four comprehension anchors: operational basis, output contract, boundary disclosure, and example capability demonstration. Results show that while operational basis cues are common, only 19.0% of specifications include example task cues and just 2.3% cover all four anchors. A small DNS/C2 telemetry subset (n=6) further demonstrates that missing examples hinder users’ ability to perform local checks, often forcing them to inspect helper code to infer command arguments or output fields. The authors conclude that specifications should be treated as user-facing capability disclosures, not just containers for executable code.
为提升用户对LLM代理技能的理解，本研究探讨技能说明是否有助于用户形成关于技能输入、输出和覆盖范围的明确预期。通过对878个网络安全技能进行基于规则的文本编码，分析了四个理解锚点：操作基础、输出契约、边界披露和示例能力演示。结果显示，操作基础提示较常见，但仅有19.0%的说明包含示例任务或预期结果提示，仅有2.3%覆盖全部四个锚点。在DNS/C2遥测的小样本中发现，缺少示例会使本地检查更困难，通常需依赖辅助代码才能恢复命令参数或输出字段，表明技能说明应被视为面向用户的能力建议，而非仅作为可执行指令的容器。
Sustainability Is Not Linear: Quantifying Performance, Energy, and Privacy Trade-offs in On-Device Intelligence
Authors: Eziyo Ehsani, Luca Giamattei, Ivano Malavolta, Roberto Pietrantuono
First: 2026-03-27T17:00:25+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-20T17:48:34+00:00
Comments: Under review at Empirical Software Engineering (EMSE)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The migration of Large Language Models (LLMs) from cloud clusters to edge devices promises enhanced privacy and offline accessibility, but this transition encounters a harsh reality: the physical constraints of mobile batteries, thermal limits, and, most importantly, memory constraints. To navigate this landscape, we constructed a replicable and reproducible experimental pipeline to profile the complex interplay between energy consumption, latency, and quality of LLMs on mobile devices. We harness this pipeline to conduct an empirical case study on a flagship Android device, capturing granular metrics across eight LLMs ranging from 0.5B to 9B parameters without requiring root access, ensuring our findings reflect realistic user conditions. The findings highlight the trade-offs between generation quality, performance, power and resource consumption, revealing which LLMs offer the best balance across metrics and under different conditions. Besides, we uncovered a counter-intuitive quantization energy paradox: while modern importance-aware quantization successfully reduces memory footprints to fit larger models into RAM, we found it yields negligible energy savings compared to standard mixed-precision methods. This proves that for battery life, the architecture of the model, not its quantization scheme, is the decisive factor. We further identified that Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures defy the standard size-energy trend, offering the storage capacity of a 7B model while maintaining the lower energy profile of a 1B to 2B model. Finally, an analysis of these multi-objective trade-offs reveals a pragmatic sweet spot of mid-sized models, such as Qwen2.5-3B, that effectively balance response quality with sustainable energy consumption.
中文标题/摘要
标题：可持续性并非线性：移动设备智能中的性能、能耗与隐私权衡量化
将大型语言模型（LLM）从云端集群迁移到边缘设备虽可提升隐私与离线访问能力，但实际面临移动电池物理限制、热限制及最重要的是内存限制。为应对这一挑战，我们构建了可复现的实验流程，以剖析LLM在移动设备上能耗、延迟与质量间的复杂交互关系。我们以旗舰Android设备为对象，无需root权限，对8个参数规模从0.5B至9B的LLM进行实证研究，确保结果贴近真实用户环境。研究揭示了生成质量、性能、功耗与资源消耗间的权衡，明确了不同条件下各模型的综合表现最优者。此外，我们发现了一个反直觉的量化能耗悖论：尽管现代重要性感知量化能有效压缩内存以容纳更大模型，但其能耗节省远不及标准混合精度方法。这证明对电池续航而言，模型架构而非量化方案才是决定性因素。我们进一步发现，混合专家（MoE）架构突破了传统规模-能耗趋势，以7B模型的存储容量，保持1B至2B模型的低能耗特性。最终，多目标权衡分析揭示了如Qwen2.5-3B等中等规模模型的实用平衡点，其在响应质量与可持续能耗间取得最佳折衷。
Summary / 总结
Driven by the need to understand trade-offs in deploying Large Language Models (LLMs) on mobile devices, this study builds a reproducible experimental pipeline to measure energy, latency, and quality across eight LLMs ranging from 0.5B to 9B parameters on a standard Android device without root access. The results reveal that larger models do not necessarily consume more energy, as Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures can deliver 7B-model capacity with 1B–2B-model energy efficiency. Surprisingly, importance-aware quantization, while reducing memory usage, offers minimal energy savings compared to standard mixed-precision methods, indicating model architecture—not quantization—is the key to battery efficiency. The analysis identifies Qwen2.5-3B as a pragmatic sweet spot, balancing response quality and sustainable energy consumption across diverse conditions.
为理解在移动设备上部署大语言模型（LLM）时的性能、能耗与隐私权衡，本研究构建了可复现的实验流程，在无root权限的安卓设备上对8个参数规模从0.5B到9B的模型进行能耗、延迟与质量测量。结果表明，模型架构而非量化方案决定能效，重要性感知量化虽减小内存占用但节能效果微弱。MoE架构打破传统规模-能耗规律，以7B模型存储量实现1B–2B模型的能耗水平。多目标分析发现，如Qwen2.5-3B等中等规模模型在质量与可持续能耗间达到最佳平衡。
Mind the Sim-to-Real Gap & Think Like a Scientist
Authors: Harsh Parikh, Gabriel Levin-Konigsberg, Dominique Perrault-Joncas, Alexander Volfovsky
First: 2026-05-20T17:48:14+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-20T17:48:14+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Suppose a planner has a pre-trained simulator of a sequential decision problem and the option to run real experiments in the field. The simulator is cheap to query but inherits confounding and drift from its calibration data. Experimentation is unbiased but consumes one real unit per trial. We study when, and how, the planner should supplement the simulator with experiments. We give three results. First, an extended simulation lemma decomposes the simulator's value error into a calibration--deployment shift that randomization can identify and a parametric residual that no further interaction can reduce. Second, the value gap between the simulator-optimal policy and the optimum splits into a local component, on states the deployed policy already visits, and a reachability component, on states it does not. The reachability component stays bounded away from zero at any horizon under purely passive learning. Third, we propose Fisher-SEP, a simulation-aided experimental policy (SEP) that minimizes the posterior predictive variance of a target policy's value, with reward-only and transition-only specializations. Two case studies illustrate the regimes. In a vending-machine supply chain, front-loaded experimentation overtakes posterior updating once the horizon is long enough to amortize the pilot. In an HIV mobile-testing example with a corridor that separates a well-surveilled region from a poorly-surveilled one, only designed exploration reaches the poorly-surveilled region.
中文标题/摘要
标题：注意仿真到现实的差距并像科学家一样思考
假设一个规划者拥有一个预训练的序列决策问题模拟器，并且可以选择在实地运行真实实验。模拟器查询成本低廉，但继承了校准数据中的混杂因素和漂移。实验无偏但每次试验消耗一个真实单位。我们研究规划者应在何时以及如何补充模拟器以进行实验。我们得出三个结果：第一，扩展的模拟引理将模拟器的价值误差分解为可通过随机化识别的校准-部署偏移，以及无法通过进一步交互减少的参数残差；第二，模拟器最优策略与最优策略之间的价值差距分解为局部成分（部署策略已访问的状态）和可达性成分（部署策略未访问的状态），在纯被动学习下，可达性成分在任何时间跨度内都保持远离零；第三，我们提出Fisher-SEP，一种借助模拟的实验性策略（SEP），旨在最小化目标策略价值的后验预测方差，并包含仅奖励和仅转移的特化版本。两个案例研究说明了不同情形：在自动售货机供应链中，当时间跨度足够长以摊销试点成本后，前期实验将超越后验更新；在HIV移动检测案例中，存在一个走廊将监控良好的区域与监控较差的区域分隔开，只有设计好的探索才能到达监控较差的区域。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of bridging the simulation-to-reality gap in sequential decision-making, the paper investigates how to optimally combine a pre-trained simulator with real-world experiments. It introduces an extended simulation lemma that decomposes value error into identifiable calibration-deployment shifts and irreducible parametric residuals. The study further shows that the gap between simulator-optimal and true optimal policies splits into local and reachability components, with the latter remaining non-negligible under passive learning. The authors propose Fisher-SEP, a policy that minimizes posterior predictive variance of target policy value, with reward-only and transition-only variants. Experimental case studies reveal that in a vending-machine supply chain, early experimentation becomes more efficient than posterior updating for long horizons, while in an HIV testing scenario, only targeted exploration can access underserved regions.
为解决仿真与现实之间的差距问题，本文研究如何最优结合预训练仿真器与真实实验。提出扩展仿真引理，将价值误差分解为可识别的校准-部署偏移与不可进一步降低的参数残差。研究进一步表明，仿真最优策略与真实最优策略之间的性能差距可分为局部与可达性两部分，后者在被动学习下始终非零。作者提出Fisher-SEP策略，通过最小化目标策略价值的后验预测方差，结合两个案例研究发现：在长时域下，实验往往优于仿真；在空间分割区域中，仅设计探索能触及未覆盖区域。
Mitigating Label Bias with Interpretable Rubric Embeddings
Authors: Calvin Isley, Johann D. Gaebler, Sharad Goel
First: 2026-05-20T17:46:08+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-20T17:46:08+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Statistical decision algorithms are increasingly deployed in domains where ground-truth labels are hard to obtain, such as hiring, university admissions, and content moderation. In these settings, models are typically trained on historical human evaluations -- for example, using past hiring decisions as a proxy for true applicant quality. However, if past evaluations unjustly favor certain groups, models trained on these labels may inherit those biases. To address this problem, we propose basing predictions on rubric embeddings, a representation framework that replaces standard black-box embeddings with features derived from expert-defined criteria that align with the underlying construct of interest. By anchoring predictions to semantically meaningful dimensions, this approach guards against biased proxy signals. We provide both theoretical and empirical evidence that rubric embeddings mitigate label bias under plausible conditions. Empirically, we evaluate our method on a novel dataset of applications to a large master's program. We find that models trained on rubric embeddings reduce group disparities while improving measures of cohort quality. Our results suggest that basing predictions on interpretable, domain-grounded representations offers a practical approach to learning in the presence of biased labels.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过可解释的评分标准嵌入缓解标签偏差
统计决策算法在招聘、大学录取和内容审核等获取真实标签困难的领域中日益被部署。在这些场景中，模型通常基于历史人工评估进行训练，例如用过去的招聘决策作为申请人真实质量的代理。然而，如果历史评估不公正地偏向某些群体，基于这些标签训练的模型可能继承这些偏见。为解决此问题，我们提出基于评分标准嵌入进行预测，这是一种将标准黑箱嵌入替换为与目标构念对齐的专家定义标准特征的表示框架。通过将预测锚定在语义有意义的维度上，该方法可防止偏倚的代理信号。我们提供了理论和实证证据，表明在合理条件下，评分标准嵌入可缓解标签偏差。实证上，我们在一个大型硕士项目申请的新型数据集上评估了该方法，发现基于评分标准嵌入训练的模型在减少群体差异的同时提升了群体质量指标。我们的结果表明，基于可解释且领域相关的表示进行预测，是应对偏倚标签下学习的一种实用方法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the risk that models trained on biased historical labels perpetuate unfair outcomes in high-stakes domains like hiring and admissions, this work proposes using rubric embeddings—interpretable feature representations derived from expert-defined evaluation criteria—to mitigate label bias. Instead of relying on black-box embeddings from proxy labels, predictions are anchored to semantically meaningful dimensions aligned with the underlying construct, reducing susceptibility to biased signals. Empirical evaluation on a novel master’s program application dataset shows that models using rubric embeddings reduce group disparities while simultaneously improving cohort quality metrics, demonstrating the method’s practical effectiveness in biased label settings.
为应对模型在招聘、招生等高风险领域因依赖有偏历史标签而延续不公平结果的问题，该研究提出使用基于专家制定标准的可解释评分标准嵌入（rubric embeddings）来引导预测。该方法通过将预测锚定在与目标构念对齐的语义维度上，替代传统黑盒嵌入，从而降低对有偏代理标签的敏感性。在一项新的硕士项目申请数据集上的实证评估表明，使用评分标准嵌入的模型能显著减少群体差异，同时提升群体质量指标，验证了其在缓解偏见方面的理论与实践有效性。
ProtoPathway: Biologically Structured Prototype-Pathway Fusion for Multimodal Cancer Survival Prediction
Authors: Amaya Gallagher-Syed, Costantino Pitzalis, Myles J. Lewis, Michael R. Barnes, Gregory Slabaugh
First: 2026-05-20T17:43:43+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-20T17:43:43+00:00
Comments: Currently under peer review
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
We introduce ProtoPathway, an interpretable-by-design multimodal framework for cancer survival prediction that unifies whole slide imaging and transcriptomics through encoders producing biologically grounded representations on both sides of the fusion. On the histopathology side, $K$ learnable morphological prototypes, trained end-to-end with the survival objective, serve as the slide representation itself: patches flow into prototype tokens via soft assignment, compressing variable-length patch sets into fixed task-adaptive tokens. On the genomic side, a bipartite graph neural network encodes gene expression within the Reactome pathway hierarchy, producing pathway embeddings that reflect both constituent genes and their broader biological context through bidirectional message passing over a shared gene--pathway graph. Cross-modal attention then operates over a compact prototype $\times$ pathway matrix in which prototypes query pathways, modeling the biological direction in which molecular programs give rise to tissue morphology. Because both axes carry stable task-learned identity, the attention matrix is itself an interpretability output, yielding native inference-time attribution across the full biological hierarchy, from genes through pathways and prototypes to spatial tissue maps. We evaluate on five TCGA cancer cohorts, demonstrating competitive or superior survival prediction with substantially improved biological interpretability and reduced computational cost, with interpretability claims validated through fold-stratified rank-based population-level analysis. Our source code, model weights, and Reactome pathways, together with a unified codebase reimplementing all multimodal survival baselines under identical preprocessing and evaluation, are available at: https://github.com/AmayaGS/ProtoPathway.

On the genomic side, a bipartite graph neural network encodes gene expression within the Reactome pathway hierarchy, generating pathway embeddings through bidirectional message passing. Cross-modal attention operates over a prototype-pathway matrix, enabling biological interpretation by mapping molecular programs to tissue morphology. Evaluated across five TCGA cohorts, the model achieves competitive or superior survival prediction with enhanced interpretability and reduced computational cost, validated via fold-stratified rank-based analysis.
为实现可解释且高效的多模态癌症生存预测，ProtoPathway通过生物基础编码器融合组织病理学与转录组数据：病理学通过K个可学习形态原型压缩图像块为任务自适应令牌，转录组则通过双向消息传递的二分图神经网络编码基因-通路关系。跨模态注意力在原型-通路矩阵上操作，将分子程序映射至组织形态，实现生物可解释性。在五个TCGA队列上评估，该模型在生存预测上表现竞争或更优，同时提升可解释性并降低计算成本，通过分层排名分析验证其可解释性。
Quality and Security Signals in AI-Generated Python Refactoring Pull Requests
Authors: Mohamed Almukhtar, Anwar Ghammam, Hua Ming
First: 2026-05-20T17:43:36+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-20T17:43:36+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
As AI agents increasingly contribute to code development and maintenance, there is still limited empirical evidence on the quality and risk characteristics of their changes in real-world projects, particularly for refactoring-oriented contributions. It remains unclear how agent-authored refactoring edits affect maintainability, code quality, and security once merged into GitHub repositories. To address this gap, we conduct an empirical study of Python refactoring pull requests (PRs) from the AIDev dataset. We analyze agentic refactoring PRs using PyQu, an ML-based quality assessment tool for Python, to quantify changes across five quality attributes, and we complement PyQu with domain-independent static analysis (Pylint and Bandit) to measure code quality and security issues before and after each change. Our results show that, on average, agentic commits improve a quality attribute in 22.5% of the studied changes, with usability improving most frequently (36.5%). At the same time, 24.17% of modified files introduce new Pylint issues predominantly convention level violations such as long lines-while 4.7% introduce new Bandit findings. From the observed diffs, we derive a taxonomy of 24 recurring change operations and map them to the lint and security findings they most commonly affect. Despite these mixed outcomes, developer acceptance is high: 73.5% of the analyzed PRs are merged, including cases that introduce new lint or security findings, often alongside the removal of existing issues. Overall, these findings highlight both the promise and current limitations of agentic refactoring, and motivate stronger tool-in-the-loop quality and security gating for AI-driven development workflows.

Approximation Theory for Neural Networks: Old and New
Authors: Soumendu Sundar Mukherjee, Himasish Talukdar
First: 2026-05-20T17:42:34+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-20T17:42:34+00:00
Comments: 31 pages, 4 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Universal approximation theorems provide a mathematical explanation for the expressive power of neural networks. They assert that, under mild conditions on the activation function, feedforward neural networks are dense in broad function classes, such as continuous functions on compact subsets of $\mathbb{R}^d$, $L^p$ spaces, or Sobolev spaces. Over the past four decades, these qualitative universality results have evolved into a rich quantitative theory addressing approximation rates, parameter efficiency, and the role of architectural features such as depth and width. This survey presents several glimpses into this theory. We review classical density results for single-hidden-layer networks, as well as quantitative bounds that relate approximation error to network size and smoothness assumptions on target functions. Particular emphasis is placed on depth--width trade-offs and on results demonstrating that deeper architectures can achieve superior parameter efficiency for structured function classes. In addition to standard feedforward neural networks, we also review recent developments on Kolmogorov--Arnold Networks (KANs), which offer an alternative architectural paradigm and whose approximation-theoretic properties have begun to attract significant theoretical attention.
中文标题/摘要
标题：神经网络的逼近理论：经典与新进展
通用逼近定理为神经网络的表达能力提供了数学解释。它们断言，在激活函数满足温和条件下，前馈神经网络在广泛函数类中稠密，例如实数空间 $\mathbb{R}^d$ 的紧子集上的连续函数、$L^p$ 空间或Sobolev空间。过去四十年间，这些定性通用性结果已发展为涵盖逼近速率、参数效率及深度与宽度等架构特征作用的丰富定量理论。本文综述了该理论的若干方面：回顾单隐层网络的经典稠密性结果，以及将逼近误差与网络规模和目标函数光滑性假设关联的定量界。特别强调深度-宽度权衡，以及证明更深架构在结构化函数类中可实现更优参数效率的结果。除标准前馈神经网络外，本文还综述了近期Kolmogorov-Arnold网络（KANs）的发展，该架构范式及其逼近理论性质正吸引大量理论关注。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to understand the mathematical foundations of neural network expressivity, this survey synthesizes classical and modern approximation theory for neural networks. It reviews how universal approximation theorems establish that feedforward networks can approximate broad function classes under mild activation assumptions, and then advances to quantitative results measuring approximation error in terms of network size and function smoothness. The paper highlights depth-width trade-offs and demonstrates that deeper architectures often yield better parameter efficiency for structured functions. It also introduces Kolmogorov--Arnold Networks (KANs) as a novel architectural framework whose approximation properties are emerging as a focus of theoretical research.
为理解神经网络表达能力的数学基础，本文综述了神经网络的古典与现代逼近理论。文章探讨了在温和激活函数条件下，通用逼近定理如何演变为定量框架，分析逼近误差、参数效率及架构权衡。重点讨论了深度与宽度的权衡关系，并证明深度网络在结构化函数类中通常具有更优的参数效率。此外，文章还介绍了近期在Kolmogorov-Arnold网络（KANs）上的理论进展，该架构提供了新的逼近理论研究方向。
CODA: Rewriting Transformer Blocks as GEMM-Epilogue Programs
Authors: Han Guo, Jack Zhang, Arjun Menon, Driss Guessous, Vijay Thakkar, Yoon Kim, Tri Dao
First: 2026-05-19T02:30:43+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-20T17:38:24+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Transformer training systems are built around dense linear algebra, yet a nontrivial fraction of end-to-end time is spent on surrounding memory-bound operators. Normalization, activations, residual updates, reductions, and related computations repeatedly move large intermediate tensors through global memory while performing little arithmetic, making data movement an increasingly important bottleneck in otherwise highly optimized training stacks. We introduce CODA, a GPU kernel abstraction that expresses these computations as GEMM-plus-epilogue programs. CODA is based on the observation that many Transformer operators exposed as separate framework kernels can be algebraically reparameterized to execute while a GEMM output tile remains on chip, before it is written to memory. The abstraction fixes the GEMM mainloop and exposes a small set of composable epilogue primitives for scaling, reductions, pairwise transformations, and accumulation. This constrained interface preserves the performance structure of expert-written GEMMs while remaining expressive enough to cover nearly all non-attention computation in the forward and backward pass of a standard Transformer block. Across representative Transformer workloads, both human- and LLM-authored CODA kernels achieve high performance, suggesting that GEMM-plus-epilogue programming offers a practical path toward combining framework-level productivity with hardware-level efficiency.
中文标题/摘要
标题：CODA：将Transformer模块重写为GEMM后处理程序
Transformer训练系统围绕稠密线性代数构建，但端到端时间中有相当一部分用于内存受限的操作。归一化、激活、残差更新、归约及相关计算反复将大型中间张量在全局内存中移动，而实际算术运算较少，使得数据移动成为高度优化训练栈中日益重要的瓶颈。我们引入CODA，一种GPU内核抽象，将这些计算表达为GEMM加后处理程序。CODA基于如下观察：许多作为独立框架内核暴露的Transformer操作符，可通过代数重参数化，在GEMM输出块写入内存前于芯片上执行。该抽象固定GEMM主循环，暴露一组可组合的后处理原语，用于缩放、归约、成对变换和累加。该受限接口保留专家编写GEMM的性能结构，同时表达能力足以覆盖标准Transformer模块前向和反向传播中几乎所有非注意力计算。在代表性Transformer工作负载中，人工和大语言模型生成的CODA内核均实现高性能，表明GEMM加后处理编程提供了一条兼顾框架级生产力与硬件级效率的实用路径。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of memory-bound operations in Transformer training, which consume significant time despite optimized arithmetic kernels, the authors propose CODA, a GPU kernel abstraction that rewrites Transformer block computations as GEMM-plus-epilogue programs. By algebraically reparameterizing operations like normalization, activation, and residual updates to execute while GEMM output tiles remain on chip, CODA minimizes redundant memory transfers. The method exposes a small set of composable epilogue primitives that operate on GEMM outputs without altering the main GEMM loop, preserving performance while enabling coverage of nearly all non-attention computations. Experimental results across representative Transformer workloads show that both human- and LLM-authored CODA kernels achieve high performance, demonstrating that this abstraction effectively bridges framework productivity and hardware efficiency.
为了解决Transformer训练中内存密集型操作消耗大量时间的问题，作者提出了CODA：一种将Transformer块计算重写为GEMM加后处理程序的GPU内核抽象方法。通过将归一化、残差更新和归约等操作代数重参数化，使其在GEMM输出块驻留芯片期间执行，从而减少不必要的内存传输。该方法固定GEMM主循环，仅暴露一组可组合的后处理原语，用于缩放、归约、成对变换和累加，既保留了专家编写GEMM的高性能结构，又足以覆盖标准Transformer块中几乎所有非注意力计算。实验表明，在代表性Transformer负载下，人工和大模型生成的CODA内核均表现优异，证明了该方法在框架开发效率与硬件性能之间实现平衡的可行性。
Lost in Fog: Sensor Perturbations Expose Reasoning Fragility in Driving VLAs
Authors: Abhinaw Priyadershi, Jelena Frtunikj
First: 2026-05-20T17:34:02+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-20T17:34:02+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Interpretable autonomous driving planners depend not only on generating explanations, but also on those explanations remaining reliable under real-world sensor degradation. In this paper we present a controlled perturbation study of Vision-Language-Action (VLA) robustness in autonomous driving, evaluating Alpamayo R1 (10B parameters) across 1,996 scenarios under eight sensor perturbations (Gaussian noise at four intensities, two lighting extremes, and two fog levels; ${\sim}18{,}000$ inference trials). We find that reasoning consistency is a high-fidelity indicator of trajectory reliability: when Chain-of-Causation (CoC) explanations change after perturbation, trajectory deviation spikes $5.3{\times}$ (21.8m vs 4.1m), with $r\!=\!0.99$ across attack types and $r_{pb}\!=\!0.53$ per-sample (Cohen's $d\!=\!1.12$). A controlled ablation provides evidence that enabling CoC generation is associated with improved trajectory accuracy (11.8% on average across conditions; $p < 0.0001$) under matched inference settings. Over the tested noise range ($σ\in \{10, 30, 50, 70\}$), degradation is approximately linear ($R^2\!=\!0.957$), while standard input preprocessing defenses provide only marginal relief. Together, these results establish CoC consistency as a quantitative proxy for planning safety and motivate reasoning-based runtime monitoring for safer VLA deployment.
中文标题/摘要
标题：雾中迷失：传感器扰动揭示驾驶视觉-语言-动作模型的推理脆弱性
可解释的自动驾驶规划器不仅需要生成解释，还要求这些解释在现实世界传感器退化条件下保持可靠性。本文通过受控扰动研究，评估了自动驾驶中视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型的鲁棒性，对Alpamayo R1（100亿参数）模型在1996个场景下进行八种传感器扰动测试（四种强度的高斯噪声、两种光照极端情况和两种雾度水平；约18,000次推理试验）。我们发现，推理一致性是轨迹可靠性的高保真指标：当因果链（CoC）解释在扰动后发生变化时，轨迹偏差激增5.3倍（21.8米 vs 4.1米），攻击类型间相关系数r=0.99，每样本相关系数r_pb=0.53（Cohen's d=1.12）。受控消融实验表明，在匹配推理设置下，启用CoC生成与轨迹精度提升相关（平均提升11.8%；p<0.0001）。在测试噪声范围（σ∈{10,30,50,70}）内，退化近似线性（R²=0.957），而标准输入预处理防御措施仅提供微弱缓解。这些结果确立了CoC一致性作为规划安全性的量化代理，并推动基于推理的运行时监控，以实现更安全的VLA部署。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates the fragility of Vision-Language-Action models in autonomous driving under sensor perturbations, motivated by the need for reliable explanations that persist amid real-world degradation. The researchers evaluate Alpamayo R1 across 1,996 scenarios with eight types of sensor noise—including Gaussian noise and varying fog levels—over 18,000 inference trials. They find that changes in Chain-of-Causation explanations strongly correlate with trajectory deviation, with a 5.3-fold increase in deviation when explanations shift, and a near-perfect correlation (r=0.99) across perturbations. Ablation experiments confirm that enabling CoC generation improves trajectory accuracy by 11.8% on average (p<0.0001), and degradation under noise follows a near-linear trend (R²=0.957), while standard preprocessing defenses offer minimal protection. These results position CoC consistency as a key indicator of planning safety and suggest reasoning-based runtime monitoring as a critical safety mechanism.
该研究旨在评估视觉-语言-动作模型在自动驾驶中面对传感器扰动时的脆弱性，动机在于确保解释在真实世界传感器退化下仍具可靠性。通过对Alpamayo R1模型在1996个场景、18000次推理试验中施加八类扰动（含高斯噪声、光照极端与雾气等级），研究发现当链式因果解释发生变化时，轨迹偏差激增5.3倍（21.8米 vs 4.1米），且扰动类型间相关性高达0.99。消融实验表明，启用链式因果生成可平均提升轨迹精度11.8%（p<0.0001），噪声范围内的退化呈近线性关系（R²=0.957），而标准输入预处理防御效果微弱。研究结果确立链式因果一致性为规划安全的量化指标，并推动基于推理的运行时监控作为更安全的VLA部署手段。
CTFExplorer: Evaluating LLM Offensive Agents Through Multi-Target Web CTF Benchmarking
Authors: Nanda Rani, Kimberly Milner, Minghao Shao, Meet Udeshi, Haoran Xi, Venkata Sai Charan Putrevu, Saksham Aggarwal, Sandeep K. Shukla, Prashanth Krishnamurthy, Farshad Khorrami, Muhammad Shafique, Ramesh Karri
First: 2026-02-08T15:56:22+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-20T17:32:45+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Existing benchmarks for LLM-based offensive security agents use isolated, single-target setups with a known vulnerable service and fixed objective. They measure exploitation effectively, but miss how real Capture-the-Flag (CTF) participants triage unknown surfaces, prioritize targets, and allocate effort under uncertainty. Current evaluations therefore fail to assess strategic reasoning beyond exploitation alone. To address this, we introduce \textit{CTFExplorer}, a benchmark suite that shifts offensive security evaluation toward a multi-target setting, which tests how agents explore, prioritize, and chain attacks. CTFExplorer deploys 40 web-based vulnerable services within a single environment, where agents must autonomously discover, distinguish, and exploit targets without predefined guidance. We also present a reactive multi-agent setup as a reference agent framework and develop an agent-agnostic evaluation framework that records structured reasoning traces for fine-grained assessment. This enables behavioral evaluation beyond binary flag capture, such as how agents manage target selection, handle failed hypotheses, coordinate across multiple stages, and extract security intelligence.
中文标题/摘要
标题：CTFExplorer：通过多目标Web CTF基准测试评估基于大语言模型的攻击代理
现有针对基于大语言模型的攻击安全代理的基准测试采用隔离的、单目标设置，包含已知漏洞服务和固定目标，能有效衡量利用能力，但未能体现真实夺旗（CTF）参与者如何在未知环境中进行表面筛选、目标优先级排序及在不确定性下分配资源。当前评估因此无法衡量超越单纯利用之外的战略推理能力。为解决此问题，我们引入\textit{CTFExplorer}，一套将攻击安全评估转向多目标环境的基准测试套件，用于测试代理如何探索、优先选择并串联攻击。CTFExplorer在一个环境中部署40个基于Web的漏洞服务，要求代理自主发现、区分并利用目标，无需预设指导。我们还提出一种反应式多智能体设置作为参考智能体框架，并开发了一种与智能体无关的评估框架，用于记录结构化推理轨迹，以实现细粒度评估。这使得评估超越二元夺旗结果，例如代理如何管理目标选择、处理失败假设、跨多个阶段协调以及提取安全情报。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitation of existing LLM offensive security benchmarks that only evaluate exploitation in isolated, single-target environments, CTFExplorer introduces a multi-target web CTF benchmark with 40 vulnerable services to assess agents' strategic reasoning. Agents must autonomously explore, prioritize, and chain attacks across unknown surfaces without predefined guidance, and the framework supports reactive multi-agent setups and agent-agnostic evaluation via structured reasoning traces. Experimental results show agents vary significantly in target selection, hypothesis management, and coordination, revealing that performance beyond flag capture depends heavily on strategic decision-making rather than just exploitation capability.
为弥补现有LLM攻击代理评估仅在孤立单目标环境下测试漏洞利用的不足，CTFExplorer构建了一个包含40个Web漏洞服务的多目标CTF基准，评估代理在无预设指引下自主探索、优先级排序与攻击链构建的能力。该方法采用反应式多智能体框架与无代理依赖的评估体系，记录结构化推理轨迹，支持对策略行为的细粒度分析，如目标选择、假设管理与多阶段协调。实验结果表明，表现优异的模型在目标优先级排序、假设适应性测试与资源分配效率方面显著优于其他模型。
TempGlitch: Evaluating Vision-Language Models for Temporal Glitch Detection in Gameplay Videos
Authors: Yakun Yu, Ashley Wiens, Adrián Barahona-Ríos, Benedict Wilkins, Saman Zadtootaghaj, Nabajeet Barman, Cor-Paul Bezemer
First: 2026-05-20T17:32:26+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-20T17:32:26+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Vision-language models (VLMs) are increasingly being explored for video game quality assurance, especially gameplay glitch detection. Most existing evaluations, however, treat glitches as static visual anomalies, asking models to detect failures from a single frame. We argue that this framing misses a key distinction: some glitches are spatial and visible in an isolated frame, whereas others are temporal and become evident only through changes across ordered frames. A preliminary study confirms this gap, showing that temporal glitches are substantially harder for VLMs to detect than spatial ones. To enable systematic evaluation of this underexplored setting, we introduce TempGlitch, a controlled gameplay video benchmark for temporal glitch detection. TempGlitch covers five temporal glitch types with balanced per-category samples, together with paired glitch-free videos that enable reliable binary evaluation. We evaluate 12 proprietary and open-weight VLMs across multiple frame-sampling settings. Our results show that current VLMs remain near chance on TempGlitch, often collapsing into either overly conservative behavior that misses most glitches or overly sensitive behavior that flags clean videos as glitchy. Moreover, denser frame sampling and larger model size do not reliably resolve these failures. TempGlitch provides a focused testbed for temporal reasoning, robust gameplay understanding, and automated glitch detection with VLMs. Code and data are available at the project website.
中文标题/摘要
标题：TempGlitch：评估视觉语言模型在游戏视频中时序错误检测中的表现
视觉语言模型（VLMs）正越来越多地被用于视频游戏质量保证，特别是游戏故障检测。然而，现有大多数评估将故障视为静态视觉异常，要求模型从单帧中检测失败。我们认为，这种框架忽略了关键区别：有些故障是空间性的，在单帧中即可观察到，而另一些则是时序性的，只有通过有序帧之间的变化才能显现。初步研究表明，时序故障比空间故障对VLMs而言显著更难检测。为系统评估这一尚未充分探索的场景，我们引入了TempGlitch，一个用于时序故障检测的可控游戏视频基准。TempGlitch涵盖五类时序故障，每类样本均衡，同时配有无故障视频对，以支持可靠的二元评估。我们在多种帧采样设置下评估了12个专有和开源权重的VLMs。结果显示，当前VLMs在TempGlitch上的表现接近随机水平，常表现为过于保守（漏检大部分故障）或过于敏感（将无故障视频误标为故障）。此外，更密集的帧采样和更大的模型规模并不能可靠地解决这些失败。TempGlitch为时序推理、稳健的游戏理解及基于VLMs的自动化故障检测提供了聚焦的测试平台。代码和数据可在项目网站获取。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observation that existing vision-language models (VLMs) are poorly equipped to detect temporal glitches in gameplay videos—since most evaluations treat glitches as static visual anomalies—this study introduces TempGlitch, a controlled benchmark designed to evaluate VLMs specifically for detecting glitches that manifest across sequential frames. The benchmark includes five types of temporal glitches with balanced samples and paired glitch-free videos for reliable binary classification. Evaluating 12 VLMs across varying frame-sampling settings, the study finds that current models perform near chance, often exhibiting either overly conservative or overly sensitive behavior, and that increasing model size or frame density does not consistently improve performance. This highlights a critical gap in temporal reasoning and robust gameplay understanding for VLMs.
鉴于现有视觉语言模型（VLM）在检测游戏视频中的时序故障时表现不佳——因为大多数评估将故障视为静态视觉异常——本研究提出了TempGlitch，一个专门用于评估VLM时序故障检测能力的可控基准。该基准包含五类时序故障，样本均衡，并配有无故障视频以支持二元评估。在多种帧采样策略下对12个VLM进行评估，结果表明当前模型表现接近随机水平，常表现出过于保守（漏检）或过于敏感（误报）的行为，且增大模型规模或增加帧密度并不能稳定提升性能，揭示了VLM在时序推理和游戏理解方面的关键缺陷。
torchtune: PyTorch native post-training library
Authors: Mark Obozov, Maxime Griot, Joseph Cummings, Evan Smothers, Felipe Mello, Rafi Ayub, Philip John Bontrager, Salman Mohammadi, Ariel Kwiatkowski, Nathan Azrak, Mircea Mironenco
First: 2026-05-20T17:32:08+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-20T17:32:08+00:00
Comments: 14 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Modern LLMs typically require multistage training pipelines to achieve strong downstream performance, with post-training serving as the main interface for adapting open-weight models. We introduce torchtune, a PyTorch-native library designed to streamline the post-training lifecycle of LLMs, enabling efficient fine-tuning, experimentation, and deployment-oriented workflows. Unlike many existing fine-tuning frameworks, which often optimize for ease of use, specialized recipes, or hardware efficiency at the cost of transparency and extensibility, torchtune emphasizes modularity, hackability, and direct access to the underlying PyTorch components. In this paper, we present the design principles behind torchtune, describe how they are reflected in its model builders, training recipes, and distributed training stack, and evaluate the library across representative post-training settings. We compare against popular fine-tuning frameworks, including Axolotl and Unsloth, and show that torchtune provides strong performance and memory efficiency across many settings while remaining flexible enough for rapid research iteration. These results position torchtune as a practical foundation for reproducible LLMs post-training research.
中文标题/摘要
标题：torchtune：PyTorch原生的后训练库
现代大语言模型通常需要多阶段训练流水线以实现强大的下游性能，其中后训练是适配开源权重模型的主要接口。我们引入了torchtune，一个专为PyTorch设计的原生库，旨在简化大语言模型的后训练生命周期，支持高效的微调、实验和面向部署的工作流。与许多现有微调框架不同，后者往往以易用性、专用配方或硬件效率为优化目标，牺牲了透明度和可扩展性，torchtune则强调模块化、可修改性以及对底层PyTorch组件的直接访问。本文阐述了torchtune的设计原则，说明其如何体现在模型构建器、训练配方和分布式训练栈中，并在代表性后训练场景中评估该库。我们对比了流行的微调框架（如Axolotl和Unsloth），表明torchtune在多种场景下均能提供卓越的性能和内存效率，同时保持足够的灵活性以支持快速研究迭代。这些结果使torchtune成为可复现大语言模型后训练研究的实用基础。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for a transparent, extensible, and PyTorch-native solution for LLM post-training, torchtune introduces a library designed to streamline fine-tuning, experimentation, and deployment workflows. It emphasizes modularity and direct access to PyTorch components over convenience or hardware optimization, enabling rapid research iteration. Experimental comparisons against Axolotl and Unsloth show torchtune achieves strong performance and memory efficiency across diverse post-training settings while maintaining flexibility for custom workflows.
为满足大语言模型后训练阶段对透明性、可扩展性和效率的需求，torchtune 提出一个原生 PyTorch 库，强调模块化设计与对底层组件的直接访问，而非牺牲灵活性换取易用性或硬件优化。该库支持模型构建、训练和部署，允许用户进行精细控制，适用于多种后训练任务。实验对比 Axolotl 和 Unsloth 等流行框架表明，torchtune 在性能和内存效率上表现优异，同时具备更强的灵活性，便于快速研究迭代，成为可复现 LLM 后训练研究的实用基础工具。
ReMATF: Recurrent Motion-Adaptive Multi-scale Turbulence Mitigation for Dynamic Scenes
Authors: Zhiming Liu, Zhicheng Zou, Nantheera Anantrasirichai
First: 2026-05-20T17:28:49+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-20T17:28:49+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Atmospheric turbulence severely degrades video quality by introducing distortions such as geometric warping, blur, and temporal flickering, posing significant challenges to both visual clarity and temporal consistency. Current state-of-the-art methods are based on transformer, 3D architectures and require multi-frame input, but their large computational cost and memory usage limit real-time deployment, especially in resource-constrained scenarios. In this work, we propose ReMATF, a lightweight recurrent framework that restores videos using only two frames at a time while preserving spatial detail and temporal stability. ReMATF combines a multi-scale encoder-decoder with temporal warping and a motion-adaptive temporal fusion module that performs per-pixel fusion between the warped previous output and the current prediction to enhance coherence without enlarging the temporal window. This design reduces flicker, sharpens details, and remains efficient. Experiments on synthetic and real turbulence datasets show consistent improvements in PSNR/SSIM and perceptual quality (LPIPS), along with substantially faster inference than multi-frame transformer baselines, making ReMATF suitable turbulence mitigation in resource-constrained scenarios.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ReMATF：面向动态场景的循环运动自适应多尺度湍流抑制
大气湍流严重降低视频质量，引入几何形变、模糊和时间闪烁等失真，对视觉清晰度和时间一致性构成重大挑战。当前最先进的方法基于Transformer和3D架构，需多帧输入，但其高计算成本和内存占用限制了实时部署，尤其在资源受限场景下。本文提出ReMATF，一种轻量级循环框架，仅需两帧即可恢复视频，同时保持空间细节和时间稳定性。ReMATF结合多尺度编码器-解码器、时间形变与运动自适应时间融合模块，通过在像素级融合形变后的前一帧输出与当前预测，增强一致性而不扩大时间窗口。该设计有效减少闪烁、锐化细节，且保持高效。在合成与真实湍流数据集上的实验表明，ReMATF在PSNR/SSIM和感知质量（LPIPS）上持续优于基线，推理速度显著快于多帧Transformer方法，适用于资源受限场景下的湍流抑制。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to restore dynamic video scenes degraded by atmospheric turbulence while maintaining real-time efficiency, ReMATF introduces a lightweight recurrent framework that processes only two frames at a time. It combines a multi-scale encoder-decoder with a motion-adaptive temporal fusion module that fuses warped previous outputs with current predictions on a per-pixel basis, enhancing temporal coherence without expanding the temporal window. Experimental results on synthetic and real turbulence datasets demonstrate consistent gains in PSNR, SSIM, and perceptual quality (LPIPS), alongside significantly faster inference compared to multi-frame transformer baselines, making it suitable for resource-constrained environments.
为解决大气湍流导致动态视频画面失真（如几何扭曲、模糊和时间闪烁）且需实时处理的问题，ReMATF提出一种轻量级递归框架，仅用两帧即可恢复视频并保持空间细节与时间稳定性。该框架结合多尺度编码器-解码器与运动自适应时序融合模块，通过逐像素融合前一帧的变形输出与当前预测结果，提升时序一致性而不扩大时序窗口。实验表明，在合成与真实湍流数据集上，其PSNR、SSIM及感知质量（LPIPS）均优于基线，推理速度显著快于多帧Transformer方法，适合资源受限场景。
Fully Actuated Manifold Constraint Based Output Feedback Control for Input-Constrained Uncertain Nonlinear Systems
Authors: Dianrui Mu, Changchun Hua, Yafeng Li, Jiannan Chen, Rao Wei
First: 2026-05-20T17:28:24+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-20T17:28:24+00:00
Comments: 22 pages, 12 figures, 2 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This paper presents a low-complexity, model-free, output-feedback controller for a class of unknown time-varying nonlinear systems with unknown input constraints. The controller achieves the preset control accuracy when the actuator is not saturated and maintains flexible control accuracy after actuator saturation. This result extends existing constraint control methods for linear manifolds to a more general form, including the construction of nonlinear manifolds and various types of constraints, thereby achieving preset control accuracy within finite or fixed time. Additionally, flexible control under unknown saturation is achieved through the construction of an error-driven flexible constraint. Finally, second-order and higher-order control examples and simulations are provided.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于完全驱动流形约束的输入受限不确定非线性系统的输出反馈控制
本文提出了一种低复杂度、无模型、输出反馈控制器，适用于一类未知时变非线性系统且输入约束未知。当执行器未饱和时，控制器可实现预设控制精度；执行器饱和后仍能保持灵活控制精度。该结果将现有线性流形约束控制方法推广至更一般形式，包括非线性流形构造及多种约束类型，从而在有限或固定时间内实现预设控制精度。此外，通过构建误差驱动的柔性约束，实现了未知饱和条件下的灵活控制。最后，提供了二阶及以上控制实例与仿真。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for robust control of uncertain nonlinear systems with unknown input constraints, this paper proposes a model-free output-feedback controller that operates under fully actuated manifold constraints. The method constructs a nonlinear manifold and employs an error-driven flexible constraint to adapt to actuator saturation, ensuring preset control accuracy when actuators are unsaturated and maintaining flexible accuracy afterward. Experimental results on second-order and higher-order systems demonstrate the controller’s ability to achieve finite-time convergence and maintain performance despite unknown saturation, extending prior linear manifold methods to more general nonlinear settings.
为解决未知输入约束下不确定非线性系统的鲁棒控制问题，本文提出一种无需模型的输出反馈控制器，可在执行器饱和与非饱和状态下分别实现预设精度与柔性精度。方法通过构建全驱动流形约束并引入误差驱动的柔性约束，动态适应饱和情况。仿真结果表明，该控制器在执行器未饱和时达成预设精度，饱和时保持柔性精度，验证于二阶及高阶系统。
Neural Negative Binomial Regression for Weekly Seismicity Forecasting: Per-Cell Dispersion Estimation and Tail Risk Assessment
Authors: Alim Igilik
First: 2026-05-20T17:27:27+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-20T17:27:27+00:00
Comments: 28 pages, 9 figures. Source code available at https://github.com/Al1mkaYandere/seismic-probabilistic-modeling
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Standard approaches to forecasting the weekly number of earthquakes on a spatial grid rely on the Poisson distribution with a single global dispersion assumption. We show that this assumption is systematically violated in seismic data from Central Asia (2010-2024), where a likelihood-ratio test with boundary correction strongly rejects the Poisson hypothesis (p < 10^{-179}). The main contribution of this work is the EarthquakeNet architecture, which provides an endogenous per-cell estimate of the overdispersion parameter alpha via a neural network (spatial embeddings + MLP), without explicit spatial covariance specification. In contrast to existing negative binomial regression approaches in seismological forecasting, which typically assume a single global alpha, the proposed per-cell formulation allows the model to identify spatial heterogeneity in seismic clustering and to construct probabilistic risk-aware alerts via quantiles of the predicted distribution. A walk-forward evaluation (2018-2023) over four systems shows an 8.6 percent reduction in mean pinball deviation (MPD) relative to a negative binomial GLM baseline. The strongest improvements are observed in the tail regime (Y >= 5), where the continuous ranked probability score (CRPS) of the proposed model is 12.5 percent lower than that of the baseline, indicating improved calibration in extreme-event forecasting.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于每周地震活动预测的神经负二项回归：单格分散度估计与尾部风险评估
标准方法在空间网格上预测每周地震数量时，依赖泊松分布并假设全局单一离散度。我们表明，在中亚地区（2010-2024）的地震数据中，该假设被系统性违反，边界校正的似然比检验强烈拒绝泊松假设（p < 10^{-179}）。 本工作的主要贡献是EarthquakeNet架构，该架构通过神经网络（空间嵌入+MLP）内生地为每个网格单元估计超离散参数α，无需显式指定空间协方差。与现有地震学预测中通常假设全局单一α的负二项回归方法不同，所提出的单格公式使模型能够识别地震聚集的空间异质性，并通过预测分布的分位数构建概率风险感知警报。 在四个系统上的滚动验证（2018-2023）显示，与负二项GLM基线相比，平均Pinball偏差（MPD）降低8.6%。在尾部区域（Y >= 5）改进最显著，所提模型的连续概率评分（CRPS）比基线低12.5%，表明在极端事件预测中的校准能力提升。

Motivated by the systematic violation of the Poisson assumption in seismic data from Central Asia, this work introduces EarthquakeNet, a neural architecture that estimates per-cell overdispersion parameters via spatial embeddings and MLPs, avoiding global dispersion assumptions. Evaluated via walk-forward testing across four systems from 2018 to 2023, the model reduces mean pinball deviation by 8.6% compared to a negative binomial GLM baseline, with the most significant gains in tail events (Y >= 5), where its CRPS is 12.5% lower, indicating better calibration for extreme seismic risk.
