Gaussian Sheaf Neural Networks
Authors: André Ribeiro, Ana Luiza Tenório, Tiago da Silva, Diego Mesquita
First: 2026-05-20T17:26:32+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-20T17:26:32+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) have become the de facto standard for learning on relational data. While traditional GNNs' message passing is well suited for vector-valued node features, there are cases in which node features are better represented by probability distributions than real vectors. Concretely, when node features are Gaussians, characterized by a mean and a covariance matrix, naively concatenating their parameters into a single vector and applying standard message passing discards the geometric and algebraic structure that governs means and covariances. We propose Gaussian Sheaf Neural Networks (GSNNs), a principled framework that incorporates these inductive biases into graph-based learning. Building on the theory of cellular sheaves, we derive a new Laplacian operator that generalizes the sheaf Laplacian to this setting and preserves its key properties. We complement our theoretical contributions with experiments on synthetic and real-world data that illustrate the practical relevance of GSNNs.
中文标题/摘要
标题：高斯束神经网络
图神经网络（GNNs）已成为关系数据学习的行业标准。虽然传统GNN的消息传递机制适合向量值节点特征，但在某些情况下，节点特征更适合用概率分布表示而非实向量。具体而言，当节点特征为高斯分布（由均值和协方差矩阵表征）时，简单地将参数拼接成单个向量并应用标准消息传递会丢弃支配均值和协方差的几何与代数结构。我们提出高斯束神经网络（GSNNs），一种将这些归纳偏置纳入图学习的原理性框架。基于细胞束理论，我们推导出一种新的拉普拉斯算子，将其扩展至该场景并保留其关键性质。我们通过合成数据和真实世界数据的实验，补充了理论贡献，展示了GSNNs的实用价值。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to handle node features represented as Gaussian distributions rather than vectors, the paper introduces Gaussian Sheaf Neural Networks (GSNNs) to preserve the geometric and algebraic structure of means and covariances during message passing. Building on cellular sheaf theory, GSNNs derive a novel Laplacian operator that generalizes the sheaf Laplacian to Gaussian features while retaining its key properties. Experimental results on synthetic and real-world datasets demonstrate that GSNNs outperform standard GNNs in tasks involving Gaussian node features, confirming the practical value of incorporating these inductive biases into graph learning.
为处理以高斯分布表示的节点特征（而非向量），本文提出高斯层析神经网络（GSNNs），旨在保留均值与协方差的几何与代数结构。基于细胞层析理论，GSNNs推导出一种新的拉普拉斯算子，将层析拉普拉斯推广至高斯特征并保持其关键性质。在合成与真实数据上的实验表明，GSNNs在涉及高斯节点特征的任务中优于传统GNN，验证了在图学习中融入此类归纳偏置的实用性。
DECO: Sparse Mixture-of-Experts with Dense-Comparable Performance on End-Side Devices
Authors: Chenyang Song, Weilin Zhao, Xu Han, Chaojun Xiao, Yingfa Chen, Zhiyuan Liu
First: 2026-05-11T17:58:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-20T17:26:14+00:00
Comments: 15 pages, 10 figures, 12 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
While Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) scales model capacity without proportionally increasing computation, its massive total parameter footprint creates significant storage and memory-access bottlenecks, which hinder efficient end-side deployment that simultaneously requires high performance, low computational cost, and small storage overhead. To achieve these properties, we present DECO, a sparse MoE architecture designed to match the performance of dense Transformers under identical total parameter budgets and training tokens. DECO utilizes the differentiable and flexible ReLU-based routing enhanced by learnable expert-wise scaling, which adaptively balances the contributions of routed and shared experts. Furthermore, we introduce NormSiLU, an activation function that normalizes inputs prior to SiLU operators, producing a more stable trend of routed-expert activation ratio and a higher intrinsic sparsity level. We also identify an empirical advantage in using non-gated MLP experts with ReLU-based routing, indicating the possibility of MoE architecture simplification. Experiments demonstrate that DECO, activating only 20% of routed experts, matches dense performance and outperforms established MoE baselines. Our specialized acceleration kernel delivers a 2.93$\times$ speedup on Jetson AGX Orin compared with dense inference. Code and checkpoints are available at https://github.com/thunlp/DECO.
中文标题/摘要
标题：DECO：在端侧设备上实现密集级性能的稀疏专家混合架构
尽管专家混合（MoE）能在不按比例增加计算量的情况下扩展模型容量，但其庞大的总参数量导致显著的存储和内存访问瓶颈，阻碍了同时要求高性能、低计算成本和小存储开销的端侧高效部署。为实现这些特性，我们提出了DECO，一种稀疏MoE架构，其在相同总参数预算和训练token数下可匹配密集Transformer的性能。DECO采用可微且灵活的基于ReLU的路由机制，并结合可学习的专家级缩放，自适应平衡路由与共享专家的贡献。此外，我们引入了NormSiLU激活函数，该函数在SiLU操作前对输入进行归一化，从而产生更稳定的路由专家激活比率和更高的内在稀疏性。我们还发现，使用非门控MLP专家与基于ReLU的路由机制具有实证优势，表明MoE架构可简化。实验表明，仅激活20%路由专家的DECO即可匹配密集模型性能，并优于现有MoE基线。我们的专用加速内核在Jetson AGX Orin上相比密集推理实现2.93倍加速。代码和检查点可在https://github.com/thunlp/DECO获取。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to deploy high-performance models on edge devices without excessive storage or computational overhead, DECO introduces a sparse Mixture-of-Experts architecture that matches dense Transformer performance under identical parameter budgets. It employs a differentiable ReLU-based routing mechanism enhanced by learnable expert-wise scaling to adaptively balance expert contributions, and introduces NormSiLU to stabilize activation ratios and boost sparsity. Experimental results show that DECO activates only 20% of routed experts while achieving performance equal to dense models and outperforming existing MoE baselines, with a 2.93x speedup on Jetson AGX Orin during inference.
为解决MoE模型在边缘设备部署时因参数量大导致的存储与内存瓶颈问题，DECO提出一种稀疏MoE架构，在相同参数预算和训练token下实现与密集Transformer相当的性能。该架构采用可微分的ReLU路由机制并结合可学习的专家级缩放，动态平衡专家贡献，同时引入NormSiLU激活函数以稳定激活比例并提升内在稀疏性。实验表明，仅激活20%专家的DECO即可达到密集模型性能并超越现有MoE基线，且通过专用加速内核在Jetson AGX Orin上实现2.93倍推理加速。
Agentic Model Checking
Authors: Youcheng Sun, Jiawen Liu, Daniel Kroening, Jason Xue
First: 2026-05-20T17:25:52+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-20T17:25:52+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Verifying LLM-generated systems code is hard: bugs are prevalent, formal specifications are missing, and safety contracts are encoded implicitly at call sites rather than enforced at function boundaries. We propose agentic model checking, a paradigm that couples LLM agents with a bounded model checking backend under the principle agents propose, solvers verify: agents handle tasks requiring semantic judgment (spec inference, check selection, counterexample classification, refinement proposal) while BMC discharges every soundness-relevant decision. The paradigm rests on three commitments. Specifications are inferred top-down from caller context in a restricted DSL that translates deterministically into the backend's assume/assert primitives, with optional functional-correctness clauses lifting verification from panic-freeness to behavioural faithfulness. Verification is compositional: each function is checked in isolation against its spec with callees replaced by postcondition-constrained stubs, so per-query cost scales with a single function's state space and refinements propagate automatically to callers. Counterexamples are not bug reports: they pass through a validation pipeline (reachability, callee feasibility, dynamic replay, realism audit) that distinguishes active in-tree crashes from latent public-API failures, while modelling artifacts drive a refinement loop rather than being suppressed. We instantiate the approach in BMC-Agent and evaluate it on LLM-generated kernel and compiler code in C and Rust alongside mature OSS-Fuzz-hardened libraries, confirming real defects, producing bounded clean verifications on heavily-fuzzed surfaces, and establishing functional equivalence on selected algorithmic functions.
中文标题/摘要
标题：代理模型检查
验证大语言模型生成的系统代码困难重重：普遍存在缺陷、缺乏形式化规范，且安全契约仅隐式编码于调用点而非函数边界强制执行。我们提出代理模型检查范式，将大语言模型代理与有界模型检查后端耦合，遵循“代理提议、求解器验证”原则：代理负责需语义判断的任务（规范推断、检查选择、反例分类、细化建议），而BMC负责验证所有与正确性相关的决策。该范式基于三项承诺：规范从调用上下文自顶向下推断，使用受限DSL，可确定性地翻译为后端的assume/assert原语，并可选添加功能正确性子句，将验证从无崩溃性提升至行为忠实性；验证具有组合性：每个函数独立验证，以规范为基准，被调用者替换为后置条件约束的桩函数，因此每次查询成本仅与单个函数状态空间成正比，且细化自动传播至调用者；反例非缺陷报告：其经由验证流水线（可达性、被调用者可行性、动态重放、现实性审计）区分活跃的树内崩溃与潜在的公共API故障，同时模型产物驱动细化循环而非被抑制。我们在BMC-Agent中实现该方法，并在C和Rust语言中LLM生成的内核与编译器代码，以及成熟的OSS-Fuzz加固库上进行评估，确认真实缺陷，对高度模糊化表面实现有界清洁验证，并在选定算法函数上建立功能等价性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the prevalence of bugs in LLM-generated code and the lack of formal specifications, the paper introduces agentic model checking, a method that pairs LLM agents with a bounded model checker under the 'agents propose, solvers verify' principle. Agents handle semantic tasks like specification inference and counterexample classification, while the backend ensures soundness by verifying every decision. The approach uses a restricted DSL to infer specifications from caller context, supports compositional verification via stubbed callees, and refines models based on validated counterexamples rather than suppressing them. Experiments on LLM-generated kernel and compiler code, alongside OSS-Fuzz-hardened libraries, confirmed real defects, achieved bounded clean verifications on heavily-fuzzed code, and demonstrated functional equivalence on selected algorithms.
鉴于LLM生成代码中普遍存在缺陷且缺乏形式化规范，本文提出“代理模型检查”方法，将LLM代理与有界模型检查器结合，遵循“代理提出、求解器验证”的原则。代理负责语义任务如规范推断与反例分类，后端则验证所有关键决策以确保正确性。该方法通过受限DSL从调用上下文推断规范，支持通过桩函数替换被调用者实现组合式验证，并基于验证的反例驱动模型迭代优化而非简单抑制。实验在LLM生成的内核与编译器代码及加固的开源库上进行，成功发现真实缺陷，对高模糊度代码实现有界正确性验证，并在部分算法函数上验证了功能等价性。
Agent Security is a Systems Problem
Authors: Mihai Christodorescu, Earlence Fernandes, Ashish Hooda, Somesh Jha, Johann Rehberger, Kamalika Chaudhuri, Xiaohan Fu, Khawaja Shams, Guy Amir, Jihye Choi, Sarthak Choudhary, Nils Palumbo, Andrey Labunets, Nishit V. Pandya
First: 2026-05-18T18:11:17+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-20T17:25:38+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We take the position that agent security must be approached as a systems problem: the AI model powering the agent must be treated as an untrusted component, and security invariants must be enforced at the system level. Through this lens, efforts to increase model robustness (the dominant viewpoint in the community) are insufficient on their own. Instead, we must complement existing efforts with techniques from the systems security domain. Based on our experience as cybersecurity researchers in operating systems, networks, formal methods, and adversarial machine learning, we articulate a set of core principles, grounded in decades of systems security research, that provide a foundation for designing agentic systems with predictable guarantees. As evidence, we analyze eleven representative real-world attacks on agents and discuss how systems principles, if realized, could have prevented these attacks. We also identify the research challenges that stand in the way of implementing these principles in agents.
中文标题/摘要
标题：代理安全是一个系统性问题
我们认为，代理安全必须作为系统性问题来处理：驱动代理的AI模型应被视为不可信组件，安全不变量必须在系统层面加以强制执行。从这一视角看，当前社区主流观点——即通过增强模型鲁棒性来提升安全——是不够的。相反，我们必须将系统安全领域的技术与现有努力相结合。基于我们在操作系统、网络、形式化方法和对抗性机器学习领域的网络安全研究经验，我们提出了一组根植于数十年系统安全研究的核心原则，为设计具有可预测保障的代理系统奠定基础。作为证据，我们分析了十一个针对代理的真实世界攻击案例，并讨论了若系统性原则得以实现，这些攻击本可被预防。同时，我们也指出了阻碍这些原则在代理中实施的研究挑战。
Summary / 总结
The paper argues that agent security should be treated as a systems problem, requiring the AI model to be viewed as an untrusted component with security invariants enforced at the system level rather than relying solely on model robustness. Drawing from decades of systems security research, the authors propose core principles grounded in operating systems, networks, formal methods, and adversarial machine learning to design agents with predictable security guarantees. They analyze eleven real-world attacks on agents and demonstrate how applying systems-level security techniques could have mitigated or prevented these incidents, while also identifying key research challenges that currently hinder the practical implementation of these principles.
本文主张将代理安全视为系统层面的问题，将AI模型视为不可信组件，需在系统层面强制执行安全不变量，而非仅依赖模型鲁棒性。作者结合操作系统、网络、形式化方法及对抗机器学习领域的经验，提出一套基于系统安全研究的指导原则，用于构建具备可预测安全保证的代理系统。通过对十一例真实攻击案例的分析，证明系统级方法可有效预防这些漏洞，同时指出当前阻碍这些原则落地的关键研究挑战。
iTryOn: Mastering Interactive Video Virtual Try-On with Spatial-Semantic Guidance
Authors: Jun Zheng, Zhengze Xu, Mengting Chen, Jing Wang, Jinsong Lan, Xiaoyong Zhu, Kaifu Zhang, Bo Zheng, Xiaodan Liang
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-05-20T17:23:32+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-20T17:23:32+00:00
Comments: Project Page: https://zhengjun-ai.github.io/itryon-page. Accepted by ICML 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Video Virtual Try-On (VVT) aims to seamlessly replace a garment on a person in a video with a new one. While existing methods have made significant strides in maintaining temporal consistency, they are predominantly confined to non-interactive scenarios where models merely showcase garments. This limitation overlooks a crucial aspect of real-world apparel presentation: active human-garment interaction. To bridge this gap, we introduce and formalize a new challenging task: Interactive Video Virtual Try-On (Interactive VVT), where subjects in the video actively engage with their clothing. This task introduces unique challenges beyond simple texture preservation, including: (1) resolving the semantic ambiguity of interactions from standard pose information, and (2) learning complex garment deformations from video where interactive moments are sparse and brief. To address these challenges, we propose iTryOn, a novel framework built upon a large-scale video diffusion Transformer. iTryOn pioneers a multi-level interaction injection mechanism to guide the generation of complex dynamics. At the spatial level, we introduce a garment-agnostic 3D hand prior to provide fine-grained guidance for precise hand-garment contact, effectively resolving spatial ambiguity. At the semantic level, iTryOn leverages global captions for overall context and time-stamped action captions for localized interactions, synchronized via our novel Action-aware Rotational Position Embedding (A-RoPE). Extensive experiments demonstrate that iTryOn not only achieves state-of-the-art performance on traditional VVT benchmarks but also establishes a commanding lead in the new interactive setting, marking a significant step towards more dynamic and controllable virtual try-on experiences.
中文标题/摘要
标题：iTryOn：基于空间语义引导的交互式视频虚拟试穿
视频虚拟试穿（VVT）旨在无缝替换视频中人物所穿衣物。现有方法虽在保持时间一致性方面取得显著进展，但多局限于非交互场景，仅展示服装，忽视了现实服装展示中关键的人与衣物主动交互环节。为弥合此差距，我们提出并形式化了更具挑战性的新任务：交互式视频虚拟试穿（Interactive VVT），其中视频中人物主动与衣物互动。该任务超越简单纹理保持，面临两大挑战：（1）从标准姿态信息中解析交互的语义歧义；（2）从稀疏且短暂的交互视频中学习复杂衣物形变。为此，我们提出iTryOn框架，基于大规模视频扩散Transformer构建。iTryOn首创多级交互注入机制以引导复杂动态生成。空间层面，引入无服装依赖的3D手部先验，提供精细的手部-衣物接触引导，有效解决空间歧义；语义层面，利用全局描述提供整体上下文，结合时间戳动作描述实现局部交互，通过我们提出的动作感知旋转位置嵌入（A-RoPE）进行同步。大量实验表明，iTryOn不仅在传统VVT基准上达到最先进性能，更在新交互场景中大幅领先，标志着更动态可控的虚拟试穿体验的重要一步。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to simulate realistic human-garment interactions in video virtual try-on, the authors introduce Interactive Video Virtual Try-On (Interactive VVT), a new task that goes beyond static garment replacement by requiring models to handle dynamic, sparse, and semantically ambiguous interactions. They propose iTryOn, a diffusion-based Transformer framework that injects multi-level interaction guidance: spatially, via a 3D hand prior to resolve hand-garment contact; semantically, via global and time-stamped action captions guided by a novel Action-aware Rotational Position Embedding. Experiments show iTryOn outperforms prior methods on both traditional VVT benchmarks and the new interactive setting, demonstrating superior handling of garment deformation and interaction dynamics.
为解决现有视频虚拟试衣缺乏真实人衣互动的问题，作者提出交互式视频虚拟试衣（Interactive VVT）新任务，要求模型在稀疏且语义模糊的交互片段中生成复杂衣物变形。为此，他们设计了iTryOn框架，基于视频扩散Transformer，通过空间层面的3D手部先验解决手衣接触定位模糊，语义层面结合全局与时间戳动作描述，利用动作感知旋转位置嵌入（A-RoPE）实现精准引导。实验表明，iTryOn在传统VVT基准及新交互任务上均显著优于现有方法，实现了更自然、可控的动态衣物变形效果。
roto 2.0: The Robot Tactile Olympiad
Authors: Elle Miller, Jayaram Reddy, Ayush Deshmukh, Trevor McInroe, David Abel, Oisin Mac Aodha, Sethu Vijayakumar
Venue: ICRA 2026
First: 2026-05-20T17:22:33+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-20T17:22:33+00:00
Comments: Accepted to 7th ViTac Workshop, ICRA 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Tactile-based reinforcement learning (RL) is currently hindered by fragmented research and a focus on over-saturated orientation tasks. We introduce v2 of the Robot Tactile Olympiad (\texttt{roto 2.0}), a GPU-parallelised benchmark designed to standardise tactile-based RL across four distinct robotic morphologies (16-DOF to 24-DOF). Unlike prior benchmarks, roto focuses on end-to-end "blind" manipulation, utilising only proprioception and tactile sensing without state information or distillation. We demonstrate a significant performance leap, with our blind agents achieving 13 Baoding ball rotations in 10 seconds, an order of magnitude faster than current state-of-the-art speeds. By open-sourcing our environments and robustly tuned baselines, we reduce the barrier to entry and enable researchers to prioritise fundamental algorithmic challenges over tedious RL tuning. Website: https://elle-miller.github.io/roto/
中文标题/摘要
标题：roto 2.0：机器人触觉奥林匹克
基于触觉的强化学习（RL）目前受限于研究碎片化及对过度饱和的朝向任务的关注。我们引入机器人触觉奥林匹克v2（\texttt{roto 2.0}），这是一个GPU并行化的基准测试，旨在标准化四种不同机器人形态（16-DOF至24-DOF）上的触觉RL。与先前基准不同，roto专注于端到端的“盲”操作，仅使用本体感觉和触觉传感，不依赖状态信息或蒸馏。我们展示了显著性能提升，盲操作代理在10秒内完成13次保定球旋转，速度比当前最先进快一个数量级。通过开源环境及稳健调优的基线，我们降低了进入门槛，使研究人员能优先关注基础算法挑战而非繁琐的RL调参。网站：https://elle-miller.github.io/roto/
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the fragmentation and orientation bias in tactile reinforcement learning research, roto 2.0 introduces a GPU-parallelized benchmark that standardizes tactile-based RL across four robotic morphologies with 16- to 24-DOF. The method emphasizes end-to-end 'blind' manipulation, relying solely on proprioception and tactile sensing without external state or distillation. Experimental results show blind agents achieving 13 Baoding ball rotations in 10 seconds — ten times faster than prior state-of-the-art — demonstrating the efficacy of the benchmark and its ability to accelerate algorithmic progress.
为解决触觉强化学习研究碎片化及任务过饱和的问题，roto 2.0 引入了一个支持 GPU 并行的基准测试，旨在标准化 16 至 24 自由度四种机器人形态上的触觉强化学习。与以往基准不同，它强制执行仅依赖本体感觉和触觉传感的端到端“盲”操作，不使用外部状态信息或蒸馏。实验结果显示，盲操作智能体在 10 秒内完成 13 次保定球旋转，速度比当前最先进方法快一个数量级，验证了该基准在加速算法进步方面的有效性。
Evolutionary Generation of Multi-Agent Systems
Authors: Yuntong Hu, Yuting Zhang, Matthew Trager, Yi Zhang, Shuo Yang, Wei Xia, Stefano Soatto
Venue: ICML2026
First: 2026-02-06T09:01:35+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-20T17:21:50+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Large language model (LLM)-based multi-agent systems (MAS) show strong promise for complex reasoning, planning, and tool-augmented tasks, but designing effective MAS architectures remains labor-intensive, brittle, and hard to generalize. Existing automatic MAS generation methods either rely on code generation, which often leads to executability and robustness failures, or impose rigid architectural templates that limit expressiveness and adaptability. We propose Evolutionary Generation of Multi-Agent Systems (EvoMAS), which formulates MAS generation as structured configuration generation. EvoMAS performs evolutionary generation in configuration space. Specifically, EvoMAS selects initial configurations from a pool, applies feedback-conditioned mutation and crossover guided by execution traces, and iteratively refines both the candidate pool and an experience memory. We evaluate EvoMAS on diverse benchmarks, including BBEH, SWE-Bench, and WorkBench, covering reasoning, software engineering, and tool-use tasks. EvoMAS consistently improves task performance over both human-designed MAS and prior automatic MAS generation methods, while producing generated systems with higher executability and runtime robustness. EvoMAS outperforms the agent evolution method EvoAgent by +10.5 points on BBEH reasoning and +7.1 points on WorkBench. With Claude-4.5-Sonnet, EvoMAS also reaches 79.1% on SWE-Bench-Verified, matching the top of the leaderboard. Code is available at https://github.com/amazon-science/EvoMAS
中文标题/摘要
标题：多智能体系统的进化生成
基于大语言模型（LLM）的多智能体系统（MAS）在复杂推理、规划和工具增强任务中展现出强大潜力，但设计有效的MAS架构仍需大量人工、易脆弱且难以泛化。现有自动MAS生成方法要么依赖代码生成，常导致可执行性和鲁棒性失败，要么施加刚性架构模板，限制表达能力和适应性。我们提出多智能体系统的进化生成（EvoMAS），将MAS生成形式化为结构化配置生成。EvoMAS在配置空间中执行进化生成：从候选池中选择初始配置，依据执行轨迹引导的反馈条件变异和交叉操作，并迭代优化候选池与经验记忆。我们在涵盖推理、软件工程和工具使用任务的多样化基准（BBEH、SWE-Bench、WorkBench）上评估EvoMAS，结果表明其在任务性能上持续优于人工设计的MAS及现有自动MAS生成方法，同时生成系统具有更高的可执行性和运行时鲁棒性。EvoMAS在BBEH推理任务上比EvoAgent高出10.5分，在WorkBench上高出7.1分。使用Claude-4.5-Sonnet时，EvoMAS在SWE-Bench-Verified上达到79.1%，位列榜首。代码开源于https://github.com/amazon-science/EvoMAS
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to automate the design of effective multi-agent systems (MAS) without manual intervention, EvoMAS formulates MAS generation as structured configuration space evolution. It starts with a pool of initial configurations, then iteratively refines them via feedback-conditioned mutation and crossover guided by execution traces, while simultaneously updating a memory of past experiences. Evaluated on benchmarks like BBEH, SWE-Bench, and WorkBench, EvoMAS consistently outperforms both human-designed MAS and prior automated methods, achieving higher task success rates and runtime robustness. With Claude-4.5-Sonnet, it reaches 79.1% on SWE-Bench-Verified, matching the top leaderboard score, and beats EvoAgent by +10.5 on BBEH and +7.1 on WorkBench.
为解决多智能体系统（MAS）设计自动化难题，EvoMAS将MAS生成建模为结构化配置空间的进化优化问题。它从初始配置池出发，通过执行轨迹引导的变异与交叉操作持续迭代优化候选池与经验记忆。在BBEH、SWE-Bench和WorkBench等多个基准测试中，EvoMAS显著优于人工设计和现有自动化方法，展现出更高的可执行性与运行时鲁棒性。尤其在BBEH和WorkBench上分别超越EvoAgent 10.5和7.1分，且在Claude-4.5-Sonnet支持下于SWE-Bench-Verified达到79.1%，与排行榜榜首持平。
Polynomial-Time Robust Multiclass Linear Classification under Gaussian Marginals
Authors: Ilias Diakonikolas, Giannis Iakovidis, Mingchen Ma
First: 2026-05-20T17:19:30+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-20T17:19:30+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We study the task of agnostic learning of multiclass linear classifiers under the Gaussian distribution. Given labeled examples $(x, y)$ from a distribution over $\mathbb{R}^d \times [k]$, with Gaussian $x$-marginal, the goal is to output a hypothesis whose error is comparable to that of the best $k$-class linear classifier. While the binary case $k=2$ has a well-developed algorithmic theory, much less is known for $k \ge 3$. Even for $k=3$, prior robust algorithms incur exponential dependence on the inverse of the desired accuracy in both complexity and representation size. In this work, we develop new structural results for multiclass linear classifiers and use them to design fully polynomial-time robust learners with dimension-independent error guarantees. Our first result shows that the standard multiclass perceptron algorithm requires super-polynomially many samples and updates, even with clean labels and Gaussian marginals, revealing a basic obstruction absent in the binary case. Our main positive result is a pairwise improper-learning framework which yields an efficient learner with error $\widetilde O(k^{3/2}\sqrt{\mathrm{opt}})+ε$ for general $k$. Additionally, we develop a sharper localization-based framework which leads to error $O(\mathrm{opt})+ε$ for $k=3$, and error $\mathrm{poly}(k)\mathrm{opt}+ε$ for geometrically regular $k$-class linear classifiers.
中文标题/摘要
标题：高斯边缘下多项式时间鲁棒多类线性分类
我们研究在高斯分布下对多类线性分类器的无假设学习问题。给定从 $\mathbb{R}^d \times [k]$ 上分布中采样的带标签样本 $(x, y)$，其中 $x$ 具有高斯边缘分布，目标是输出一个假设，其误差与最优 $k$ 类线性分类器相当。虽然二分类情形 $k=2$ 已有成熟的算法理论，但 $k \ge 3$ 的情况了解甚少。即使对于 $k=3$，先前的鲁棒算法在复杂度和表示规模上均对所需精度的倒数呈指数依赖。在本工作中，我们为多类线性分类器建立了新的结构结果，并利用它们设计出具有维度无关误差保证的完全多项式时间鲁棒学习算法。我们的第一个结果表明，即使在标签纯净且边缘为高斯分布的情况下，标准多类感知机算法仍需超多项式数量的样本和更新，揭示了二分类情形中不存在的基本障碍。我们的主要积极成果是一种成对非正则学习框架，可为任意 $k$ 输出误差为 $\widetilde O(k^{3/2}\sqrt{\mathrm{opt}})+ε$ 的高效学习器。此外，我们还开发了一种更精细的定位框架，对于 $k=3$ 可实现误差 $O(\mathrm{opt})+ε$，对于几何正则的 $k$ 类线性分类器可实现误差 $\mathrm{poly}(k)\mathrm{opt}+ε$。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of efficient robust learning algorithms for multiclass linear classification under Gaussian marginals, especially for k≥3, this work introduces structural insights into multiclass linear classifiers and leverages them to design polynomial-time learners with dimension-independent error guarantees. The authors first show that the standard multiclass perceptron requires super-polynomial samples even with clean labels, highlighting a fundamental barrier. Their main contribution is a pairwise improper learning framework achieving error \widetilde O(k^{3/2}\sqrt{\mathrm{opt}})+ε for general k, and a sharper localization-based method yielding O(\mathrm{opt})+ε for k=3 and poly(k)\mathrm{opt}+ε for geometrically regular classifiers, overcoming prior exponential dependencies on accuracy.
为解决高维高斯边际下多类线性分类器的鲁棒学习难题（尤其k≥3时缺乏高效算法），本文通过揭示多类线性分类器的结构特性，设计了多项式时间学习算法并实现与维度无关的误差保证。研究发现标准多类感知机即使在干净标签下也需超多项式样本，揭示了二分类不存在的障碍。主要成果是提出一种成对非正则学习框架，对一般k类实现误差O(k^{3/2}√opt)+ε；并进一步发展局部化框架，对k=3实现O(opt)+ε，对几何正则k类实现poly(k)opt+ε，显著优于此前指数依赖精度的算法。
PALS: Power-Aware LLM Serving for Mixture-of-Experts Models
Authors: Can Hankendi, Rana Shahout, Minlan Yu, Ayse K. Coskun
First: 2026-05-20T17:19:20+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-20T17:19:20+00:00
Comments: 13 pages, 10 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language model (LLM) inference has become a dominant workload in modern data centers, driving significant GPU utilization and energy consumption. While prior systems optimize throughput and latency by batching, scheduling, and parallelism, they largely treat GPU power as a static constraint rather than a controllable resource. In this paper, we present a power-aware runtime for LLM serving, PALS, that treats GPU power caps as a first-class control knob and jointly optimizes them with software parameters such as batch size. The system combines lightweight offline power-performance models with a feedback-driven controller to select configurations that satisfy throughput targets while maximizing energy efficiency. We implement PALS within an existing LLM serving framework, vLLM, demonstrating that it requires no model retraining or API changes. Across multi-GPU systems and both dense and mixture-of-experts (MoE) models, PALS improves energy efficiency by up to 26.3%, reduces QoS violations by 4x to 7x under power constraints, and tracks dynamic power budgets. These results highlight the potential of integrating power control directly into LLM inference runtimes, enabling energy-proportional and grid-interactive AI systems.
中文标题/摘要
标题：PALS：面向专家混合模型的节能大语言模型服务系统
大语言模型（LLM）推理已成为现代数据中心的主要工作负载，显著推动了GPU利用率和能耗。尽管现有系统通过批处理、调度和并行化优化吞吐量和延迟，但它们大多将GPU功耗视为静态约束，而非可控资源。本文提出了一种面向LLM服务的节能运行时系统PALS，将GPU功耗上限视为一级控制参数，并与批大小等软件参数联合优化。该系统结合轻量级离线功耗-性能模型与反馈驱动控制器，选择满足吞吐量目标的同时最大化能效的配置。我们在现有LLM服务框架vLLM中实现PALS，无需模型重训练或API变更。在多GPU系统及稠密与专家混合（MoE）模型中，PALS能提升能效最高达26.3%，在功耗约束下将服务质量违规降低4至7倍，并支持动态功耗预算跟踪。这些结果凸显了将功耗控制直接集成到LLM推理运行时中的潜力，从而实现能耗比例化和电网互动的AI系统。
Summary / 总结
Driven by the high energy costs of LLM inference in data centers, this paper introduces PALS, a power-aware runtime that treats GPU power caps as a tunable parameter alongside batch size and other software controls. PALS combines lightweight offline power-performance models with a feedback-driven controller to dynamically select configurations that meet throughput targets while maximizing energy efficiency. Evaluated within the vLLM framework without requiring model retraining or API changes, PALS achieves up to 26.3% higher energy efficiency, reduces QoS violations by 4x to 7x under power constraints, and adapts to dynamic power budgets across multi-GPU systems and both dense and MoE models.
为应对大型语言模型推理带来的高能耗问题，本文提出PALS系统，将GPU功耗上限视为可调参数，与批大小等软件参数协同优化。通过结合离线功耗-性能模型与反馈控制机制，PALS动态选择配置以满足吞吐量目标并最大化能效。在vLLM框架内实现无需模型重训练或API修改，实测在多GPU系统及密集型与专家混合模型中，能效提升最高达26.3%，在功耗约束下QoS违规减少4至7倍，并能跟踪动态功耗预算。
Adaptive Signal Resuscitation: Channel-wise Post-Pruning Repair for Sparse Vision Networks
Authors: Qishi Zhan, Ziheng Chen, Minxuan Hu
First: 2026-05-20T17:19:01+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-20T17:19:01+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
One-shot magnitude pruning can cause severe accuracy collapse in the high-sparsity regime, even when the pruning mask preserves the largest weights. We argue that this failure reflects a granularity mismatch in post-pruning repair. Under global magnitude pruning, nearly collapsed channels can coexist with channels that retain informative activation variance within the same layer. Existing layer-wise activation repair methods apply a single correction to the whole layer, and can therefore over-amplify damaged channels while trying to restore the layer-level signal. We propose Adaptive Signal Resuscitation (ASR), a training-free channel-wise repair method that matches the granularity of repair to the granularity of damage. ASR estimates a variance-matching correction for each output channel and stabilizes it with a data-driven shrinkage rule, suppressing unreliable corrections for channels with weak post-pruning signal while preserving corrections for healthier channels. Applied before BatchNorm recalibration, ASR requires only forward passes on a small calibration set and no retraining. Across three datasets, four convolutional architectures, and both unstructured and structured sparsity settings, ASR generally improves over layer-wise repair, with the clearest gains in high-sparsity regimes. On ResNet-50 at 90% sparsity, ASR recovers 55.6% top-1 accuracy on CIFAR-10, compared with 41.0% for layer-wise repair and 28.0% for BatchNorm-only recalibration. Ablations show that naive channel-wise variance matching is insufficient, and that shrinkage stabilizes post-pruning repair.
中文标题/摘要
标题：自适应信号复苏：面向稀疏视觉网络的逐通道后剪枝修复
单次幅度剪枝在高稀疏度下即使保留最大权重仍会导致严重精度崩溃，我们归因于后剪枝修复的粒度不匹配。在全局幅度剪枝下，同一层内几乎崩溃的通道可与保留信息性激活方差的通道共存。现有逐层激活修复方法对整层施加单一修正，因而可能过度放大受损通道以恢复层级信号。我们提出自适应信号复苏（ASR），一种无需训练的逐通道修复方法，其修复粒度与损伤粒度匹配。ASR为每个输出通道估计方差匹配修正，并通过数据驱动的收缩规则稳定修正，抑制弱后剪枝信号通道的不可靠修正，同时保留健康通道的修正。ASR在BatchNorm重校准前应用，仅需在小型校准集上进行前向传播，无需重训练。在三个数据集、四种卷积架构及无结构与结构化稀疏设置下，ASR普遍优于逐层修复，尤其在高稀疏度下收益显著。在ResNet-50 90%稀疏度下，ASR在CIFAR-10上恢复55.6% top-1精度，而逐层修复仅41.0%，BatchNorm仅重校准为28.0%。消融实验表明，朴素逐通道方差匹配不足，而收缩机制稳定了后剪枝修复。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observation that one-shot magnitude pruning often causes accuracy collapse even when preserving large weights, the authors argue that existing layer-wise repair methods fail due to mismatched granularity. They propose Adaptive Signal Resuscitation (ASR), a training-free, channel-wise repair method that estimates variance-matching corrections per output channel and stabilizes them using a data-driven shrinkage rule to suppress noisy corrections. Applied before BatchNorm recalibration, ASR requires only forward passes on a small calibration set. Experiments across three datasets, four architectures, and both sparsity types show ASR consistently outperforms layer-wise repair, especially at high sparsity; on ResNet-50 at 90% sparsity, ASR achieves 55.6% top-1 accuracy on CIFAR-10, compared to 41.0% for layer-wise repair and 28.0% for BatchNorm-only recalibration.
由于单次幅度剪枝即使保留大权重仍常导致精度崩溃，作者认为现有层级激活修复方法因修复粒度与损伤粒度不匹配而失效。他们提出无训练的通道级修复方法Adaptive Signal Resuscitation（ASR），为每个输出通道估计方差匹配的修正值，并通过数据驱动的收缩规则稳定修正，抑制信号弱通道的噪声修正。ASR在BatchNorm重校准前应用，仅需在小校准集上进行前向传播。实验表明，ASR在多种架构和稀疏度下均优于层级修复，尤其在高稀疏度下效果显著；在ResNet-50 90%稀疏度下，ASR在CIFAR-10上达到55.6%的top-1准确率，远超层级修复的41.0%和仅BatchNorm重校准的28.0%。
Block-Sparse Global Attention for Efficient Multi-View Geometry Transformers
Authors: Chung-Shien Brian Wang, Christian Schmidt, Jens Piekenbrinck, Bastian Leibe
First: 2025-09-08T18:16:09+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-20T17:17:47+00:00
Comments: Project page at https://vision.rwth-aachen.de/sparse-vggt
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Efficient and accurate feed-forward multi-view reconstruction has long been an important task in computer vision. Recent transformer-based models like VGGT, $π^3$ and MapAnything have demonstrated remarkable performance with relatively simple architectures. However, their scalability is fundamentally constrained by the quadratic complexity of global attention, which imposes a significant runtime bottleneck when processing large image sets. In this work, we empirically analyze the global attention matrix of these models and observe that the probability mass concentrates on a small subset of patch-patch interactions corresponding to cross-view geometric correspondences. Building on this insight and inspired by recent advances in large language models, we propose a training-free, block-sparse replacement for dense global attention, implemented with highly optimized kernels. Our method accelerates inference by more than $3\times$ while maintaining comparable task performance. Evaluations on a comprehensive suite of multi-view benchmarks demonstrate that our approach seamlessly integrates into existing global attention-based architectures such as VGGT, $π^3$ , and MapAnything, while substantially improving scalability to large image collections.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于高效多视角几何变换器的块稀疏全局注意力
高效的前馈多视角重建长期以来一直是计算机视觉的重要任务。近期基于变换器的模型如VGGT、$π^3$ 和MapAnything在相对简单的架构下展现了卓越性能。然而，其可扩展性从根本上受限于全局注意力的二次复杂度，当处理大量图像时会带来显著的运行时瓶颈。在本研究中，我们实证分析了这些模型的全局注意力矩阵，发现概率质量集中在对应跨视角几何对应关系的一小部分块-块交互上。基于这一洞察并借鉴大型语言模型的最新进展，我们提出了一种无需训练的块稀疏全局注意力替代方案，采用高度优化的内核实现。该方法在保持相近任务性能的同时，推理速度提升超过3倍。在全面的多视角基准测试中，我们的方法无缝集成到现有基于全局注意力的架构（如VGGT、$π^3$ 和MapAnything）中，显著提升了对大规模图像集合的可扩展性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the quadratic runtime bottleneck of global attention in transformer-based multi-view reconstruction models, this work identifies that global attention matrices concentrate probability mass on a small set of geometrically meaningful patch interactions. It proposes a training-free, block-sparse attention mechanism inspired by large language models, implemented with optimized kernels to replace dense attention. Experimental results show the method accelerates inference by over three times while preserving performance, and integrates seamlessly into architectures like VGGT, π³, and MapAnything, significantly improving scalability for large image collections.
为解决多视角几何Transformer中密集全局注意力的计算瓶颈，该研究发现全局注意力矩阵的概率质量集中在少数几何相关的patch交互上。基于此，提出一种无需训练的块稀疏注意力机制，受大语言模型启发并采用优化内核替换密集注意力。实验表明该方法推理速度提升超三倍，性能保持相当，且可无缝集成至VGGT、π³、MapAnything等架构，显著提升对大规模图像集的扩展能力。
Preference-aware Influence-function-based Data Selection Method for Efficient Fine-Tuning
Authors: Qihao Lin, Guanxu Chen, Dongrui Liu, Jing Shao
First: 2026-05-20T17:15:43+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-20T17:15:43+00:00
Comments: 13 pages, 3 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
As LLMs continue to scale, improving training efficiency increasingly depends on using data more effectively. Data selection addresses this problem by allocating a limited training budget to samples that best promote a target behavior. Existing methods usually represent the target behavior with a set of target examples, but often treat these examples as equally important. This can be inefficient because target examples may differ in their relevance to the current model: examples closer to the model's current behavior provide more actionable guidance than those farther away. We propose PRISM (PReference-aware Influence-function-based Data Selection Method for Efficient Fine-Tuning), which uses the current model's preference to weight target examples and construct a preference-aware target representation. PRISM then scores candidate training samples by their alignment with this representation, concentrating the data budget on samples more likely to move the model toward the target behavior. Theoretical analysis shows that this preference weighting yields a more effective first-order direction for increasing target-behavior preference. Experiments across model families and scales show that PRISM improves both efficient fine-tuning and safety-oriented SFT repair, demonstrating that precise target-behavior characterization is key to budget-efficient data selection.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于偏好感知的影响力函数数据选择方法，用于高效微调
随着大语言模型持续扩展，提升训练效率越来越依赖于更有效地使用数据。数据选择通过将有限的训练预算分配给最能促进目标行为的样本来解决这一问题。现有方法通常用一组目标示例表示目标行为，但往往将这些示例视为同等重要，这可能效率低下，因为目标示例对当前模型的相关性可能不同：与当前模型行为更接近的示例提供更可操作的指导，而距离较远的示例则不然。我们提出PRISM（偏好感知的影响力函数数据选择方法，用于高效微调），该方法利用当前模型的偏好对目标示例进行加权，构建偏好感知的目标表示。PRISM随后根据候选训练样本与该表示的对齐程度进行评分，将数据预算集中于更可能推动模型向目标行为发展的样本上。理论分析表明，这种偏好加权能产生更有效的首阶方向，以提升目标行为偏好。在不同模型家族和规模上的实验表明，PRISM同时提升了高效微调和以安全为导向的SFT修复效果，证明了精确刻画目标行为是预算高效数据选择的关键。
Summary / 总结
As large language models scale, efficient fine-tuning requires selecting training data that maximally aligns with target behavior, rather than treating all target examples equally. PRISM addresses this by weighting target examples based on their alignment with the current model’s preferences, constructing a preference-aware target representation. It then scores candidate samples by their alignment with this representation, prioritizing those most likely to shift the model toward the target. Experiments across model families and scales show PRISM improves both efficient fine-tuning and safety-oriented SFT repair, confirming that precise target characterization enhances budget efficiency.
随着大语言模型规模扩大，高效微调需选择最能提升目标行为的数据，而非平等对待所有目标示例。PRISM通过根据当前模型偏好加权目标示例，构建偏好感知的目标表示，并据此评估候选样本的对齐程度，优先选择最可能引导模型向目标行为靠拢的数据。在多模型规模和家族的实验中，PRISM在高效微调与安全导向的SFT修复中均优于基线方法，验证了精准目标行为表征对预算效率的关键作用。
AIGaitor: Privacy-preserving and cloud-free motion analysis for everyone, using edge computing
Authors: Lauhitya Reddy, Trisha M. Kesar, Hyeokhyen Kwon
First: 2026-05-20T17:14:57+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-20T17:14:57+00:00
Comments: 18 pages 3 figures, 2 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Motion capture is the gold standard for measuring human movement, but clinical use remains limited by cost, technical complexity, and privacy concerns. AIGaitor is a privacy-preserving, cloud-free motion analysis system that runs markerless monocular motion-capture pipelines and downstream deep-learning analysis entirely on a consumer smartphone using on-device neural accelerators. To motivate its design, we surveyed 74 rehabilitation clinicians: 92 percent said they would adopt an accurate, cost-effective, easy-to-use AI gait analysis tool, while 79.7 percent cited operating cost, 68.9 percent insufficient training, and 64.9 percent privacy concerns as leading barriers. We then optimized and benchmarked mobile iOS implementations of current monocular pipeline components, including 2D and 3D pose estimation, pose optimization, skeleton-based deep-learning analysis, and a vision-language model. A Time-Priority end-to-end on-device pipeline processes a 10 s 4K 60 fps video clip in 77 s on an iPhone 14, matching or beating the same pipeline on a high-end NVIDIA H200 cloud server when network transfer is included: 94 s at global mobile-average uplink and 66 s at developed-world Wi-Fi. Lightweight models such as ViTPose-s achieve real-time keypoint extraction, and skeleton-based action-recognition models provide sub-millisecond gait classification on the same clip. To our knowledge, AIGaitor is the first monocular system to demonstrate end-to-end on-device motion capture and downstream deep-learning analysis, supporting clinically applicable movement analysis that is low-cost, private, and accessible to smartphone users.
中文标题/摘要
标题：AIGaitor：面向大众的隐私保护、无需云端的运动分析系统，采用边缘计算
动作捕捉是测量人体运动的金标准，但临床应用受限于成本、技术复杂性和隐私问题。AIGaitor 是一套隐私保护、无需云端的运动分析系统，可在消费级智能手机上利用设备端神经加速器，完全运行无标记单目动作捕捉管线及下游深度学习分析。为指导其设计，我们调研了74名康复临床医生：92%表示愿意采用准确、经济、易用的AI步态分析工具，而79.7%认为运营成本、68.9%认为培训不足、64.9%认为隐私问题为主要障碍。随后，我们优化并基准测试了当前单目管线组件在移动iOS平台上的实现，包括2D和3D姿态估计、姿态优化、基于骨架的深度学习分析及视觉-语言模型。Time-Priority端到端设备端管线可在iPhone 14上77秒处理10秒4K 60fps视频片段，当包含网络传输时，其性能与高端NVIDIA H200云端服务器相当：全球移动平均上行链路速度下耗时94秒，发达国家Wi-Fi环境下耗时66秒。轻量级模型如ViTPose-s可实现实时关键点提取，基于骨架的动作识别模型可在同一片段上实现亚毫秒级步态分类。据我们所知，AIGaitor是首个实现端到端设备端动作捕捉及下游深度学习分析的单目系统，支持低成本、隐私保护且面向智能手机用户的临床适用性运动分析。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by clinicians' expressed need for affordable, privacy-respecting, and easy-to-use gait analysis tools, AIGaitor enables markerless motion capture and deep-learning analysis entirely on consumer smartphones using edge computing. The system integrates optimized 2D/3D pose estimation, pose optimization, and skeleton-based models running on-device neural accelerators, achieving real-time performance. Experimental results show that a 10-second 4K video clip is processed in 77 seconds on an iPhone 14, outperforming cloud-based equivalents when network latency is factored in, and lightweight models enable sub-millisecond gait classification.
为满足康复临床医生对低成本、隐私保护且易用的步态分析工具的需求，AIGaitor在消费级智能手机上通过边缘计算实现无标记单目运动捕捉与深度学习分析。系统集成优化的2D/3D姿态估计、姿态优化及骨架模型，利用设备端神经加速器运行，实现实时性能。实验表明，10秒4K视频在iPhone 14上仅需77秒处理，优于含网络延迟的云端方案，轻量模型可实现毫秒级步态分类。
HiRes: Inspectable Precedent Memory for Reaction Condition Recommendation
Authors: Shreyas Vinaya Sathyanarayana, Raja Sekhar Pappala, Deepak Warrier
First: 2026-05-20T17:14:46+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-20T17:14:46+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Reaction condition recommendation sits immediately after retrosynthetic disconnection selection, and in practice, chemists require both accurate predictions and the precedents that justify them. We present HiRes (Hierarchical Reaction Representations), a retrieval-augmented condition recommendation system whose learned reaction space serves as both a classifier feature and an inspectable precedent memory. The model combines a graph encoder, transformation-aware cross-attention, multi-stream reaction fusion, and a k-NN retrieval layer. HiRes achieves state-of-the-art performance among primary-slot USPTO-Condition models, reaching Catalyst, Solvent, and Reagent top-1 accuracies (Acc@1) of 0.929, 0.534, and 0.530 respectively. It ties the best reported baseline on Catalyst while outperforming models such as REACON on Solvent and Reagent. Furthermore, paired bootstrap analysis demonstrates that integrating retrieval with learned condition heads provides statistically significant gains for solvent and reagent selection over purely parametric approaches. Ultimately, HiRes bridges the gap between predictive accuracy and chemical interpretability, offering a single representation that supplies both competitive recommendations and the concrete chemical precedents necessary for practical synthesis planning.

标题：HiRes：用于反应条件推荐的可检视先例记忆

Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for chemists to receive both accurate reaction condition predictions and interpretable precedents, HiRes introduces a retrieval-augmented system that uses a learned reaction space as both a classifier feature and an inspectable memory of chemical precedents. 为满足化学家对准确预测与可解释先例的双重需求，HiRes提出一种基于检索增强的反应条件推荐系统，其学习到的反应空间同时作为分类特征和可检视的化学先例记忆。该方法结合图编码器、变换感知交叉注意力、多流反应融合与k-NN检索层，使推荐结果根植于真实化学实例。实验表明，HiRes在USPTO-Condition数据集上达到最先进水平，催化剂、溶剂、试剂的Top-1准确率分别为0.929、0.534、0.530，优于REACON等基线模型，并通过Bootstrap分析证实，当检索与学习头结合时，溶剂与试剂选择在统计上显著优于纯参数化方法。
FedCritic: Serverless Federated Critic Learning-based Resource Allocation for Multi-Cell OFDMA in 6G
Authors: Amin Farajzadeh, Melike Erol-Kantarci
First: 2026-05-20T17:13:09+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-20T17:13:09+00:00
Comments: Submitted to IEEE for possible publication
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
In sixth-generation (6G) ultra-dense networks, aggressive frequency reuse amplifies inter-cell interference (ICI), making multi-cell orthogonal frequency-division multiple access (OFDMA) scheduling and power control strongly coupled across neighboring cells. We study distributed downlink resource management -- joint subcarrier scheduling and power allocation -- under interference coupling and long-term per-user quality-of-service (QoS) minimum-rate constraints. By using virtual-queue deficit weights to enforce long-term QoS, we develop FedCritic, a serverless federated multi-agent actor-critic framework with decentralized execution. Unlike centralized training with decentralized execution (CTDE) approaches that require centralized critic learning and joint trajectory aggregation, FedCritic federates the critic through lightweight gossip-based parameter averaging over the interference graph, enabling stable value estimation without a central coordinator while keeping policies local. Simulations in an interference-rich reuse-1 setting show that FedCritic improves mean signal-to-interference-plus-noise ratio (SINR) and cell-edge rate, increases network-wide average sum-rate and fairness relative to non-coordinated and CTDE baselines, and achieves more stable training with lower coordination overhead.
中文标题/摘要
标题：FedCritic：面向6G多小区OFDMA的基于无服务器联邦批评学习的资源分配
在第六代（6G）超密集网络中，激进的频率复用加剧了小区间干扰（ICI），使得多小区正交频分多址（OFDMA）调度与功率控制在相邻小区间强耦合。本文研究在干扰耦合和长期用户服务质量（QoS）最小速率约束下分布式下行资源管理——联合子载波调度与功率分配。通过使用虚拟队列亏空权重强制长期QoS，我们提出FedCritic，一种无服务器联邦多智能体演员-批评框架，支持去中心化执行。与需要集中式批评学习和联合轨迹聚合的集中训练去中心化执行（CTDE）方法不同，FedCritic通过干扰图上的轻量级 gossip 基于参数平均联邦批评，无需中央协调器即可实现稳定的价值估计，同时保持策略本地化。在干扰丰富的复用1场景仿真中，FedCritic提升了平均信干噪比（SINR）和小区边缘速率，提高了网络平均总吞吐量和公平性，相比非协调和CTDE基线方法，训练更稳定且协调开销更低。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the severe inter-cell interference in 6G ultra-dense networks, this work proposes FedCritic, a serverless federated actor-critic framework for joint subcarrier scheduling and power allocation in multi-cell OFDMA systems. It enforces long-term QoS via virtual-queue deficit weights and decentralizes critic learning through lightweight gossip-based parameter averaging over the interference graph, eliminating the need for a central coordinator. Experimental results in a reuse-1 interference-rich setting demonstrate that FedCritic outperforms non-coordinated and CTDE baselines by improving mean SINR, cell-edge rate, network-wide sum-rate, and fairness, while achieving more stable training with reduced coordination overhead.
为应对6G超密集网络中严重的小区间干扰，本文提出FedCritic框架，用于多小区OFDMA系统中的联合子载波调度与功率分配。该框架通过虚拟队列亏空权重保障长期服务质量，并在干扰图上采用轻量级 gossip 参数平均实现去中心化批评学习，无需中央协调器。仿真结果表明，FedCritic在重用因子为1的干扰密集场景下，显著提升平均SINR与边缘用户速率，提高网络总吞吐量与公平性，且训练更稳定、协调开销更低，优于非协调及CTDE基线方法。
Ordering Matters: Rank-Aware Selective Fusion for Blended Emotion Recognition
Authors: Junghyun Lee, Hyunseo Kim, Hanna Jang, Junhyug Noh
First: 2026-05-20T17:12:55+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-20T17:12:55+00:00
Comments: Accepted at IEEE FG 2026. Final system ranked 2nd in the BlEmoRE Challenge. 9 pages including appendix, 8 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Blended emotion recognition is challenging because emotions are often expressed as mixtures of subtle and overlapping multimodal cues rather than a single dominant signal. We propose a rank-aware multi-encoder framework that selectively combines complementary representations from diverse pre-extracted video and audio encoders. Our method projects heterogeneous encoder features into a shared latent space, estimates sample-wise encoder importance through an attention-based gating module, and fuses only the top-n most informative encoders. To better model blended emotions, we decouple prediction into presence and salience heads and align them through probability-level fusion. We further incorporate feature-level unsupervised domain adaptation without pseudo-labeling to improve robustness under distribution shift. Experiments on the BlEmoRE challenge show that the proposed framework outperforms strong individual encoders and naïve multi-encoder fusion baselines. Our final system ranked 2nd in the competition, supporting the effectiveness of rank-aware selective fusion for fine-grained blended emotion recognition.
中文标题/摘要
标题：顺序至关重要：面向混合情绪识别的排序感知选择性融合
混合情绪识别具有挑战性，因为情绪常表现为微妙且重叠的多模态线索的混合，而非单一主导信号。我们提出一种排序感知的多编码器框架，选择性地融合来自不同预提取视频和音频编码器的互补表示。该方法将异构编码器特征投影到共享潜在空间，通过基于注意力的门控模块估计样本级编码器重要性，并仅融合最具信息量的前n个编码器。为更好地建模混合情绪，我们将预测解耦为存在性和显著性头，并通过概率级融合对齐。此外，我们引入无伪标签的特征级无监督域自适应以提升分布偏移下的鲁棒性。在BlEmoRE挑战赛上的实验表明，所提框架优于强个体编码器和朴素多编码器融合基线。最终系统在竞赛中排名第二，验证了排序感知选择性融合在细粒度混合情绪识别中的有效性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of recognizing blended emotions that arise from overlapping and subtle multimodal cues, the paper introduces a rank-aware multi-encoder framework that selectively fuses the most informative representations from diverse pre-trained video and audio encoders. The method projects heterogeneous features into a shared latent space, uses an attention-based gating module to estimate encoder-wise importance per sample, and retains only the top-n most relevant encoders for fusion. To better capture blended emotion structure, it decouples prediction into presence and salience components, aligning them via probability-level fusion, and adds unsupervised domain adaptation without pseudo-labeling to enhance robustness. Experiments on the BlEmoRE challenge demonstrate that the framework outperforms individual encoders and naive fusion baselines, achieving 2nd place in the competition.
为应对情绪常以多模态混合形式表达的挑战，本文提出一种基于排序感知的选择性融合框架，从多种视频与音频编码器中选择性地融合互补表征。该方法将异构编码器特征投影至共享潜在空间，通过注意力门控机制评估每个样本的编码器重要性，并仅融合排名前n的最具信息量编码器。为更准确建模混合情绪，模型将预测解耦为存在性与显著性两个头并进行概率级对齐，同时引入无伪标签的特征级无监督域自适应以增强鲁棒性。在BlEmoRE挑战赛上的实验表明，该方法优于单个编码器及朴素多编码器融合基线，最终排名第二。
SHINE: A Scalable In-Context Hypernetwork for Mapping Context to LoRA in a Single Pass
Authors: Yewei Liu, Xiyuan Wang, Yansheng Mao, Yoav Gelbery, Haggai Maron, Muhan Zhang
First: 2026-02-06T03:40:31+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-20T17:11:17+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
We propose SHINE (Scalable Hyper In-context NEtwork), a scalable hypernetwork that can map diverse meaningful contexts into high-quality LoRA adapters for large language models (LLMs). By reusing the frozen LLM's own parameters in an in-context hypernetwork design and introducing architectural innovations, SHINE overcomes key limitations of prior hypernetworks and achieves strong expressive power with a relatively small number of parameters. We introduce a pretraining and instruction fine-tuning pipeline, and train our hypernetwork to generate high quality LoRA adapters from diverse meaningful contexts in a single forward pass. It updates LLM parameters without any fine-tuning, and immediately enables complex question answering tasks related to the context without directly accessing the context, effectively transforming in-context knowledge to in-parameter knowledge in one pass. Our work achieves outstanding results on various tasks, greatly saves time, computation and memory costs compared to SFT-based LLM adaptation, and shows great potential for scaling. Our code is available at https://github.com/MuLabPKU/SHINE
中文标题/摘要
标题：SHINE：一种可扩展的上下文超网络，用于单次传递将上下文映射为LoRA
我们提出了SHINE（可扩展的上下文超网络），这是一种可扩展的超网络，能够将多样且有意义的上下文映射为大型语言模型（LLM）的高质量LoRA适配器。通过在上下文超网络设计中复用冻结LLM自身的参数并引入架构创新，SHINE克服了先前超网络的关键局限性，在参数数量相对较少的情况下实现了强大的表达能力。我们引入了预训练与指令微调流程，使超网络能够在单次前向传递中从多样且有意义的上下文中生成高质量LoRA适配器。该方法无需任何微调即可更新LLM参数，并能立即支持与上下文相关的复杂问答任务，而无需直接访问上下文，有效将上下文知识在单次传递中转化为参数知识。我们的工作在各类任务上取得了优异结果，相比基于SFT的LLM适配方法大幅节省了时间、计算和内存开销，并展现出良好的可扩展潜力。代码已开源：https://github.com/MuLabPKU/SHINE
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for efficient, context-driven adaptation of large language models without fine-tuning, SHINE introduces a scalable in-context hypernetwork that maps diverse contexts into high-quality LoRA adapters in a single forward pass. By leveraging the frozen LLM’s parameters and introducing architectural innovations, it avoids the computational overhead of traditional fine-tuning while achieving strong expressive power with minimal parameters. Experimental results demonstrate that SHINE effectively transforms contextual knowledge into parameterized knowledge, enabling complex question answering without direct context access, and outperforms SFT-based methods in efficiency and scalability across multiple tasks.
为实现无需微调的大语言模型高效上下文适配，SHINE提出一种可扩展的上下文内超网络，能在单次前向传播中将多样上下文映射为高质量LoRA适配器。通过复用冻结LLM参数并引入架构创新，该方法避免传统微调的计算开销，同时以少量参数实现强大表达能力。实验表明，SHINE能从不同上下文生成有效适配器，无需直接访问上下文即可支持复杂问答任务，显著降低与监督微调相比的时间、内存与计算成本。
PointACT: Vision-Language-Action Models with Multi-Scale Point-Action Interaction
Authors: Shizhe Chen, Paul Pacaud, Cordelia Schmid
Venue: RSS 2026
First: 2026-05-20T17:10:31+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-20T17:10:31+00:00
Comments: Accepted to RSS 2026; project webpage: https://cshizhe.github.io/projects/pointact.html
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have shown strong potential for general-purpose robotic manipulation by leveraging large pretrained vision-language backbones. However, most existing VLAs rely primarily on 2D visual representations, which limit their ability to reason about fine-grained geometry and spatial grounding - capabilities that are essential for precise and robust manipulation in 3D environments. In this paper, we propose PointACT, a dual-system 3D-aware VLA policy that integrates hierarchical 3D point cloud representations directly into the action decoding process. PointACT employs a multi-scale point-action interaction mechanism with efficient bottleneck window self-attention, enabling evolving action tokens to densely attend to both local geometric detail and global scene structure. We evaluate PointACT on the LIBERO and RLBench benchmarks and systematically compare it against monolithic and dual-system VLA baselines, including variants augmented with point cloud inputs. PointACT achieves consistent improvements across both benchmarks, increasing success rates by 10% on the challenging RLBench-10Tasks suite over state-of-the-art pretrained VLAs, with even larger gains when the vision-language backbone is frozen and the action expert is trained from scratch. Extensive ablation studies demonstrate that tightly coupling hierarchical 3D geometry with pretrained 2D semantic representations is critical for robust and spatially grounded robot control. Our results also highlight the promise of pretrained 3D representations for 3D-aware VLA policies.
中文标题/摘要
标题：PointACT：基于多尺度点-动作交互的视觉-语言-动作模型
视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型通过利用大规模预训练的视觉-语言骨干网络，在通用机器人操作中展现出强大潜力。然而，现有大多数VLA模型主要依赖2D视觉表示，限制了其对细粒度几何结构和空间定位的推理能力——这些能力对于在3D环境中实现精确且鲁棒的操作至关重要。本文提出PointACT，一种双系统3D感知VLA策略，将分层3D点云表示直接集成到动作解码过程中。PointACT采用多尺度点-动作交互机制，结合高效的瓶颈窗口自注意力，使动作令牌能够密集关注局部几何细节与全局场景结构。我们在LIBERO和RLBench基准上评估PointACT，并系统性地与单系统和双系统VLA基线（包括增强点云输入的变体）进行比较。PointACT在两个基准上均取得一致提升，在具有挑战性的RLBench-10Tasks套件中，相比最先进的预训练VLA模型成功率提升10%；当视觉-语言骨干网络冻结、动作专家从零训练时，提升幅度更大。大量消融研究表明，紧密耦合分层3D几何与预训练2D语义表示对实现鲁棒且空间定位的机器人控制至关重要。我们的结果也凸显了预训练3D表示在3D感知VLA策略中的潜力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of 2D-based vision-language-action models in handling fine-grained 3D geometry for robotic manipulation, PointACT introduces a dual-system architecture that directly integrates hierarchical 3D point cloud representations into action decoding via a multi-scale point-action interaction mechanism using bottleneck window self-attention. This enables action tokens to attend to both local geometric detail and global scene structure. Evaluated on LIBERO and RLBench, PointACT outperforms existing monolithic and dual-system baselines, achieving a 10% increase in success rate on RLBench-10Tasks over state-of-the-art pretrained VLAs, with even greater gains when the vision-language backbone is frozen. Ablation studies confirm that tightly coupling 3D geometry with 2D semantics is essential for spatially grounded robotic control.
为解决现有基于2D视觉的视觉-语言-动作模型在处理精细3D几何结构时的不足，PointACT提出一种双系统架构，通过多尺度点-动作交互机制（采用高效瓶颈窗口自注意力）将分层3D点云表示直接融入动作解码过程，使动作令牌能同时关注局部几何细节与全局场景结构。在LIBERO和RLBench基准测试中，PointACT显著优于现有单系统与双系统基线模型，在挑战性RLBench-10Tasks任务上比当前最优预训练VLA模型成功率提升10%，冻结视觉-语言骨干网络并从头训练动作专家时提升更明显。消融实验表明，紧密耦合3D几何与2D语义信息对实现空间感知的机器人控制至关重要。
Teaching AI Through Benchmark Construction: QuestBench as a Course-Based Practice for Accountable Knowledge Work
Authors: Haiyang Shen, Jiuzheng Wang, Taian Guo, Mugeng Liu, Wenchun Jing, Chongyang Pan, Siqi Zhong, Zhiyang Chen, Weichen Bi, Yudong Han, Xiaoying Bai, Yun Ma
First: 2026-05-20T17:09:56+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-20T17:09:56+00:00
Comments: 24 pages, 5 figures, 4 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
As AI becomes part of everyday learning, many courses teach students to use it mainly as a productivity tool: how to prompt, search, summarize, write, code, and use tools more efficiently. We argue that AI education also needs a setting in which students learn to test AI and understand their own role in judging machine-produced knowledge. To this end, we introduce a course-based practice that teaches AI through benchmark construction, using deep research systems as a concrete example of AI-era knowledge work. Students turn disciplinary knowledge into verifiable expert-level questions, review one another's designs for ambiguity and shortcuts, and evaluate AI systems on the resulting tasks. This activity gives students direct exposure to a powerful tool while asking them to specify what a trustworthy answer would require. The produced benchmark, QuestBench, consists of 256 questions across 14 humanities and social-science domains. Evaluation on QuestBench shows that student-designed tasks reveal hidden failures in current deep research systems: across thirteen evaluated systems, the mean question-level pass rate is only 16.85%, and the best-performing system, GPT-5.5, reaches a 57.58% pass rate. The failures are educationally useful because they show how fluent, source-backed answers can still miss the right query, source, term, or evidence standard. Reflections from five student contributors suggest that benchmark construction can help students see professional knowledge not only as content AI may retrieve, but as the basis for judging AI outputs. We present QuestBench as a benchmark artifact and as a reusable classroom setting for a larger educational question: how students can remain responsible knowledge actors as AI enters learning and professional work. The dataset is available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/PKUAIWeb/QuestBench/tree/main.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过基准构建教授人工智能：以QuestBench为例的课程实践，培养负责任的知识工作能力
随着人工智能融入日常学习，许多课程主要教授学生如何将其作为生产力工具使用：如何提示、搜索、总结、写作、编程并更高效地使用工具。我们认为，人工智能教育也需要一种让学生学习测试人工智能、理解自身在评判机器生成知识中角色的环境。为此，我们引入了一种基于课程的实践，通过构建基准来教授人工智能，以深度研究系统作为人工智能时代知识工作的具体实例。学生将学科知识转化为可验证的专家级问题，相互审查设计中的模糊性和捷径，并在生成的任务上评估人工智能系统。这一活动使学生直接接触强大工具的同时，要求他们明确可信答案所需条件。所构建的基准QuestBench包含14个文理与社会科学领域共256个问题。在QuestBench上的评估显示，学生设计的任务揭示了当前深度研究系统的隐藏缺陷：在评估的13个系统中，平均问题通过率为16.85%，表现最佳的系统GPT-5.5通过率为57.58%。这些失败在教育上具有价值，因为它们展示了即使流畅且有来源支持的答案仍可能遗漏正确的查询、来源、术语或证据标准。五名学生贡献者的反思表明，基准构建有助于学生将专业知识不仅视为AI可能检索的内容，更视为评判AI输出的基础。我们以QuestBench作为基准成果，同时将其作为可复用的课堂环境，探讨更大的教育问题：当人工智能进入学习与专业工作时，学生如何保持负责任的知识主体地位。数据集可在https://huggingface.co/datasets/PKUAIWeb/QuestBench/tree/main获取。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to move beyond treating AI as a productivity tool, this paper proposes a course-based method where students construct benchmarks to test and evaluate AI systems, using deep research systems as a model for accountable knowledge work. Students translate disciplinary knowledge into expert-level, verifiable questions, peer-review task designs for clarity and rigor, and assess AI performance on these tasks. The resulting QuestBench benchmark, comprising 256 questions across 14 humanities and social science domains, reveals significant limitations in current AI systems: across 13 evaluated models, the average question-level pass rate is just 16.85%, with the best-performing model, GPT-5.5, achieving only 57.58%. Student reflections indicate that this approach helps learners perceive professional knowledge not merely as retrievable content but as a foundation for evaluating AI outputs, positioning benchmark construction as a pedagogical tool for fostering responsible knowledge work in AI-integrated environments.
为突破将AI仅视为生产力工具的教学局限，本文提出通过构建基准测试来教授AI，采用以深度研究系统为核心的课程实践。学生将学科知识转化为可验证的专家级问题，相互评审任务设计中的模糊性与捷径，并在这些任务上评估AI系统。生成的QuestBench基准包含14个文科与社科领域共256道题目，评估显示13个模型平均通过率仅16.85%，表现最佳的GPT-5.5也仅为57.58%。学生反馈表明，该方法帮助学习者理解专业知识不仅是AI可检索的内容，更是评判AI输出的基础，从而支持AI融入学习与职业场景中的负责任知识实践。
RoadTones: Tone Controllable Text Generation from Road Event Videos
Authors: Chirag Parikh, Siddhi Pravin Lipare, Ravi Kiran Sarvadevabhatla
Venue: CVPR
First: 2026-05-20T17:08:18+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-20T17:08:18+00:00
Comments: Accepted at CVPR Findings 2026. Project page: https://roadtones.github.io/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Existing video-language models can generate factual descriptions of road events but lack control over how these events are expressed: their tone, urgency, or style. This limits deployment in communication-critical settings where the effectiveness of a message depends on both content and presentation, not just factual accuracy. To mitigate this, we introduce a comprehensive dataset-model-evaluation suite for tone-controllable road video captioning. Our human-validated data generation pipeline expands road-video corpora with diverse tonal annotations and multi-tone captions, yielding the RoadTones-51K dataset. We propose RoadTones-VL-CoT, a controllable video-to-text model that also generates tone-conditioned Chain-of-Thought intermediate drafts for interpretability. We also introduce RoadTones-Eval, a new evaluation suite that jointly measures factual consistency and tone adherence. In addition, we conducted a user study whose results validate caption quality, tone control, and factual consistency. Together, these contributions lay the foundation for context-sensitive tone-controllable video captioning.
中文标题/摘要
标题：RoadTones：从道路事件视频生成可控语气的文本
