Evaluated on a newly constructed Antarctic dataset (SLCANT), DiffGF achieves high-fidelity restoration both quantitatively and qualitatively, and its utility is validated through downstream crevasse segmentation, demonstrating its effectiveness in recovering usable imagery from historical archives.
为解决南极地区因2003年Landsat 7 ETM+扫描线校正器失效导致约22%像素缺失、且缺乏可靠参考影像的问题，本文提出DiffGF，一种无需外部参考数据的扩散模型修复框架。该框架采用两阶段设计：先在潜在空间进行扩散生成初步修复，再在像素空间进行精细化调整以提升细节与真实度。在新构建的南极数据集SLCANT上评估，DiffGF在定量与定性指标上均表现优异，并通过下游冰裂隙分割任务验证其实际应用价值，证明其能有效利用历史Landsat 7数据支持南极研究。
Federated Learning of Nonlinear Temporal Dynamics with Graph Attention-based Cross-Client Interpretability
Authors: Ayse Tursucular, Ayush Mohanty, Nazal Mohamed, Nagi Gebraeel
First: 2026-02-13T21:41:52+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-20T16:35:42+00:00
Comments: Manuscript under review
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Networks of modern industrial systems are increasingly monitored by distributed sensors, where each system comprises multiple subsystems generating high dimensional time series data. These subsystems are often interdependent, making it important to understand how temporal patterns at one subsystem relate to others. This is challenging in decentralized settings where raw measurements cannot be shared and client observations are heterogeneous. In practical deployments each subsystem (client) operates a fixed proprietary model that cannot be modified or retrained, limiting existing approaches. Nonlinear dynamics further make cross client temporal interdependencies difficult to interpret because they are embedded in nonlinear state transition functions. We present a federated framework for learning temporal interdependencies across clients under these constraints. Each client maps high dimensional local observations to low dimensional latent states using a nonlinear state space model. A central server learns a graph structured neural state transition model over the communicated latent states using a Graph Attention Network. For interpretability we relate the Jacobian of the learned server side transition model to attention coefficients, providing the first interpretable characterization of cross client temporal interdependencies in decentralized nonlinear systems. We establish theoretical convergence guarantees to a centralized oracle and validate the framework through synthetic experiments demonstrating convergence, interpretability, scalability and privacy. Additional real world experiments show performance comparable to decentralized baselines.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于图注意力机制的跨客户端可解释非线性时序动态联邦学习
现代工业系统的网络日益由分布式传感器监控，每个系统包含多个子系统，生成高维时序数据。这些子系统常相互依赖，因此理解一个子系统中的时序模式如何关联其他子系统至关重要。在去中心化环境中，原始测量数据无法共享，客户端观测异质，且每个子系统（客户端）运行固定专有模型，无法修改或再训练，限制了现有方法。非线性动态进一步使跨客户端时序依赖难以解释，因为它们嵌入在非线性状态转移函数中。我们提出一种联邦框架，在这些约束下学习跨客户端的时序依赖关系。每个客户端使用非线性状态空间模型将高维本地观测映射为低维潜在状态。中央服务器利用图注意力网络学习通信潜在状态上的图结构神经状态转移模型。为实现可解释性，我们将所学服务器端转移模型的雅可比矩阵与注意力系数关联，首次提供了去中心化非线性系统中跨客户端时序依赖的可解释表征。我们建立了收敛至集中式理想模型的理论保证，并通过合成实验验证框架，展示其收敛性、可解释性、可扩展性和隐私保护。附加真实世界实验表明其性能与去中心化基线相当。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to understand temporal interdependencies across subsystems in decentralized industrial networks where raw data cannot be shared, this work proposes a federated learning framework that uses graph attention networks to model cross-client temporal dynamics. Each client compresses high-dimensional time series into low-dimensional latent states via a nonlinear state space model, while a central server learns a graph-structured neural transition model over these states. Interpretability is achieved by linking the server’s transition model Jacobian to attention coefficients, enabling the first principled analysis of nonlinear temporal dependencies across clients. Experiments on synthetic data confirm convergence to a centralized oracle, demonstrate scalability, privacy preservation, and interpretability, with real-world tests showing performance matching decentralized baselines.
为了解决分布式工业监控系统中各子系统间时序依赖关系难以理解的问题——尤其在原始数据不可共享、客户端模型不可修改的场景下，该研究提出一种联邦学习框架：每个客户端使用非线性状态空间模型将高维时序数据映射为低维隐状态，中央服务器则基于图注意力网络学习这些隐状态间的图结构神经状态转移模型，并通过将服务器模型的雅可比矩阵与注意力系数关联，实现跨客户端时序依赖的可解释性。合成实验验证了框架的收敛性、可解释性、可扩展性和隐私保护能力，真实世界实验表明其性能与现有分布式基线相当。
Findings of the Fifth Shared Task on Multilingual Coreference Resolution: Expanding Datasets for Long-Range Entities
Authors: Michal Novák, Miloslav Konopík, Anna Nedoluzhko, Martin Popel, Ondřej Pražák, Jakub Sido, Milan Straka, Zdeněk Žabokrtský, Daniel Zeman
First: 2026-05-20T16:35:09+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-20T16:35:09+00:00
Comments: Accepted to CODI-CRAC 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This paper describes the fifth edition of the Shared Task on Multilingual Coreference Resolution, held in conjunction with the CODI-CRAC 2026 workshop. Building on previous iterations, the task required participants to develop systems capable of mention identification and identity-based coreference clustering. The 2026 edition specifically emphasizes long-range entities, defined as coreferential chains spanning significant distances, across many words and sentences. The task expanded its linguistic scope by incorporating five new datasets and two additional languages. These additions leverage version 1.4 of CorefUD, a harmonized multilingual collection comprising 27 datasets in 19 languages. In total, ten systems participated, including four LLM-based approaches (three fine-tuned models and one few-shot approach). While traditional systems still maintained their lead, LLMs demonstrated significant potential, suggesting they may soon challenge established approaches in future editions.
中文标题/摘要
标题：第五届多语言指代消解共享任务成果：扩展长距离实体数据集
本文介绍了与CODI-CRAC 2026研讨会联合举办的第五届多语言指代消解共享任务。在以往版本基础上，本任务要求参与者开发能够进行提及识别和基于身份的指代聚类的系统。2026年版特别强调长距离实体，即跨越大量词句、距离显著的指代链。任务通过引入五个新数据集和两种新增语言扩展了语言范围，这些新增内容基于CorefUD 1.4版本，该版本是一个包含19种语言、27个数据集的统一多语言语料库。共十支系统参与，包括四类基于大语言模型的方法（三类微调模型和一类少样本方法）。尽管传统系统仍保持领先，但大语言模型展现出巨大潜力，预示其可能在未来的版本中挑战现有方法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to advance multilingual coreference resolution for long-range entities, this paper presents the fifth Shared Task on Multilingual Coreference Resolution, which expanded datasets and languages to better capture coreferential chains spanning multiple sentences. Participants developed systems for mention identification and identity-based clustering, with ten submissions including four LLM-based methods. Results showed traditional systems still outperformed LLMs, but the latter demonstrated strong potential, indicating they may soon rival or surpass conventional approaches in future iterations.
为推动多语言核心指代解析在长距离实体上的发展，本文介绍了第五届多语言核心指代解析共享任务，要求系统识别提及并按身份进行跨远距离句段的聚类。2026年版新增五个语料库和两种语言，依托CorefUD v1.4的27种语言统一语料库。十支系统参赛，含四类基于大语言模型的方法，尽管传统方法仍占优势，但大模型展现出强劲潜力，预示未来可能挑战或超越现有方法。
"I didn't Make the Micro Decisions": Measuring, Inducing, and Exposing Goal-Level AI Contributions in Collaboration
Authors: Eunsu Kim, Jessica R. Mindel, Kyungjin Kim, Sherry Tongshuang Wu
First: 2026-05-20T16:28:34+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-20T16:28:34+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
As large language models (LLMs) increasingly shape how users form, refine, and extend their goals, attributing contributions in human-AI collaboration becomes critical for users calibrating their own reliance and for evaluators assessing AI-assisted work. Yet existing methods focus on final artifacts, missing the process through which goals themselves are jointly shaped. We introduce a goal-level attribution framework, CoTrace, that decomposes explicit goals into verifiable requirements and traces both direct contributions and indirect influences across dialogue turns. Applying CoTrace to 638 real-world collaboration logs, we find that while models account for only 11-26% of goal-shaping contribution, they contribute substantially more on introducing lower-level concrete requirements, and make various kinds of indirect contributions. Through controlled simulations, we show that interaction design choices significantly affect model goal-shaping behavior. In a user study, exposing participants to goal-level analyses shifts their perceived contributions by nearly 2 points on a 5-point scale, revealing systematic miscalibration in how users understand their own AI-assisted work.
中文标题/摘要
标题：“我没有做出微小决策”：衡量、引导和揭示协作中目标层级的人工智能贡献
随着大型语言模型（LLMs）日益影响用户如何形成、完善和扩展其目标，衡量人类与AI协作中的贡献对用户调整自身依赖程度及评估者评价AI辅助成果至关重要。然而现有方法聚焦于最终成果，忽略了目标本身如何共同塑造的过程。我们提出目标层级归因框架CoTrace，将明确目标分解为可验证的需求，并追踪对话回合中的直接贡献与间接影响。通过对638个真实协作日志应用CoTrace，我们发现模型仅占目标塑造贡献的11-26%，但在引入低层级具体需求方面贡献显著更多，并产生多种间接贡献。通过受控模拟，我们表明交互设计选择显著影响模型目标塑造行为。在用户研究中，向参与者展示目标层级分析使其对贡献的感知在5分量表上提升了近2分，揭示了用户对自身AI辅助工作的系统性误判。
Summary / 总结
As large language models increasingly influence how users formulate and refine goals in collaboration, accurately measuring AI contributions at the goal level becomes essential for users and evaluators. The paper introduces CoTrace, a framework that breaks down explicit goals into verifiable requirements and tracks both direct and indirect AI contributions across dialogue turns. Applied to 638 real-world collaboration logs, CoTrace revealed that LLMs account for only 11–26% of overall goal-shaping, but contribute more significantly to introducing concrete, lower-level requirements and exert various indirect influences. Controlled simulations showed that interaction design affects AI’s goal-shaping behavior, and a user study demonstrated that exposing participants to goal-level attribution shifted their perceived AI contribution by nearly two points on a five-point scale, indicating widespread miscalibration in users’ understanding of AI’s role.
随着大语言模型日益影响用户在协作中形成和调整目标，准确衡量AI在目标层面的贡献对用户和评估者至关重要。本文提出CoTrace框架，将目标分解为可验证的需求，并追踪对话轮次中AI的直接与间接贡献。在638个真实协作日志中，CoTrace发现LLM仅占目标塑造贡献的11–26%，但在具体低层级需求的引入上贡献显著，并产生多种间接影响。控制实验表明交互设计会影响AI的目标塑造行为，用户研究显示，向参与者展示目标级分析可使其对AI贡献的感知提升近两点（五分制），揭示用户普遍高估或低估AI作用的系统性偏差。
LASH: Adaptive Semantic Hybridization for Black-Box Jailbreaking of Large Language Models
Authors: Abdullah Al Nomaan Nafi, Fnu Suya, Swarup Bhunia, Prabuddha Chakraborty
First: 2026-05-20T16:27:00+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-20T16:27:00+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Jailbreak attacks expose a persistent gap between the intended safety behavior of aligned large language models and their behavior under adversarial prompting. Existing automated methods are increasingly effective but each commits to a single attack family (e.g., one refinement loop, one tree search, one mutation space, or one strategy library) and no single family dominates: the best-performing method shifts across target models and harm categories, suggesting complementary strengths that per-prompt composition could exploit. We introduce LASH (LLM Adaptive Semantic Hybridization), a black-box framework that treats outputs from multiple base attacks as reusable seed prompts and adaptively composes them for each target request. Given a seed pool, LASH searches over seed subsets and softmax-normalized mixture weights; a composition module synthesizes a single candidate prompt, and a derivative-free genetic optimizer updates the weights using black-box target feedback and a two-stage fitness function combining keyword-based refusal detection with LLM-judge scoring. On JailbreakBench, which contains 100 harmful prompts across 10 categories, we evaluate LASH on six common target models. LASH achieves an average attack success rate of 84.5% under keyword-based evaluation and 74.5% under two-stage evaluation, where responses are first filtered for refusals and then scored by an LLM judge for whether they substantively fulfill the original harmful request. LASH outperforms five state-of-the-art baselines on both metrics with only 30 mean target queries. LASH also remains competitive under three defense mechanisms and induces more success-like internal representations. These results suggest that adaptive composition across heterogeneous jailbreak strategies is a promising direction for black-box red-teaming.
中文标题/摘要
标题：LASH：面向大语言模型黑盒越狱的自适应语义混合方法
越狱攻击揭示了对齐的大语言模型在对抗性提示下与其预期安全行为之间的持续差距。现有自动化方法日益有效，但每种方法仅专注于单一攻击家族（如单次优化循环、单次树搜索、单次变异空间或单一策略库），且无单一家族能主导所有场景：表现最佳的方法随目标模型和危害类别变化而变化，表明不同攻击策略具有互补优势，可通过按需组合加以利用。我们提出LASH（LLM自适应语义混合），一种黑盒框架，将多个基础攻击的输出视为可复用的种子提示，并为每个目标请求自适应组合。给定种子池，LASH搜索种子子集及经softmax归一化的混合权重；组合模块合成单一候选提示，无导数遗传优化器利用黑盒目标反馈及结合关键词拒绝检测与LLM裁判评分的两阶段适应度函数更新权重。在包含100个有害提示（分属10类）的JailbreakBench上，我们在六个常见目标模型上评估LASH。关键词评估下平均攻击成功率84.5%，两阶段评估下74.5%（先过滤拒绝响应，再由LLM裁判评分是否实质满足原始有害请求）。LASH在仅平均30次目标查询下，于两项指标均优于五种最新基线方法。LASH在三种防御机制下仍保持竞争力，并诱导出更多成功类内部表征。结果表明，跨异质越狱策略的自适应组合是黑盒红队测试的有前景方向。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observation that no single jailbreak method consistently outperforms others across models and harm categories, the authors propose LASH, a black-box framework that adaptively composes multiple base attack strategies by treating their outputs as reusable seed prompts. LASH searches over subsets of seeds and their softmax-weighted mixtures, synthesizes a single prompt via a composition module, and refines weights using a derivative-free genetic optimizer guided by a two-stage fitness function combining keyword refusal detection and LLM-judge scoring. Evaluated on JailbreakBench with 100 harmful prompts across 10 categories and six target models, LASH achieves 84.5% success under keyword-based evaluation and 74.5% under two-stage evaluation, outperforming five state-of-the-art baselines with only 30 mean target queries, while remaining effective under three defenses and generating more success-like internal representations.
为克服单一攻击策略在不同模型和危害类别上表现不一的问题，LASH提出一种黑盒框架，通过自适应组合多种基础攻击提示生成针对每个目标请求的优化提示。它利用遗传优化器搜索提示子集及其softmax加权混合，依据结合关键词拒绝检测与LLM裁判评分的双阶段适应函数进行优化。在JailbreakBench上对六种大模型评估，LASH在关键词评估下成功率达84.5%，LLM裁判评估下为74.5%，以仅30次平均目标查询超越五种最新基线方法，同时在多种防御机制下仍保持竞争力，并诱导出更接近成功的内部表征。
Uncertainty-Calibrated Explainable Artificial Intelligence for Fetal Ultrasound Plane Classification: A Systematic Review
Authors: Gustav Olaf Yunus Laitinen-Fredriksson Lundström-Imanov, Özkan Günalp
First: 2026-01-02T21:32:26+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-20T16:26:04+00:00
Comments: 12 pages, 5 figures, 1 table, 75 references; systematic review (PRISMA 2020); manuscript prepared for submission to The Lancet Digital Health (Reviews section)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Fetal ultrasound is the cornerstone of antenatal care, and accurate recognition of a small set of standard anatomical planes underpins biometry, growth surveillance, and detection of structural anomalies. Deep learning classifiers now match or exceed expert accuracy on curated benchmarks, but most remain opaque and miscalibrated, leaving clinicians without the calibrated confidence or faithful explanations needed for safe decision support. We systematically reviewed 78 studies published between January 1, 2015 and April 30, 2026 that paired automated fetal plane classification with explainability or predictive uncertainty quantification, following PRISMA 2020. Pooled balanced accuracy across six standard planes was 0.93 (95% CI 0.91 to 0.95), but only 19 studies (24%) reported calibration and 14 (18%) reported selective prediction. We propose CALIB-XFUS, a 22-item reporting framework that operationalises calibration, explanation faithfulness, and fairness for regulated fetal ultrasound artificial intelligence. The framework spans six domains: clinical task and indication for use; dataset provenance and representativeness; model and training pipeline; calibration and selective prediction; explanation faithfulness and clinician validation; and post-market surveillance. We argue that uncertainty-calibrated, faithfully explained, and fairness-audited fetal ultrasound AI is now both technically feasible and regulatorily expected under the FDA Good Machine Learning Practice principles and the EU AI Act high-risk obligations.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于胎儿超声切面分类的不确定性校准可解释人工智能：系统评价
胎儿超声是产前护理的基石，准确识别有限数量的标准解剖切面是生物测量、生长监测和结构异常检测的基础。深度学习分类器在精选基准测试中已达到或超过专家水平，但多数模型仍不透明且未校准，使临床医生无法获得安全决策支持所需的校准置信度和可靠解释。我们系统回顾了2015年1月1日至2026年4月30日期间发表的78项研究，这些研究将胎儿切面自动分类与可解释性或预测不确定性量化相结合，遵循PRISMA 2020指南。六种标准切面的综合平衡准确率为0.93（95%置信区间0.91至0.95），但仅有19项研究（24%）报告了校准，14项（18%）报告了选择性预测。我们提出CALIB-XFUS框架，包含22项报告标准，旨在操作化胎儿超声人工智能的校准、解释忠实性和公平性评估。该框架涵盖六个领域：临床任务与使用指征；数据来源与代表性；模型与训练流程；校准与选择性预测；解释忠实性与临床医生验证；以及上市后监测。我们认为，具备不确定性校准、忠实解释和公平性审计的胎儿超声人工智能，目前在FDA良好机器学习实践原则和欧盟AI法案高风险义务下，已具备技术可行性并被监管机构所期待。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for reliable, interpretable, and calibrated AI in fetal ultrasound plane classification to support clinical decision-making, this systematic review analyzed 78 studies from 2015–2026 that combined automated classification with explainability or uncertainty quantification. The review found that while deep learning models achieved high pooled balanced accuracy (0.93), only a minority reported calibration (19/78, 24%) or selective prediction (14/78, 18%). To address this gap, the authors propose CALIB-XFUS, a 22-item framework covering clinical context, data provenance, model training, calibration, explanation fidelity, and post-market surveillance. The framework is designed to meet regulatory expectations under FDA ML guidelines and EU AI Act high-risk standards, emphasizing that uncertainty-calibrated, explainable, and fairness-audited fetal ultrasound AI is now both technically viable and legally required.
为提升胎儿超声AI在临床决策中的可信度，该系统性综述分析了2015–2026年间78项结合自动平面分类与可解释性或不确定性量化研究，发现尽管深度学习模型在六种标准平面的综合准确率达0.93，但仅有24%的研究报告了校准，18%报告了选择性预测，凸显关键缺口。为此，作者提出CALIB-XFUS框架，涵盖临床任务、数据来源、模型训练、校准、解释忠实性与公平性六大领域，旨在满足FDA和欧盟AI法案对高风险AI系统的监管要求。
Gen-AI-tecture: using generative AI to support architectural students in design tasks
Authors: Timo Kapsalis
First: 2026-05-20T16:24:59+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-20T16:24:59+00:00
Comments: Pre-print. Submitted to the Journal of Architectural Education
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The "Gen-AI-tecture" project embeds a locally executed, discipline-specific tool into a mixed-methods focus-group design, structured around three research objectives: (a) to evaluate how generative AI tools impact students' creativity in design-thinking processes and outcomes, (b) to assess whether these tools enhance inclusivity in learning processes, and (c) to examine how they develop students' AI-handling skills with a view to boosting future employability. Findings indicate enhanced creative fluency, broadened participation across diverse learner profiles, and strengthened confidence in AI-supported design processes. The study contributes evidence-based guidance for integrating generative-AI workflows into architectural pedagogy, demonstrating how such tools can operationalise constructivist principles of learner-led meaning-making, support connectivist understandings of learning as participation in human-AI networks, and advance universal learning theories by promoting more inclusive, flexible and accessible educational practices for contemporary learners.
中文标题/摘要
标题：生成式人工智能建筑学：利用生成式AI支持建筑学生的设计任务
“Gen-AI-tecture”项目将一个本地执行、学科特定的工具嵌入混合方法焦点小组设计中，围绕三个研究目标展开：(a) 评估生成式AI工具对学生设计思维过程及成果创造力的影响；(b) 评估这些工具是否提升学习过程中的包容性；(c) 探讨这些工具如何培养学生处理AI的能力，以增强其未来就业竞争力。研究发现，学生的创造性流畅性得到增强，不同学习者背景的参与度更广，对AI支持设计过程的信心也更强。该研究为将生成式AI工作流融入建筑教育提供了实证指导，展示了此类工具如何落实建构主义学习者主导意义建构原则，支持连接主义关于学习即参与人机网络的理解，并通过促进更具包容性、灵活性和可及性的教育实践，推动通用学习理论的发展。

为探究生成式AI如何重塑建筑教育，Gen-AI-tecture项目采用混合方法焦点小组，评估AI工具对学生创造力、学习包容性及AI技能掌握的影响。研究发现，学生创意流畅性提升、多元背景学习者参与度扩大、对AI辅助设计的信心增强，表明生成式AI可支持建构主义、连接主义及普适学习理论在建筑教育中的实践。
LLM Jaggedness Unlocks Scientific Creativity
Authors: Shray Mathur, J. Anibal Boscoboinik, Esther H. R. Tsai, Kevin G. Yager
First: 2026-05-11T13:47:48+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-20T16:24:26+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
As artificial intelligence advances, models are not improving uniformly. Instead, progress unfolds in a jagged fashion, with capabilities growing unevenly across tasks, domains, and model scales. In this work, we examine this dynamic jaggedness through the lens of scientific idea generation. We introduce SciAidanBench, a benchmark of open-ended scientific questions designed to measure the scientific creativity of large language models (LLMs). Given a scientific question, models are asked to generate as many unique and coherent ideas as possible, with the total number of valid responses serving as a proxy for creative potential. Evaluating 19 base models across 8 providers (30 total variants including reasoning versions), we find that jaggedness manifests both across models and within models. First, in a cross-task comparison between general and scientific creativity, improvements in general creativity do not translate uniformly to scientific creativity, revealing divergent capability profiles across models. Second, at the prompt level, stronger models do not improve uniformly; instead, they exhibit high variability, with bursts of creativity on some questions and limited performance on others. Third, at the domain level, individual models display uneven strengths across scientific subfields, reflecting fragmented internal capability profiles. Finally, we show that this jaggedness can be harnessed. We explore mechanisms of inference-time compute, knowledge pooling, and brainstorming to combine models effectively and construct meta-model ensembles that outperform any single model. Our results position jaggedness not as a limitation, but as a resource, a structural feature of AI progress that, when understood and leveraged, can amplify LLM-driven scientific creativity.
中文标题/摘要
标题：大语言模型的非均匀进步释放科学创造力
随着人工智能的发展，模型并非均匀进步，而是呈现出非均匀、断续式的发展，其能力在不同任务、领域和模型规模上增长不一。本研究通过科学创意生成的视角考察这一动态非均匀性。我们引入SciAidanBench，一个用于衡量大语言模型（LLM）科学创造力的开放性科学问题基准。给定科学问题，模型需生成尽可能多的独特且连贯的创意，有效响应总数作为创意潜力的代理指标。评估8家厂商的19个基础模型（含30个变体，包括推理版本），我们发现非均匀性既存在于模型间，也存在于模型内：首先，在通用与科学创意的跨任务比较中，通用创意的提升并不均匀转化为科学创意的提升，揭示模型能力分布的差异；其次，在提示层面，更强模型的提升亦不均匀，其表现存在高变异性，部分问题上创意爆发，部分问题上表现受限；第三，在领域层面，单个模型在科学子领域间能力分布不均，反映其内部能力结构的碎片化；最后，我们证明这种非均匀性可被利用。通过探索推理时计算机制、知识聚合与头脑风暴等方法，我们有效组合模型，构建超越单一模型的元模型集成。我们的结果表明，非均匀性并非局限，而是一种资源，是AI进步的结构性特征，若能理解并加以利用，可放大LLM驱动的科学创造力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observation that large language models advance in uneven, jagged patterns rather than uniformly, this study investigates how such jaggedness affects scientific creativity. Using SciAidanBench—a benchmark of open-ended scientific questions—the researchers evaluate 30 LLM variants across 8 providers, measuring creative output by counting valid, unique, and coherent ideas generated per prompt. Results show jaggedness manifests across models (general vs. scientific creativity diverges), within models (stronger models vary wildly in performance per question), and across scientific domains (models excel unevenly in subfields). Crucially, the study demonstrates that jaggedness can be leveraged: by combining models via inference-time compute, knowledge pooling, and brainstorming, meta-ensembles outperform individual models, suggesting jaggedness is a structural feature that can be harnessed to amplify scientific creativity.
鉴于大语言模型在不同任务和领域中的进步呈现不均匀的‘锯齿状’特征，本研究探讨这种不均衡性如何影响科学创造力。通过SciAidanBench这一开放性科学问题基准，研究人员评估了8家厂商的30个模型变体，以生成有效且独特的科学想法数量作为创造力指标。结果表明，锯齿状特征在模型间（通用与科学创造力表现不同）、模型内（强模型表现波动大）及科学子领域间（模型在不同领域能力不均）均有体现。更重要的是，研究证明这种不均衡性可被利用：通过推理时计算、知识聚合与头脑风暴等机制组合模型，构建的元模型集成表现优于任何单个模型，表明锯齿状特征是可被驾驭的结构性资源，能增强大模型驱动的科学创造力。
Flow Map Language Models: One-step Language Modeling via Continuous Denoising
Authors: Chanhyuk Lee, Jaehoon Yoo, Manan Agarwal, Sheel Shah, Jerry Huang, Aditi Raghunathan, Seunghoon Hong, Nicholas M. Boffi, Jinwoo Kim
First: 2026-02-18T19:23:07+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-20T16:23:05+00:00
Comments: 58 pages, 40 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Language models based on discrete diffusion have attracted widespread interest for their potential to provide faster generation than autoregressive models. Despite their promise, these models typically produce samples whose quality sharply degrades in the few-step regime, preventing a dramatic speedup in practice. Here, we show that language models based on continuous flows over one-hot token embeddings can outperform discrete diffusion in both quality and speed. Importantly, our continuous formulation defines a unique flow map that can be learned directly for efficient few-step inference, a structure we show is unavailable to discrete methods. In this setting, we show that both the flow and its associated flow map can be learned with simple cross-entropy objectives that respect the simplex geometry of the data, and we identify three distinct choices for flow map distillation whose performance we compare in practice. Using these insights, we build a flow language model (FLM), a continuous flow that matches state-of-the-art discrete diffusion baselines on the One Billion Words (LM1B) and OpenWebText (OWT) datasets. We then distill FLM into a flow map language model (FMLM), whose one-step generation exceeds the 8-step quality of recent few-step discrete diffusion language models. Our work challenges the widely-held hypothesis that discrete noising processes are necessary for generative modeling over discrete modalities and paves the way toward accelerated language modeling at scale. Code is available at https://github.com/david3684/flm.
中文标题/摘要
标题：流映射语言模型：通过连续去噪实现一步语言建模
基于离散扩散的语言模型因其潜在的生成速度优势而受到广泛关注，但其在少量步骤下的样本质量急剧下降，限制了实际应用中的加速效果。本文表明，基于单热编码的连续流语言模型在质量和速度上均优于离散扩散模型。重要的是，我们的连续公式定义了一个可直接学习的唯一流映射，从而支持高效的少量步骤推理，而离散方法无法实现此结构。在此框架下，我们证明流及其关联的流映射可通过简单交叉熵目标学习，且符合数据的单纯形几何结构，并识别三种不同的流映射蒸馏方法进行实际性能比较。基于这些见解，我们构建了流语言模型（FLM），其在One Billion Words（LM1B）和OpenWebText（OWT）数据集上达到当前离散扩散基线的最先进水平。随后，我们将FLM蒸馏为流映射语言模型（FMLM），其一步生成质量超越了近期少量步骤离散扩散语言模型的8步质量。本工作挑战了普遍认为离散噪声过程对离散模态生成建模是必要的假设，并为大规模加速语言建模铺平了道路。代码见https://github.com/david3684/flm。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for faster language generation without sacrificing quality, this work proposes continuous flow-based language models that operate via one-step denoising rather than autoregressive or discrete diffusion steps. The method defines a unique flow map over one-hot token embeddings, learnable via simple cross-entropy objectives that respect simplex geometry, and compares three distillation strategies for mapping the flow to a compact one-step generator. Experiments show that the resulting Flow Map Language Model (FMLM) achieves generation quality surpassing 8-step discrete diffusion models on LM1B and OWT, while enabling true one-step inference, challenging the assumption that discrete noising is essential for discrete generative modeling.
为实现更快的语言生成而不牺牲质量，本文提出基于连续流的语言模型，通过一步去噪替代自回归或离散扩散方法。作者在one-hot词嵌入空间构建连续流，学习一个独特的流映射，支持高效少步推理，而离散扩散方法无法实现。他们使用与词空间单纯形几何兼容的简单交叉熵目标训练流及其映射，并比较三种流映射蒸馏策略。实验表明，其Flow Language Model（FLM）在LM1B和OWT数据集上达到当前离散扩散模型的性能水平，其蒸馏版本Flow Map Language Model（FMLM）的一步生成质量超越了近期8步离散扩散模型。
Classification of Single and Mixed Partial Discharges under Switching Voltage Using an AWA-CNN Framework
Authors: Md Rafid Kaysar Shagor, Zannatul Ferdousy Mouri, Farhina Haque, Anindya Bijoy Das
First: 2026-05-20T16:16:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-20T16:16:51+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The growing use of fast-switching power electronics has made partial discharge (PD) analysis under switching-voltage excitation increasingly important, yet more challenging than under sinusoidal conditions due to activity concentrated at voltage transitions. This work presents an Amplitude-Width-Area (AWA) pattern representation for source-oriented PD analysis under switching-voltage excitation. In the proposed method, time domain PD pulses are characterized using pulse amplitude, width, and area, and mapped into a visual pattern where amplitude and area define the coordinate axes and width is encoded by color. The generated AWA patterns are used to distinguish six single and mixed PD source conditions: corona, internal, surface, corona+internal, corona+surface, and internal+surface. To evaluate the classification capability of the proposed representation, a Random Forest baseline and two Convolutional Neural Network (CNN) models, InceptionV3 and ResNet-18, are compared. The AWA patterns show distinguishable source-dependent distributions, and CNN-based classification achieves testing accuracy above 96%, compared with 73.33% for Random Forest. The results indicate that AWA patterns provide a visual representation of PD pulses suitable for multi-class PD source classification under switching-voltage excitation.

标题：基于AWA-CNN框架的开关电压下单相与混合局部放电分类
随着快速开关功率电子器件的广泛应用，开关电压激励下的局部放电（PD）分析日益重要，但相比正弦激励条件更具挑战性，因放电活动集中在电压转换时刻。本文提出了一种面向源的局部放电分析方法，采用振幅-宽度-面积（AWA）模式表示。该方法通过脉冲振幅、宽度和面积对时域PD脉冲进行表征，并映射为可视化模式，其中振幅和面积定义坐标轴，宽度通过颜色编码。生成的AWA模式用于区分六种单相与混合PD源条件：电晕、内部、表面、电晕+内部、电晕+表面、内部+表面。为评估该表示方法的分类能力，与随机森林基线模型及两种卷积神经网络（CNN）模型（InceptionV3和ResNet-18）进行比较。AWA模式展现出源依赖的可区分分布，CNN分类测试准确率超过96%，而随机森林仅为73.33%。结果表明，AWA模式为开关电压激励下多类PD源分类提供了一种可视化脉冲表示方法。

You Are What You Say: Exploiting Linguistic Content for VoicePrivacy Attacks
Authors: Ünal Ege Gaznepoglu, Anna Leschanowsky, Ahmad Aloradi, Prachi Singh, Daniel Tenbrinck, Emanuël A. P. Habets, Nils Peters
First: 2025-06-11T08:46:18+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-20T16:16:47+00:00
Comments: 5 pages, 6 figures, 1 table, accepted at INTERSPEECH 2025 update reason: change to the acknowledgements
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Speaker anonymization systems hide the identity of speakers while preserving other information such as linguistic content and emotions. To evaluate their privacy benefits, attacks in the form of automatic speaker verification (ASV) systems are employed. In this study, we assess the impact of intra-speaker linguistic content similarity in the attacker training and evaluation datasets, by adapting BERT, a language model, as an ASV system. On the VoicePrivacy Attacker Challenge datasets, our method achieves a mean equal error rate (EER) of 35%, with certain speakers attaining EERs as low as 2%, based solely on the textual content of their utterances. Our explainability study reveals that the system decisions are linked to semantically similar keywords within utterances, stemming from how LibriSpeech is curated. Our study suggests reworking the VoicePrivacy datasets to ensure a fair and unbiased evaluation and challenge the reliance on global EER for privacy evaluations.
中文标题/摘要
标题：你所说即你所是：利用语言内容进行语音隐私攻击
说话人匿名化系统在隐藏说话人身份的同时保留其他信息，如语言内容和情感。为评估其隐私保护效果，采用自动说话人验证（ASV）系统进行攻击。本研究通过将BERT语言模型适配为ASV系统，评估攻击者训练与评估数据集中说话人语言内容相似性的影响。在VoicePrivacy攻击挑战数据集上，我们的方法实现平均等错误率（EER）为35%，部分说话人仅凭其语句文本内容即可达到2%的EER。可解释性研究表明，系统决策与语句中语义相似的关键词相关，源于LibriSpeech数据集的编纂方式。本研究建议重构VoicePrivacy数据集，以确保公平无偏的评估，并质疑以全局EER作为隐私评估标准的可靠性。

The Human-AI Delegation Dilemma: Individual Strategies, Collective Equilibria and Sociotechnical Lock-in
Authors: Angjelin Hila
First: 2026-05-20T16:15:52+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-20T16:15:52+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This paper takes an ecological approach toward large-scale models of hybrid human-AI intelligence. Emerging models of human-AI interaction predominantly advance the complementarity thesis variously dubbed human-AI collaboration and human-AI hybrid intelligence. However, this constitutes an over-simplification of the modalities of human-AI interaction and possibility-space for both individual and collective action that human-AI interaction potentiates. To fill these gaps, this paper develops a decision and game-theoretic approach to the human-AI delegation-verification dilemma. First, we map out canonical decision-theoretic strategies that account for adaptive user trajectories, modeling how agents transition between strategies based on interaction feedback to reach stable equilibria. Second, we scale individually stable strategies to collective equilibria using three extrapolation principles: (a) non-communicative aggregation (b) local social signaling and (c) institutional norms setting. The analysis identifies the emergence of sociotechnical lock-in, a macro-behavioral state where individually adaptive delegation, in the absence of communicative and institutional safeguards, aggregates into a systemic collective action problem modeled as a prisoner's dilemma that degrades shared epistemic standards. We argue that adoption under higher communicative standards and institutional norms can mitigate suboptimal collective equilibria by imposing social commitments on individual users.
中文标题/摘要
标题：人机委托困境：个体策略、集体均衡与社会技术锁定
本文采用生态学视角研究混合人机智能的大规模模型。新兴的人机交互模型主要推进互补性理论，即所谓的人机协作与人机混合智能。然而，这简化了人机交互的模式及个体与集体行动的可能性空间。为填补这些空白，本文发展了一种决策与博弈论方法，用于分析人机委托验证困境。首先，我们梳理了经典决策理论策略，考虑适应性用户轨迹，建模代理如何根据交互反馈在策略间转换，以达到稳定均衡。其次，我们通过三种外推原则将个体稳定策略扩展为集体均衡：(a) 非沟通性聚合 (b) 局部社会信号 (c) 制度规范设定。分析发现，当缺乏沟通与制度保障时，个体适应性委托会聚合为系统性集体行动问题，表现为囚徒困境，导致共享认知标准退化。我们认为，在更高沟通标准与制度规范下，可通过施加社会承诺缓解次优集体均衡。
Summary / 总结
This paper addresses the limitations of prevailing human-AI collaboration models by introducing a decision and game-theoretic framework to analyze the human-AI delegation-verification dilemma. It first identifies adaptive individual strategies that evolve based on interaction feedback, leading to stable equilibria. Then, it scales these individual strategies to collective behavior using three extrapolation principles: non-communicative aggregation, local social signaling, and institutional norms. The analysis reveals that without communicative or institutional safeguards, individually rational delegation choices can aggregate into a prisoner’s dilemma, resulting in sociotechnical lock-in—a systemic degradation of shared epistemic standards. The study concludes that higher communicative standards and institutional norms can prevent this suboptimal collective outcome by imposing social commitments on users.
本文针对当前人类-人工智能协作模型的简化假设，提出一种决策与博弈论框架，用于分析人类-人工智能系统中的委托验证困境。研究识别出个体可根据反馈调整策略并趋向稳定均衡的决策路径，并通过三种扩展原则——非沟通性聚合、局部社会信号和制度规范——将个体策略扩展至集体行为。分析发现，缺乏沟通与制度保障时，个体适应性委托行为会聚合为一种“社会技术锁定”状态，类似于囚徒困境，导致共享认知标准退化。研究认为，提高沟通标准与制度规范可约束个体行为，从而避免集体非最优均衡。
Free-Grained Hierarchical Visual Recognition
Authors: Seulki Park, Zilin Wang, Stella X. Yu
Venue: CVPR 2026
First: 2025-10-16T14:35:18+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-20T16:14:57+00:00
Comments: Accepted to CVPR 2026. 31 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Hierarchical image recognition seeks to predict class labels along a semantic taxonomy, from broad categories to specific ones, typically under the tidy assumption that every training image is fully annotated along its taxonomy path. Reality is messier: A distant bird may be labeled only bird, while a clear close-up may justify bald eagle. We introduce free-grain training, where labels may appear at any level of the taxonomy and models must learn consistent hierarchical predictions from incomplete, mixed-granularity supervision. We build benchmark datasets with varying label granularity and show that existing hierarchical methods deteriorate sharply in this setting. To make up for missing supervision, we propose two simple solutions: One adds broad text-based supervision that captures visual attributes, and the other treats missing labels at specific taxonomy levels as a semi-supervised learning problem. We also study free-grained inference, where the model chooses how deep to predict, returning a reliable coarse label when a fine-grained one is uncertain. Together, our task, datasets, and methods move hierarchical recognition closer to the way labels arise in the real world.
中文标题/摘要
标题：自由粒度层次视觉识别
层次图像识别旨在沿语义分类学预测类别标签，从宽泛类别到具体类别，通常基于一个整洁的假设：每张训练图像在其分类路径上均有完整标注。现实更复杂：远处的鸟可能仅标注为“鸟”，而清晰的近景则可标注为“白头鹰”。我们引入自由粒度训练，允许标签出现在分类学的任意层级，模型需从不完整、混合粒度的监督中学习一致的层次预测。我们构建了具有不同标签粒度的基准数据集，表明现有层次方法在此设置下性能急剧下降。为弥补缺失监督，我们提出两种简单方案：一种添加基于文本的宽泛监督以捕捉视觉属性；另一种将特定分类层级缺失标签视为半监督学习问题。我们还研究了自由粒度推理，模型可自主决定预测深度，当细粒度标签不确定时返回可靠的粗粒度标签。综合来看，我们的任务、数据集与方法使层次识别更贴近现实世界标签生成方式。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the real-world inconsistency in hierarchical image labeling, where annotations often lack full taxonomy granularity, this work introduces free-grained training to handle mixed-level supervision. It proposes two methods: one leveraging broad text-based attributes to compensate for missing fine-grained labels, and another treating absent labels as a semi-supervised problem. Experiments on newly constructed benchmarks show that existing hierarchical models degrade significantly under partial supervision, while the proposed approaches recover performance and enable reliable inference by allowing models to choose prediction depth based on confidence.
为应对现实世界中图像标签在语义层级上不一致的问题，该研究提出自由粒度训练方法以处理混合粒度监督。其方法包括：一是利用宽泛的文本属性补充缺失的细粒度标签，二是将缺失标签视为半监督学习问题。实验表明，现有层级模型在该监督条件下性能急剧下降，而所提方法能有效恢复性能，并支持模型根据置信度选择预测深度，从而实现可靠推理。
Insights Generator: Systematic Corpus-Level Trace Diagnostics for LLM Agents
Authors: Akshay Manglik, Apaar Shanker, Kaustubh Deshpande, Jason Qin, Yash Maurya, Veronica Chatrath, Vijay S. Kalmath, Levi Lentz, Yuan, Xue
First: 2026-05-20T16:13:53+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-20T16:13:53+00:00
Comments: Under review
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Diagnosing failures in LLM agents remains largely manual. Practitioners inspect a small subset of execution traces, form ad-hoc hypotheses, and iterate. This process misses patterns that only emerge across trace populations and does not scale to production corpora where individual traces span tens of thousands of tokens. We formalize the problem of corpus-level trace diagnostics. Given a corpus of execution traces, the goal is to produce grounded natural-language insights that characterize systematic behavioral patterns across trace groups, each linked to supporting evidence. We present the Insights Generator (IG), a multi-agent system that answers diagnostic questions by proposing and testing hypotheses across the trace corpus to produce an evidence-backed insights report. We evaluate IG across qualitative and objective dimensions, spanning rubric-based report assessment and downstream performance improvements achieved by implementing IG insights. Human experts using IG reports improve scaffold performance by 30.4pp over the unmodified baseline scaffold, and coding agents leveraging IG-derived insights show consistent and stable gains. Across benchmarks, IG's scout-investigator architecture produces findings comparable in detection coverage to competing approaches, while domain experts rated IG reports as leading depth and evidence quality.
中文标题/摘要
标题：洞察生成器：面向大语言模型代理的系统性语料级执行轨迹诊断
诊断大语言模型代理的故障目前仍主要依赖人工。从业者仅检查少量执行轨迹，形成临时假设并反复迭代，此过程无法捕捉仅在轨迹群体中显现的模式，且难以扩展至生产语料（单条轨迹可达数万token）。我们形式化了语料级轨迹诊断问题：给定执行轨迹语料，目标是生成基于证据的自然语言洞察，刻画各轨迹组间的系统性行为模式。我们提出洞察生成器（IG），一种多智能体系统，通过在轨迹语料中提出并验证假设，生成证据支持的洞察报告。我们在定性与客观维度评估IG：包括基于评分标准的报告评估，以及实施IG洞察后下游性能的提升。使用IG报告的人类专家，其框架性能较未修改基线提升30.4pp；利用IG洞察的编码代理则表现出稳定持续的性能增益。在多个基准测试中，IG的侦察-调查架构在检测覆盖率上与竞品相当，而领域专家评价IG报告在深度与证据质量方面领先。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of manual LLM agent failure diagnosis, which overlooks systemic patterns in large trace corpora, the paper introduces Insights Generator (IG), a multi-agent system that automates corpus-level trace diagnostics by proposing and testing hypotheses across execution traces to generate evidence-backed natural-language insights. Evaluated through qualitative rubrics and quantitative performance metrics, IG demonstrates significant improvements: human experts using IG reports achieve a 30.4-point gain over baseline scaffolds, while coding agents show consistent performance boosts. IG’s scout-investigator architecture matches or exceeds competing methods in detection coverage and is rated superior by domain experts in insight depth and evidence quality.
为解决人工诊断LLM代理失败效率低下、难以发现跨大规模执行轨迹系统性模式的问题，本文提出Insights Generator（IG），一种多智能体系统，通过在执行轨迹间提出并验证假设，自动生成有证据支持的自然语言洞察。评估显示，IG使人工专家的框架性能提升30.4个百分点，编码代理表现稳定提升；其侦察-调查架构在检测覆盖率上不逊于竞品，且专家评价其洞察深度与证据质量居首。
Data-Efficient Neural Operator Training via Physics-Based Active Learning
Authors: Alicja Polanska, Lorenzo Zanisi, Vignesh Gopakumar, Stanislas Pamela
Venue: ICLR 2026
First: 2026-05-20T16:13:53+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-20T16:13:53+00:00
Comments: Presented at the ICLR 2026 Workshop on Artificial Intelligence and Partial Differential Equations
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Solving partial differential equations with neural operators significantly reduces computational costs but remains bottlenecked by high training data requirements. Active learning offers a natural framework to mitigate this by selectively acquiring the most informative samples in an iterative manner. We introduce physics-based acquisition - a novel physics-informed active learning algorithm that leverages the partial differential equation residual to guide data selection. We validate the method by presenting numerical experiments for the 1D Burgers equation and the 2D compressible Navier-Stokes equations. We show that, in our experiments, physics-based acquisition consistently outperforms random acquisition and matches the state of the art in data efficiency. At the same time, it has the unique advantage of injecting a physics inductive bias into the training process, ensuring that simulation cost is spent where the model's physical understanding is weakest.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于物理驱动的主动学习实现数据高效的神经算子训练
使用神经算子求解偏微分方程可显著降低计算成本，但受限于高训练数据需求。主动学习提供了一种自然框架，通过迭代性地选择最具信息量的样本以缓解此问题。我们提出了一种基于物理的采样方法——一种新型的物理信息主动学习算法，利用偏微分方程残差引导数据选择。我们通过1D Burgers方程和2D可压缩Navier-Stokes方程的数值实验验证了该方法。实验表明，基于物理的采样方法在数据效率上始终优于随机采样，并达到当前最优水平。同时，它具有独特优势：在训练过程中注入物理归纳偏置，确保计算资源被分配到模型物理理解最薄弱的区域。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high data demands of training neural operators for solving partial differential equations, this work proposes physics-based active learning to selectively sample informative data points guided by the PDE residual. The method integrates physical principles into the acquisition strategy, enhancing data efficiency without sacrificing accuracy. Experimental results on 1D Burgers and 2D compressible Navier-Stokes equations demonstrate that physics-based acquisition outperforms random sampling and matches state-of-the-art data efficiency while embedding physics inductive bias to focus training on regions where the model’s physical understanding is weakest.
为解决神经算子求解偏微分方程时对训练数据量需求高的问题，本文提出基于物理的主动学习方法，利用PDE残差引导数据采样，以提升训练效率。该方法通过迭代选择最具信息量的样本增强模型学习。在1D Burgers方程和2D可压缩Navier-Stokes方程上的实验表明，该方法在数据效率上持续优于随机采样，达到当前最优水平，同时通过注入物理先验，将计算资源集中于模型物理理解薄弱之处。
Prediction of Challenging Behaviors Associated with Profound Autism in a Classroom Setting Using Wearable Sensors
Authors: Yadhu Kartha, Conor Anderson, Jenny Foster, Theresa Hamlin, Johanna Lantz, Ryan Lay, Juergen Hahn, Gari D. Clifford, Hyeokhyen Kwon
First: 2026-05-17T19:22:48+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-20T16:12:38+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Autism Spectrum Disorder (ASD) is characterized by challenges with social interaction and communication and by restricted or repetitive patterns of thought and behavior, with significant variability in presentation. Approximately a quarter of children with ASD are classified as having profound autism, who often exhibit challenging behaviors, such as self-injurious behavior, aggression, elopement, or pica, that pose serious safety risks and disrupt learning in educational settings. Prior work has applied wearable sensors and machine learning to detect challenging behaviors, but has been largely confined to controlled laboratory environments. This work demonstrates that predicting challenging behavior episodes is feasible in a real-world special education classroom. We collected approximately 110.7 hours of labeled multimodal wearable data comprising accelerometry, electrodermal activity (EDA), and skin temperature from 9 children and young adults aged 10 to 21 years across standard classroom sessions. We fine-tuned state-of-the-art foundation models for multimodal wearable time-series analysis and show that challenging behavior episodes can be predicted up to 10 minutes in advance with an AUC-ROC of 0.78. These results establish a concrete foundation for developing proactive in-class intervention systems that enable teachers to minimize the safety risks of challenging behaviors in special education classrooms
中文标题/摘要
标题：利用可穿戴传感器预测特殊教育课堂中深度自闭症儿童的挑战性行为
自闭症谱系障碍（ASD）以社交互动和沟通困难，以及思维和行为的受限或重复模式为特征，表现形式差异显著。约四分之一的ASD儿童被归类为深度自闭症，常表现出自伤、攻击、逃逸或异食等挑战性行为，严重威胁安全并干扰学习。此前研究虽已应用可穿戴传感器和机器学习检测挑战性行为，但多局限于受控实验室环境。本研究证明，在真实世界特殊教育课堂中预测挑战性行为是可行的。我们收集了9名10至21岁儿童及青少年在标准课堂活动中约110.7小时的多模态可穿戴数据（含加速度计、皮肤电活动和皮肤温度），并微调了最先进的多模态可穿戴时序分析基础模型，结果显示挑战性行为可在10分钟前预测，AUC-ROC达0.78。这些成果为开发主动式课堂干预系统奠定了基础，使教师能有效降低特殊教育课堂中挑战性行为的安全风险。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to anticipate and mitigate safety risks in special education classrooms for children with profound autism, this study leverages wearable sensors and machine learning to predict challenging behaviors. Researchers collected multimodal physiological data—including accelerometry, electrodermal activity, and skin temperature—from nine individuals aged 10 to 21 during real classroom sessions, totaling 110.7 hours of labeled data. Using fine-tuned state-of-the-art foundation models for time-series analysis, the system achieved a 0.78 AUC-ROC score, successfully predicting challenging behavior episodes up to 10 minutes in advance, demonstrating feasibility for proactive intervention in naturalistic educational settings.
为在特殊教育课堂中提前应对严重自闭症儿童的挑战性行为并降低安全风险，本研究结合可穿戴传感器与机器学习进行预测。研究人员从10至21岁的9名受试者在真实课堂环境中收集了包含加速度、皮肤电反应和皮肤温度的多模态生理数据，总计110.7小时。通过微调先进的多模态时序分析基础模型，研究发现挑战性行为可在发作前10分钟内被预测，AUC-ROC达0.78，为开发主动干预系统提供了切实基础。
